### [1] [Making MLLM Latents Matter: Factor-Aware Predictive Learning for Cinematic Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30926)
*Songlin Yang,Zhe Wang,Xiaotong Zhao,Xi Wang,Ran Zhang,Shuai Li,Taiyi Wu,Songlian Li,Eric Liu,Alan Zhao,Anyi Rao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Factor-Aware Predictive Learning (FAPL)，通过三阶段互补学习信号使MLLM隐表示真正可被视频生成器（DiT）利用，实现稀疏引导下的时间推理与角色/场景/动作/镜头等因素解耦控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电影级视频生成需要兼具两项能力：从有限输入（稀疏而非逐帧引导）进行时间推断，以及对角色、场景、动作、镜头进行因素感知控制。MLLM虽可提供先验，但其表示并不能自动作用于生成：DiT可能通过原生条件通路绕过MLLM隐表示，且使用MLLM并不保证因素解耦控制或与生成兼容的时间预测。

Method: FAPL先将"有效利用MLLM隐表示"确立为共享前提，再以三类互补信号分别训练：(1) latent-only生成器接地：冻结MLLM与connector，训练DiT强化其对MLLM隐表示的依赖；(2) 因素感知控制：用成对视频指定目标因素改变与非目标保持，通过factor-span replacement训练DiT在输出中实现该关系；(3) 时间推断：文本-only与文本+完整视频两种观测共享同一视频目标，此阶段冻结DiT，通过其反向优化MLLM与connector，使有限输入表示面向生成，同时保留完整观测重建作为辅助训练模式。

Result: 在三项电影级视频生成任务（稀疏到视频、因素感知编辑、多镜头生成）上均稳定优于基线，并验证了FAPL对MLLM特征利用、时间控制与因素协调的贡献。

Conclusion: FAPL表明：将MLLM隐表示的有效落地作为前提，并以互补学习信号分治因素控制与时间推断，可统一支撑稀疏引导的电影级视频生成与解耦控制。

Abstract: Cinematic video generation requires both temporal inference from limited inputs (i.e., sparse rather than frame-by-frame guidance) and factor-aware control of character, scene, action, and camera. Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) offer useful priors for both capabilities, but their representations are not automatically actionable for video generation: a Video Diffusion Transformer (DiT) may bypass them through native conditioning pathways, and using them does not ensure either factor-aware control or generation-compatible temporal prediction. We propose Factor-Aware Predictive Learning (FAPL), which establishes effective MLLM latent use as a shared prerequisite and then targets the two capabilities with complementary learning signals. First, latent-only generator grounding trains the DiT with a frozen MLLM and connector, strengthening the DiT's use of MLLM latents for generation. Second, for factor-aware control, paired videos specify a target-factor change and non-target preservation, and factor-span replacement trains the DiT to realize this relationship in its output. Third, for temporal inference, text-only and text-plus-complete-video observations share a video target, and the trained DiT is then frozen while the MLLM and connector are optimized through it. This final stage trains limited-input representations for generation while retaining complete-observation reconstruction as a complementary training mode. Extensive experiments demonstrate our consistently better performance on three cinematic video generation tasks (i.e., sparse-to-video, factor-aware editing, and multi-shot generation) over baselines and validate FAPL's contributions to MLLM feature use, temporal control, and factor coordination.

</details>


### [2] [Learning from Next-Frame Prediction: Towards Unified and Scalable Visual Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30724)
*Yifeng Yao,Jinghan Li,Zhihao Zhang,Yajiao Xiong,Yang Jin,Kun Xu,Huishuai Zhang,Dongyan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VANE：在冻结语义分词器提供的固定嵌入空间中做“下一帧预测”，以时间演化作为主要学习信号，并辅以轻量生成解码器保留空间细节；图像通过局部到全局裁剪序列统一建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频本质提供“接下来发生什么”的自监督信号，但掩码视频预训练侧重由上下文恢复缺失内容而非由过去预测未来；自回归方法逐token向前预测，却在表征质量上不及联合嵌入方法。这一差距促使作者重新审视未来预测的粒度与预测空间。

Method: 提出VANE自监督视觉预训练框架：因果编码器将历史观测汇总为状态，状态预测器推断整帧的表示；预测在冻结语义分词器给出的固定嵌入空间中进行，以强调场景内容与结构；轻量级辅助生成解码器以预测表示为条件重建未来帧patch以保留细粒度空间细节；对静态图像构造局部到全局的裁剪序列，使同一编码器在同一目标下预测新显露区域，实现图像与视频统一建模。

Result: VANE随模型与数据规模扩展而提升；在统一编码器中于多数预判、识别与稠密感知基准上取得SOTA；在EPIC-KITCHENS-100动作预判上，mean-class recall@5较V-JEPA 2.1提升7.1个点。

Conclusion: 嵌入空间中的下一帧预测对视觉世界建模是有效且可扩展的，可作为统一图像与视频的自监督预训练范式。

Abstract: Video provides a natural source of supervision: what happens next serves as a learning signal for visual world modeling. Yet masked video pretraining focuses on recovering missing content from surrounding context rather than predicting the future from the past. Autoregressive methods instead predict forward one token at a time, but have yet to match joint-embedding methods in representation quality. This gap motivates us to revisit the granularity and space of future prediction. In this work, we introduce VANE, a self-supervised visual pretraining framework built on next-frame prediction in embedding space. A causal encoder summarizes past observations for a state predictor that infers the representation of the entire next frame, making temporal evolution the primary learning signal. This prediction is carried out in a fixed embedding space provided by a frozen semantic tokenizer, emphasizing scene content and structure. To retain fine-grained spatial detail alongside semantic content, a lightweight auxiliary generative decoder reconstructs future-frame patches conditioned on the predicted representations. To unify visual modeling across images and videos, we construct a local-to-global crop sequence for still images, allowing the same encoder to predict newly revealed regions from earlier crops under the same objective. Experiments show that VANE improves with model and data scaling, achieving SOTA on most anticipation, recognition, and dense perception benchmarks among unified encoders. Especially on EPIC-KITCHENS-100 action anticipation, it surpasses V-JEPA 2.1 by 7.1 points in mean-class recall@5. These results demonstrate that next-frame prediction in embedding space is effective and scalable for visual world modeling.

</details>


### [3] [One-shot federated unsupervised domain adaptation with Smoothed Knowledge Distillation and teacher refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30646)
*Ali Abedi,Q. M. Jonathan Wu,Ning Zhang,Farhad Pourpanah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出单轮联邦无监督域适应框架SKD-ETR，结合平滑知识蒸馏与基于EMA的教师精炼，高效且鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦无监督域适应（FUDA）方法需多轮通信、依赖复杂聚合策略，且受噪声伪标签与源域知识不一致问题困扰。

Method: SKD-ETR包含两部分：(1) 平滑知识蒸馏（SKD）：以多个源域模型集成的平滑软伪标签训练目标域学生模型，降低过度自信并抑制噪声；(2) 基于指数移动平均的教师精炼（ETR）：将每个源模型参数经EMA向学生插值，把目标域知识回传至教师。学生模型随机初始化，仅做单轮蒸馏，无需复杂聚合，无额外通信开销。

Result: 在OfficeHome、Office-Caltech和DomainNet上的大量实验表明，SKD-ETR性能具竞争力，同时在通信与计算上高效，并在噪声监督下保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: SKD-ETR通过单轮蒸馏与双向精炼，在保护隐私的前提下同时提升伪标签质量与学生泛化能力，为高效FUDA提供了简洁有效的方案。

Abstract: Federated unsupervised domain adaptation (FUDA) addresses the challenge of adapting models to an unlabeled target domain using decentralized source domains while preserving data privacy. However, existing FUDA methods typically require multiple communication rounds, rely on complex aggregation strategies, and often struggle with noisy pseudo labels and inconsistent source knowledge. To address these challenges, we propose SKD-ETR, a novel one-shot FUDA framework that combines Smoothed Knowledge Distillation (SKD) and Exponential moving average-based Teacher Refinement (ETR). SKD trains a student model on the target domain using smoothed soft pseudo labels generated by the ensemble of source models. This reduces overconfidence, mitigates noise, and improves robustness. ETR further refines each source model by interpolating its parameters toward the student via exponential moving average (EMA), thereby transferring target-domain knowledge back to the teachers. This bidirectional refinement enhances pseudo-label quality and student generalization without additional communication overhead. SKD-ETR eliminates complex aggregation by initializing the student model randomly and performing a single-round distillation process. Extensive experiments on OfficeHome, Office-Caltech, and DomainNet demonstrate that SKD-ETR achieves competitive performance while being communication- and computation-efficient, and robust under noisy supervision. The code for this paper is available at https://github.com/A-Abedi/SKD_ETR.

</details>


### [4] [Query-Time Calibration of CLIP Features for Open-Vocabulary 3D Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30908)
*Dominik Engel,Yousef Rajeh,Leon Sick,Ivan Viola*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种仅作用于查询阶段的标定方法，通过场景方向去除与文本原型白化，在无需重训练的情况下提升3D高斯重建中CLIP特征的开放词汇分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将CLIP等视觉-语言特征提升到3D高斯重建中可实现开放词汇分割，但单场景的提升特征常呈现强烈的方向集中性，而原始余弦相似度未考虑场景特有的特征分布。作者认为，改进"固定特征场与文本的比较方式"本身即可带来显著分割收益。

Method: （1）对视觉特征与文本原型部分减去共享的场景方向（scene centering）；（2）利用固定的通用词参考词表对文本原型做白化；（3）多类预测采用软聚类；（4）单查询预测采用带相关性边际（relevance margin）的CSLS，并以参考词表为基准。

Result: 场景中心化不仅降低了方向集中性，还在所有评估场景中提升了类间相对特征分离度；在7种提升方法与4个数据集上，该查询时标定在两类分割任务中均一致提升mIoU。收益无需重训练、无需重编码源图像、无需重新提升特征。

Conclusion: 场景条件化的特征比较方式能更充分地利用已有提升表示，是一种低成本、即插即用的开放词汇3D分割改进方案。

Abstract: Lifting vision-language features such as CLIP into 3D Gaussian reconstructions enables open-vocabulary segmentation through natural-language queries. Although CLIP represents a broad range of semantic concepts, the lifted features of individual scenes can exhibit strong directional concentration, while raw cosine similarity does not account for the scene-specific feature distribution. We show that substantial segmentation gains can be obtained by changing how a fixed lifted feature field is compared with text. Our method partially subtracts a shared scene direction from visual features and text prototypes and whitens text prototypes using a fixed reference vocabulary of generic terms. It then uses soft clustering for multiclass prediction and CSLS with a relevance margin against the reference vocabulary for single-query prediction. Beyond reducing directional concentration, scene centering improves class-relative feature separation in every evaluated scene. Across seven lifting methods and four datasets, the resulting query-time calibration consistently improves mean intersection over union in both multiclass and single-query segmentation. These gains require no retraining, source-image re-encoding, or feature re-lifting, demonstrating the value of scene-conditioned comparison for making better use of existing lifted representations.

</details>


### [5] [V-JEPA 2 Meets AQA: Phase-Based Scoring and Reference-Based Feedback for Mountain Biking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30160)
*Santhiya Rajan,Savio Cardoz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究在弱标签（仅好/差二分类）条件下开展山地车速降动作质量评估（AQA），采用冻结V-JEPA 2特征与2D姿态/运动量、四阶段启发式划分、Transformer打分器及参考层生成纠正线索；五折AUROC 0.768/AP 0.812，但存在epoch选择与骑手未分离的偏倚，外部探针表现接近随机，故不足以支撑骑手无关评分或可靠纠正反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实训练语料仅有粗粒度二分类标签（好/差技术），缺少连续专家评分、阶段边界与方面级标注，使得细粒度AQA与可解释纠正反馈难以直接监督学习；作者希望明确在如此弱监督条件下各输出（评分、阶段线索、方向建议）能达到的边界与实证需求。

Method: 95段场地跳跃视频；用冻结V-JEPA 2视频特征加二维姿态与运动度量，以四种启发式规则汇总阶段；小型Transformer输出二分类得分；独立参考层将各阶段度量与"好技术"剪辑对比，提出阶段、身体参数与方向三类线索；按剪辑级五折交叉验证评估，并进行消融（去除生物力学输入）、内部选择运行、无监督方面头与外部的17段探针检验。

Result: 全模型池化折外AUROC 0.768、AP 0.812（探索性，报告折同时用于选epoch，且折间未按骑手分离）；移除生物力学评分输入后AUROC升至0.809；单独内部选择运行AUROC 0.678（每外折约57而非76训练剪辑），故其差异不能量化选择偏倚；无监督方面头未学出可区分因子；17段外部探针中较早检查点二分类准确率0.412、与专家评分Spearman -0.135；候选线索对两位教练的精确率0.118、召回率1.0，且方向判断分歧明显。

Conclusion: 结果不支持骑手无关的评分或可靠的纠正反馈；工作定位为探索性诊断，明确刻画了弱监督/弱验证协议下各输出的局限，并指出评估这两类用途所需的实证工作（骑手分离验证、独立选择与更大外部探针等）。

Abstract: We study mountain-bike action quality assessment (AQA) with weak labels and candidate feedback. The training corpus comprises 95 field-recorded jump clips with binary labels denoting good or poor technique. These are coarse categorical labels, not continuous expert quality scores; phase-boundary and aspect-level annotations are unavailable. Our pipeline summarizes four heuristic phases using frozen V-JEPA 2 video features and two-dimensional pose and motion measurements. A small transformer predicts a binary-label score. A separate reference layer compares phase-specific measurements with those of clips labelled as good technique to propose phase, body-parameter, and direction cues. We detail the measurements, network, and evaluation so that the limits of each output are explicit. In five-fold clip-level cross-validation, the full scorer obtained pooled out-of-fold AUROC 0.768 and average precision 0.812. These are exploratory figures because the reporting fold also selected the neural training epoch and riders were not separated between folds. Removing biomechanical scoring inputs raised AUROC to 0.809. A separate inner-selection run obtained AUROC 0.678, but used about 57 rather than 76 training clips per outer fold, so its difference from the test-selected run does not quantify selection bias. The unsupervised aspect head did not learn distinct factors. On a 17-clip external probe, an earlier checkpoint obtained binary accuracy 0.412 and Spearman association -0.135 with expert scores. Candidate cues had precision 0.118 and recall 1.0 against each of two coaches, with substantial disagreement about direction. These findings do not establish rider-independent scoring or reliable corrective feedback; they identify the empirical work needed to evaluate either use.

</details>


### [6] [RecallGS: Geometry-Grounded Recall for Online Semantic Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31293)
*Thanh-Khoi Nguyen,Thien-Phuc Tran,Minh-Triet Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RecallGS 是一种无需训练、轻量化的在线语义 3DGS 机制，将历史观测作为非参数化几何-语义先验，通过"召回-接地-融合"管线为每个高斯聚合语义特征，在 ScanNet/ScanNet++ 上以 2.48 FPS 取得较强分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线开放词汇语义 3DGS 方法依赖额外学习的 3D 网络来补全图像语义特征缺失的几何上下文，带来额外训练与运行时开销。作者观察到这类几何-语义证据其实已存在于智能体的观测历史中，可被"检索"而非"学习"。

Method: 提出 RecallGS（Geometry-Grounded Recall for Online Semantic Gaussian Splatting）：将历史观测视作非参数化几何-语义先验，采用三步管线——（1）CLIP 检索相关历史观测；（2）利用 alpha 合成权重识别具有几何支撑的视图；（3）在每个高斯的渲染足迹（rendered footprint）上聚合这些视图的语义特征。

Result: 在 ScanNet 与 ScanNet++ 上实现较强的语义分割性能，运行速度达 2.48 FPS。

Conclusion: 几何-语义上下文可有效从观测历史中恢复，无需额外的学习式 3D 分支，验证了基于召回的非参数化先验在在线语义 3DGS 中的可行性与效率优势。

Abstract: Online semantic 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables open-vocabulary scene understanding from streaming observations. However, existing methods recover geometric context missing from image-derived semantic features using an additionally learned 3D network, incurring extra training and runtime overhead. We observe that such geometric-semantic evidence may already exist in the agent's observation history and can be retrieved rather than learned. Based on this insight, we introduce RecallGS (Geometry-Grounded Recall for Online Semantic Gaussian Splatting), a lightweight, training-free mechanism that treats past observations as a non-parametric geometric-semantic prior. RecallGS follows a recall-ground-fuse pipeline: CLIP retrieves relevant observations, alpha-compositing weights identify geometrically supported views, and aggregate their semantic features over each Gaussian's rendered footprint. On ScanNet and ScanNet++, RecallGS achieves strong semantic segmentation performance at 2.48 FPS, demonstrating that geometric-semantic context can be effectively recovered from observation history without an additional learned 3D branch.

</details>


### [7] [LoRA Superposition across Pretrained Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30691)
*Keigo Watanabe,Hiroshi Kera,Kazuhiko Kawamoto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoRA通常绑定单一预训练骨干。本文提出"LoRA叠加"(LoRA superposition)：将同一任务在不同骨干上独立训练的LoRA直接相加，再原样应用到所有贡献骨干。在7个ViT-B/16 CLIP骨干×8个分类任务共56组上，精确求和使全部组合均提升，平均较未适配骨干提升30.21个百分点，仅比各自单独训练的solo LoRA低0.28个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LoRA是否真的必须与特定预训练骨干耦合：能否让同一任务的多骨干LoRA合并为一份通用参数，避免为每个骨干单独存储/路由LoRA，从而检验跨模型LoRA是否彼此干扰。

Method: 在7个ViT-B/16 CLIP骨干上针对8个图像分类任务分别独立训练LoRA；将同一任务的各骨干LoRA按元素直接相加，构成superposition，并原样应用到每个贡献骨干。设置对照：solo LoRA（单独训练）与leave-one-model-out (LOMO)求和（排除目标骨干自身的LoRA）。分析参数空间正交性及跨骨干成对响应矩阵。

Result: 精确求和使56个骨干-任务组合全部改善，平均较未适配骨干提升30.21个百分点，仅低于solo LoRA平均0.28个百分点；LOMO求和平均仅提升0.06个百分点。骨干专属solo LoRA在参数空间中近乎正交，跨骨干成对响应高度集中在对角线上。

Conclusion: 结果支持"分量式互不干扰"(component-wise non-interference)假设：冻结骨干相当于隐式选择器，自动选取自身对应的LoRA分量，无需显式路由或跨模型对齐，即可实现LoRA叠加复用。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is usually tied to the pretrained backbone on which it is learned. We ask whether LoRAs trained independently for the same task on different pretrained backbones can be summed and applied unchanged to all contributing backbones. We call this construction LoRA superposition. Across seven ViT-B/16 CLIP backbones and eight image-classification tasks, the exact sum improves all 56 backbone-task pairs and achieves a mean accuracy gain of 30.21 percentage points over the unadapted backbone. The exact sum achieves a mean accuracy only 0.28 percentage points below that of individually trained solo LoRAs. In contrast, a leave-one-model-out (LOMO) sum produces a mean gain of only 0.06 percentage points over the unadapted backbones. Backbone-specific solo LoRAs are nearly orthogonal in parameter space, while pairwise cross-backbone responses are strongly concentrated on the diagonal. These results support a component-wise non-interference hypothesis, under which the frozen backbone acts as an implicit selector of its own component without explicit routing or cross-model alignment.

</details>


### [8] [Asking Less, Adapting Better: Feedback-Efficient Test-Time Adaptation for Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30643)
*Zixu Wang,Huaquan Zhang,Shoukai Xu,Jinwu Hu,Zhiquan Wen,Mingkui Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FETA：通过分层样本选择与单步离散反馈实现反馈高效、优化可靠的VLN测试时自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN智能体在未见环境中因分布偏移性能严重下降；基于无监督熵最小化的TTA会沿轨迹传播误差导致级联失败；引入人类反馈的方法在轨迹级对所有步均匀施加监督，未定位真正有信息量的动作，导致反馈利用率低、优化粒度不匹配。

Method: 提出FETA：(1) 分层样本选择——先筛选归一化策略熵波动大的轨迹，再在轨迹内用熵-梯度显著性准则选出关键动作，仅对其查询一次二元正确性反馈；(2) 差异感知熵优化目标——仅在被查询步更新策略，并按预测-反馈差异自适应加权，差异越大权重越高。

Result: 在多个基准上取得SOTA性能，且人类干预更少；在噪声反馈与持续自适应场景下保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: 将反馈从轨迹级细化到关键动作级，并配合差异感知加权，可同时提升VLN测试时自适应的反馈效率与优化可靠性。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) requires agents to follow instructions and navigate to target locations. However, current VLN agents often suffer severe performance degradation in unseen environments due to distribution shifts. Test-Time Adaptation (TTA) offers a promising paradigm to bridge this gap, yet existing methods that rely on unsupervised entropy minimization can propagate errors through the trajectory, causing cascading failures in navigation. Recent methods mitigate this by incorporating human feedback, but they apply trajectory-level supervision uniformly across all steps without localizing the actions is truly informative, leading to inefficient feedback utilization and mismatched optimization granularity. To address these limitations, we propose FETA, a Feedback-Efficient Test-Time Adaptation method that improves both feedback efficiency and optimization reliability. To reduce unnecessary human interventions, we introduce a hierarchical sample selection strategy to localize informative samples. Specifically, we first filter trajectories whose normalized policy entropy exhibits large fluctuations. Within each selected trajectory, we further select the critical action via an entropy-gradient saliency criterion and query a single binary feedback on its correctness. A discrepancy-aware entropy optimization objective then updates the policy only at the queried step, with adaptive scaling that assigns larger relative weights to samples with greater prediction-feedback discrepancy. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that FETA achieves state-of-the-art performance with fewer human interventions, while remaining robust under noisy feedback and continual adaptation settings.

</details>


### [9] [Visual Grounding First, Multimodal In-context Learning Follows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30592)
*Minhyuk Seo,Minjae Lee,Chaeeun Lee,Wei Lin,Muhammad Jehanzeb Mirza,Tinne Tuytelaars,Jonghyun Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文指出视觉定位（visual grounding）是多模态上下文学习（ICL）的关键瓶颈，提出ProCoRe，通过对多模态对比数据进行对比强化学习来增强视觉定位，从而提升多模态ICL性能，并构建了PICL基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态ICL方法依赖ICL专用数据做额外训练或任务向量提取，虽在适配所用基准上有效，但泛化性差，且会导致已解决任务的遗忘。作者将问题归因于MLLM在演示中无法关注任务相关视觉证据，过度依赖文本线索并产生幻觉输出。

Method: 提出ProCoRe：对对比图像对生成描述（caption），将其分解为命题（proposition），并优化对比奖励——鼓励描述与对应图像对齐、抑制与不匹配图像对齐，通过对比强化学习提升视觉定位，且不接触任何ICL格式训练样本。

Result: 在off-the-shelf Qwen-VL-3-8B基础上，多模态ICL分类准确率平均提升5.6%，描述任务ROUGE-L提升13.8%，跨基准优于依赖ICL数据的SOTA方法；并提出PICL个性化多模态ICL描述基准，用于检验MLLM是否真正理解视觉演示而非依赖预训练语义先验。

Conclusion: 视觉定位是多模态ICL的核心瓶颈，通过对比强化学习增强视觉定位可在不依赖ICL数据的情况下实现更强、更可泛化的多模态ICL能力；PICL为评估真实视觉理解提供了新测试手段。

Abstract: LLMs have shown strong in-context learning (ICL) capability, but extending it to Multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) remains challenging. Prior multimodal ICL methods often rely on ICL-specific datasets for additional training or task vector extraction, which can improve performance on the same ICL benchmark used for adaptation. However, they do not generalize well to other benchmarks and often induce forgetting in previously well-solved tasks. For this reason, we identify 'visual grounding' as the key bottleneck in multimodal ICL; MLLMs often fail to attend to task-relevant visual evidence in demonstrations, instead over-relying on textual cues and producing hallucinated outputs. To address this, we propose ProCoRe to enhance visual grounding through contrastive reinforcement learning on multimodal contrastive data for improving multimodal ICL. ProCoRe generates captions for contrastive image pairs, decomposes them into propositions, and optimizes contrastive rewards that encourage alignment with corresponding images while discouraging mismatched ones. Despite never seeing ICL-formatted training examples, ProCoRe improves average multimodal ICL classification accuracy by 5.6% and captioning ROUGE-L by 13.8% over the off-the-shelf Qwen-VL-3-8B model, outperforming ICL-data-dependent state of the arts across benchmarks. Finally, we introduce PICL, a personalized multimodal ICL captioning benchmark that tests whether MLLMs genuinely understand visual demonstrations rather than relying on pre-trained semantic priors.

</details>


### [10] [Direct Forcing: A Single-Stage Training Recipe for Few-Step Autoregressive Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30861)
*Haojun Xu,Jie Huang,Xin Lu,Mingchen Zhong,Zihao Fan,Linjiang Huang,Si Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Direct Forcing：单阶段少步自回归视频生成框架，用参考视频同时作为因果延续的配对样本与自生成rollout的分布目标，实现因果适配与分布匹配的统一训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有少步自回归视频生成需将因果适配与分布匹配分离训练，基于表征的方法从ODE初始化检查点出发，即便目标分布已由参考视频直接定义仍保留两阶段流程，导致流程冗余、缺少统一高效的训练范式。

Method: 提出Direct Forcing单阶段框架，包含两部分：(1) Causal Continuation Alignment，在参考历史条件下学习成对时空对应关系；(2) Rollout Distribution Matching，用最大均值差异（MMD）对齐自生成视频与参考视频的表征分布。并引入Dynamic Gradient Balancing动态调节两项损失的梯度贡献。从双向Wan2.1-1.3B出发训练。

Result: 仅250次更新即将双向Wan2.1-1.3B转为四步自回归生成器，无需独立初始化阶段或在线扩散分数监督，取得VBench Total 84.63、Quality 86.01。消融实验验证动态梯度平衡的收益，并揭示表征偏好差异：深层特征利于延续对齐，中间层特征更利于rollout分布匹配。

Conclusion: Direct Forcing证明因果适配与目标分布匹配可在单一训练阶段联合完成，兼顾低延迟流式生成与高质量，并为不同训练信号的特征层选择提供经验性指导。

Abstract: Few-step autoregressive video generation enables low-latency streaming, yet prevailing training pipelines separate causal adaptation from distribution matching. Recent representation-based text-to-video methods retain this separation by starting from ODE-initialized checkpoints, even when the target distribution is defined directly by reference videos. We introduce Direct Forcing, a single-stage framework that uses reference videos both as paired examples of how a history continues and as a target population for self-generated rollouts. Causal Continuation Alignment learns paired spatiotemporal correspondence under reference histories, while Rollout Distribution Matching uses maximum mean discrepancy to align the representation distributions of self-generated and reference videos. Together, these complementary signals allow a pretrained video model to learn causal continuation and the target video distribution in a single training stage. Dynamic Gradient Balancing adapts the relative gradient contributions of continuation alignment and distribution matching during training. Starting from bidirectional Wan2.1-1.3B, Direct Forcing learns a four-step autoregressive generator in 250 updates without a separate initialization stage or online diffusion-score supervision, achieving 84.63 VBench Total and 86.01 Quality. Ablation studies further demonstrate the benefits of dynamic gradient balancing and reveal distinct representation preferences: deeper features benefit continuation alignment, whereas intermediate features yield better performance for rollout distribution matching.

</details>


### [11] [MoG-VLM: Efficient Video Anomaly Detection through Motion-Guided Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31478)
*Khaled Waleed Dawoud,Muhammad Zaigham Zaheer,Khan Muhammad,Muhammad Haris Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoG-VLM提出以运动作为轻量控制信号的自适应视频异常检测框架，仅在信息量大的片段激活VLM推理，在四个基准上取得SOTA并带来1.31x加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM用于视频异常检测潜力大，但稠密逐帧推理难以实时部署；现有均匀采样忽略内容动态性，要么在静止片段浪费算力，要么漏检局部异常。

Method: 1) 时序梯度幅值（TGM）作为内容感知采样信号，将计算集中于信息区域、避免冗余处理；2) 分层双流设计：以锚点触发上下文窗口扩展并配合早停，仅在高异常概率帧附近激活稠密多帧VLM推理；3) 融合模块将瞬时语义线索与时序动作动态相协调。

Result: 在XD-Violence、UCF-Crime、MSAD、ShanghaiTech上AUC-ROC分别为91.91%、84.30%、87.88%、78.96%，零样本免训练设定下三个基准取得新SOTA；XD-Violence上较此前最佳提升+0.57%，并实现1.31x推理加速。

Conclusion: 内容感知地分配VLM算力——更智能地处理更少帧，而非均匀加密所有帧推理——是比均匀稠密化更有效的性能提升方向。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have shown strong potential for video anomaly detection, but dense frame-level inference makes them impractical for real-time deployment. Existing acceleration commonly relies on uniform sampling, which ignores content dynamics and can either waste computation on static segments or miss localized anomalies. We propose MoG-VLM a motion-adaptive anomaly detection framework that leverages motion purely as a lightweight control signal to decide when VLM inference is necessary. To this end, we introduce Temporal Gradient Magnitude (TGM) which performs content-aware sampling to concentrate computation on informative regions while avoiding redundant processing. We further introduce a hierarchical dual-stream design in which dense multi-frame VLM reasoning is activated only around frames with elevated anomaly likelihood, via anchor-triggered context-window expansion with early termination. A fusion module then reconciles these instantaneous semantic cues with temporal action dynamics. Across four benchmarks, our method achieves 91.91%, 84.30%, 87.88%, and 78.96% AUC-ROC on XD-Violence, UCF-Crime, MSAD, and ShanghaiTech, respectively, establishing new state of the art on three benchmarks under the zero-shot, training-free setting. On XD-Violence, we improve over the previous best result by +0.57% while delivering a 1.31x inference speedup. These results demonstrate that content-aware allocation of VLM capacity, processing fewer frames more intelligently rather than all frames uniformly, is a more effective axis of progress than uniformly densifying VLM inference across frames. Code is available on GitHub: https://github.com/kdawoud91/MoG-VLM.

</details>


### [12] [SplatLab: Autonomous Scientific Discovery for 3DGS Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30468)
*Seemandhar Jain,Manmohan Chandraker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SplatLab是一个面向3D Gaussian Splatting（3DGS）的自主研究系统，可从研究提示、目标论文URL或无输入出发，完成基线复现、扩展提出、在Mip-NeRF 360与LLFF上训练与消融、撰写论文，并将每个报告数值绑定到已记录的训练运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复现一篇3DGS论文需数周专家工程，方可提出扩展；既有自主研究系统受限于短固定模板、低可执行性与弱可追溯性，且尚未在活跃的计算机视觉子领域实现端到端验证。

Method: 五项设计：(i) 候选由经核实复现的骨干网络的插件变异生成，骨干与已发表基线对齐；(ii) 跨八个岛屿的级联加权Elo锦标赛，以实测GPU PSNR打分并结合言语梯度反馈；(iii) 常驻证伪器在失败时扣减Elo，将压力测试存活率纳入选择；(iv) 每个报告数值绑定记录的训练运行，存在未支撑声明时运行不可终止；(v) 七类3DGS分类体系以领域对齐脚手架包裹EVOLVE-BLOCK标记，可跨3DGS代码库迁移。

Result: 在PSNR目标运行中，稿件较其复现目标提升+0.18至+3.78 dB，其中天体摄影星云渲染提升+3.78 dB（此前无对应3DGS方法）。研究周期从数月研究生人力压缩至数天以自主计算为主的运行。

Conclusion: SplatLab实现了3DGS领域端到端自主研究闭环，兼顾可执行性与可追溯性；代码、全部稿件、实验台账及跨运行论文库已公开。

Abstract: Reproducing a published 3DGS paper takes weeks of expert engineering before any extension can be proposed. Prior autonomous-research systems read papers, draft hypotheses, and write code, but are limited by short fixed templates, low executability, and weak traceability, and none has been demonstrated end-to-end in a live computer-vision subfield with intense activity. SplatLab is an autonomous research system for 3D Gaussian Splatting that addresses these limitations. Given a research prompt, a target-paper URL, or no input at all, it reproduces a published baseline, proposes an extension, trains and ablates across Mip-NeRF 360 and LLFF, writes the manuscript, and binds every reported number to a logged training run. Five design choices distinguish it: (i) candidates mutate plugins from a verified-reproduction backbone matched to published baselines; (ii) a cascade-weighted Elo tournament across eight islands scores candidates on measured GPU PSNR with verbal-gradient feedback; (iii) a standing falsifier subtracts Elo on failure, integrating stress-test survival into selection; (iv) every reported number is bound to a logged training run, and the run cannot terminate while any claim is unbacked; (v) a seven-category 3DGS taxonomy wraps EVOLVE-BLOCK markers in domain-aligned scaffolds transferable across 3DGS repositories. On PSNR-targeted runs, SplatLab's manuscripts beat their reproduced targets by +0.18 to +3.78 dB, including +3.78 dB on astrophotographic nebula rendering, for which no prior 3DGS method exists. The research cycle compresses from months of graduate-student time to days of mostly autonomous compute. Code, all manuscripts, experiment ledgers, and the cross-run paper store are available on the project page.

</details>


### [13] [One Evaluation Is Enough: Inversion-Free Image Editing with Trajectory Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30827)
*Lifan Jiang,Taiyang Chen,Senlin cheng,Bolei Xu,Xiaobo Li,Can Ye,Dehai Zhao,Deng Cai,Boxi Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Endpoint-Calibrated Transport（ECT），一种闭式、免训练的单步图像编辑器：仅需一次噪声层级评估、一次去噪器批量前向与一次潜变量更新即可完成反演无关的编辑，性能稳定且耗时极短。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实图像编辑通常依赖反演加迭代采样；高效的单步编辑器往往需在多个噪声层级上估计并平均编辑场以稳定大更新。作者追问更严格的问题：单次时间场评估是否足以支撑可靠的无反演编辑。

Method: 利用VP（方差保持）单步模型中此前未被充分利用的对偶性：在共享噪声状态下的单个源—目标查询同时确定局部角度语义切向量与干净端点位移。方法将切向量沿解析已知的剩余VP角度积分，得到名义单步传输；再用端点位移通过约束投影校正其长程外推。整体为"先传输、后校正"的闭式构造，无需反演、轨迹积分、第二次时间观测或迭代优化，可直接用于预训练epsilon预测的VP单步生成器。

Result: 在所报告的基准设定下，ECT在极短编辑时间内取得编辑质量的稳定提升。

Conclusion: 单一时间场评估足以实现可靠的无反演编辑；基于VP角度对偶性的传输—校正框架为单步编辑提供了高效、免训练且闭式的新范式。

Abstract: Real-image editing commonly relies on inversion and iterative sampling, while efficient one-step editors often stabilize a large update by estimating and averaging editing fields at multiple noise levels. We ask a stricter question: is a single temporal field evaluation sufficient for reliable inversion-free editing? Our answer rests on a previously underused duality in a variance-preserving (VP) one-step model: one source-target query at a shared noisy state simultaneously determines a local angular semantic tangent and a clean endpoint displacement. We integrate the tangent over the analytically known remaining VP angle to obtain a nominal one-step transport, and use the endpoint displacement to correct its long-horizon extrapolation through a constrained projection. This transport-first, correction-second construction yields Endpoint-Calibrated Transport (ECT), a closed-form, training-free editor requiring only one noise level, two text conditions packed into one denoiser batch, and one latent update. ECT requires no image inversion, trajectory integration, second temporal observation, or iterative optimization, and can be directly applied to pretrained epsilon-prediction VP one-step generators. Across the reported benchmark settings, ECT delivers stable gains in editing quality within an extremely short editing time.

</details>


### [14] [Watch and Learn: Teaching Visual In-Context Learning by Removing Textual Shortcuts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30913)
*Nimrod Shabtay,Asaf Yehudai,Avihu Dekel,Eli Schwartz,Raja Giryes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VLMs 的视觉上下文学习（vICL）能力不足源于训练数据缺陷；论文构建无文本捷径的 MIME 训练集并提��� DTAD 注意力丢弃方法，显著提升 vICL 且不损失单图能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLMs 相比纯文本模型在上下文学习上明显弱势。初步研究发现 VLM 依赖文本捷径（shortcut），未能充分利用视觉示范，作者据此假设其缺乏针对视觉上下文学习（vICL）的训练。

Method: 1) 构建 MIME：任务无关的 vICL 训练集，指令中不陈述示范图像与答案之间的规则，迫使模型仅从视觉示范中推断规则，消除文本捷径；2) 提出 DTAD（Demonstration Text Attention Dropout）：训练时随机屏蔽示范标签，使模型必须从示范图像推断规则。

Result: 在三个 VLM 上微调 MIME 使 vICL 准确率提升 8.1–18.0 点，单图性能保持，多图与纯文本 ICL 也提升；若训练数据中陈述规则则增益大幅下降。DTAD 在 vICL 准确率上与 MIME 微调相当，但对示范图像的注意力更一致。TrueMICL 上，MIME 训练的 Qwen3-VL-8B 可匹敌 Claude Opus 5 等前沿模型，DTAD 更进一步超越。将 MIME 加入 LLaVA 1.5 13B 的指令微调混合数据使 vICL 准确率翻倍以上，而同等规模的多图数据集仅带来微小提升。

Conclusion: VLM 的 vICL 短板主要来自训练数据缺乏显式视觉规则推断的训练信号；vICL 数据应成为 VLM 训练的标准组成部分。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) struggle with in context learning compared to their textual counterparts. Our preliminary study shows that VLMs rely on textual shortcuts and underutilize the visual demonstrations. We therefore hypothesize that VLMs are insufficiently trained to perform visual in-context learning (vICL). To this end, we construct MIME, a task-agnostic vICL training set without textual shortcuts. We ensure the instruction does not state the rule linking the demonstration images to their answers, so the model must infer it from the visual demonstrations. Fine-tuning on MIME improves vICL accuracy by 8.1 to 18.0 points across three VLMs, preserves single-image performance, and improves multi-image and text only in-context learning. In contrast, stating the rule in the training data sharply reduces this gain. We further propose DTAD (Demonstration Text Attention Dropout), which randomly withholds demonstration labels during training so that the VLM must infer the rule from the demonstration images. While achieving comparable vICL accuracy to fine-tuning on MIME, DTAD yields more consistent attention to the demonstration images. On TrueMICL, Qwen3-VL-8B trained on MIME matches frontier models such as Claude Opus 5, and surpasses them with DTAD. Finally, adding MIME to the instruction-tuning mix of LLaVA 1.5 13B more than doubles its vICL accuracy without hurting single-image abilities, whereas a same-size multi-image set improves it only slightly. These results suggest that vICL data should be a standard component of VLM training.

</details>


### [15] [Hierarchical Fine-Grained Cross-Modal Alignment via Dynamic Granularity Discovery and Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30912)
*Pengfeng Song,Shaozhong Zhang,Pengfei Shao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HFCMA：将图像块与文本词元动态聚合成自适应视觉区域与文本短语，并以熵正则最优传输求解区域-短语结构化对齐，在双编码器检索框架下提升细粒度图文检索的判别力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度图文检索不仅要评估全局语义一致性，还需为局部文本语义（对象、属性、数量、动作、关系）建模视觉证据，以区分语义相近的候选。现有方法以固定图像块、检测区域或文本词元为匹配单元，再通过最大池化、交叉注意力或相似度累加聚合局部交互，在语义单元构建与全局匹配一致性两方面均受限。

Method: 提出分层细粒度跨模态对齐框架HFCMA：1）动态粒度发现（DGD）将原始图像块与词元分别聚合为语义紧凑的视觉区域与文本短语；2）将区域-短语匹配建模为熵正则最优传输问题，在全局边缘约束下估计结构化对齐矩阵；3）引入背景去偏、文本重要性加权与原型一致性正则，抑制背景噪声与局部语义漂移；整体嵌入全局检索打分流程。

Result: 在Flickr30K与MS-COCO上的实验表明，HFCMA在双编码器检索框架下有效结合了动态语义单元构建与基于最优传输的结构化匹配；所得细粒度分数为候选排序提供结构化局部对齐信息，提升了对细微语义差异的区分能力。

Conclusion: 动态语义单元构建与OT结构化匹配的结合是提升细粒度检索判别力的有效途径，可在保持双编码器高效性的同时提供结构化局部对齐信息。

Abstract: Fine-grained image-text retrieval requires a model not only to assess global semantic consistency between an image and a text, but also to model visual evidence for local textual semantics, including objects, attributes, quantities, actions, and relations, so as to distinguish semantically similar candidates. Existing methods commonly use fixed image patches, detected regions, or text tokens as the basic matching units and then aggregate local interactions through max pooling, cross attention, or similarity accumulation. Such strategies remain limited in semantic unit construction and global matching consistency. This paper proposes a hierarchical fine-grained cross-modal alignment framework, termed HFCMA. The framework progressively transforms low-level patch and token representations into adaptive visual regions and textual phrases, and then performs structured region-phrase matching within a global retrieval scoring process. Specifically, HFCMA first adopts dynamic granularity discovery (DGD) to aggregate raw patches and tokens into semantically compact visual regions and textual phrases. It then formulates region-phrase matching as an entropy-regularized optimal transport problem, thereby estimating a structured alignment matrix under global marginal constraints. To reduce background noise and local semantic drift, HFCMA further introduces background debiasing, textual importance weighting, and prototype consistency regularization. Experiments on Flickr30K and MS-COCO show that HFCMA effectively combines dynamic semantic unit construction with OT-based structured matching under a dual-encoder retrieval framework. The resulting fine-grained score provides structured local alignment information for candidate ranking and improves the discrimination of subtle semantic differences.

</details>


### [16] [CAVC: Convergence-Adaptive Audio-Visual Cache for Modality-Asymmetric Diffusion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30854)
*Lifan Jiang,Yibo Zhao,Senlin cheng,Bolei Xu,Xiaobo Li,Can Ye,Dehai Zhao,Deng Cai,Boxi Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出训练无关的模态非对称缓存框架CAVC，依据音视频可恢复性差异，对音频分支中段缓存、视频分支全程精修，实现1.10–1.35倍加速并改善视觉动态指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散式音视频生成在整条采样轨迹上对所有时间步统一执行两模态的独立去噪分支与跨模态注意力，计算冗余大。作者观察到关键的"模态非对称可恢复性"：中段缓存引起的音频漂移可被少数末端联合去噪步骤显著修正，而视频侧因缓存导致的退化会造成运动与时序细节的持续丢失。因此统一联合计算并非必要，推理加速应只缓存可恢复的音频侧计算而保留视频侧完整精修。

Method: CAVC 为无需训练的非对称缓存框架，采用"三明治"式计算分配：先检测音频去噪器输出进入"缓存容忍区"，随后在中段缓存并复用音频去噪器输出，跳过音频去噪与跨模态注意力，同时视频分支保持独立的时空精修；末端恢复少数联合去噪步以削减累积的跨模态漂移、提升同步性。

Result: 在多个公开基准上取得1.10–1.35倍实际耗时（wall-clock）加速，若干视觉动态与时序统计指标有所提升，同时保持具竞争力的音频保真度与音视频对齐性能。

Conclusion: 基于模态可恢复性非对称性的选择性缓存是音视频扩散生成推理加速的有效途径：对可恢复模态激进缓存、对不可恢复模态保留全程精修，可在不训练、不显著损失质量的前提下获得稳定的实际加速。

Abstract: Diffusion-based audio-visual generation methods typically perform joint denoising of audio and video latents throughout the entire sampling trajectory, repeatedly executing modality-specific denoising branches and cross-modal attention at every timestep. However, we observe that such uniform joint computation is not always necessary. The key is a modality-asymmetric recoverability: audio-side drift introduced by mid-stage caching can be substantially reduced by only a few terminal joint denoising steps, whereas video-side degradation caused by caching leads to persistent loss of motion and temporal details. This asymmetry suggests that inference acceleration should selectively cache recoverable audio-side computation while preserving full video refinement. Motivated by this observation, we propose \textbf{Convergence-Adaptive Audio-Visual Cache (CAVC)}, a training-free modality-asymmetric caching framework. CAVC adopts a sandwich-structured computation allocation: after detecting that audio denoiser outputs enter a cache-tolerant regime, it caches and reuses the audio denoiser output to bypass audio denoising and cross-modal attention in the middle stage, while the video branch continues independent spatio-temporal refinement; a few joint steps are then restored at the end to reduce accumulated cross-modal drift and improve synchronization. Experiments across multiple public benchmarks demonstrate that CAVC achieves 1.10--1.35x wall-clock acceleration, improves several visual dynamics and temporal statistics, and maintains competitive audio fidelity and audio-visual alignment.

</details>


### [17] [Loop to Look Better: Learning Adaptive Depth for Question-Guided Perception in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30824)
*Wencheng Ye,Anning Hu,Hongye Fang,Tianyi Wang,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoopLook 是一个轻量级编码器端循环感知框架，让预训练 VLM 获得自适应视觉深度，通过循环重访编码器中后段层、注入问题表示、学习式停止机制与 token 级 Mixture-of-Looks，将视觉感知从一次性单遍编码转变为按需迭代精炼。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 虽支持更长的推理链，但视觉 token 仍由单次、任务无关的编码器前向得到，形成"证据获取瓶颈"：模型可能推理更久，却未精炼回答问题所需的视觉证据。作者希望把循环机制从推理端回推到感知端，使视觉表示能根据问题与后续处理需求迭代精炼。

Method: 提出 LoopLook：在视觉编码器的中后段层窗口上进行循环重访；每一轮循环注入问题表示以引导视觉精炼；引入可学习的停止机制自适应确定循环深度；并设计 token 级 Mixture-of-Looks（MoL）融合不同循环步的表示，使每个视觉 token 选择性整合跨步信息。整体为轻量级、可插拔于预训练 VLM 的方案。

Result: 在 4 个骨干模型、8 个基准上一致优于基线，MMStar 最高提升 8.6%，HallusionBench 最高提升 8.1%。分析显示 LoopLook 渐进式精炼视觉注意力，并按任务难度自适应分配深度；在降低输入分辨率时能更好保留任务相关视觉信息，而首 token 时延（TTFT）仅增加 5.1%–6.5%。

Conclusion: 将循环从推理引入感知是有效且低开销的方向：LoopLook 把视觉感知由一次性"一瞥"转变为自适应的循环精炼过程，在性能、鲁棒性与效率间取得较好平衡。

Abstract: Visual perception should be iterative and task-directed, with representations refined in response to the question and the need for further processing. While Vision-Language Models (VLMs) increasingly support extended reasoning, their visual tokens are still typically acquired in a single, task-agnostic encoder pass. This creates an evidence-acquisition bottleneck: models may reason longer without refining the visual evidence needed to answer the question. We propose LoopLook, a lightweight encoder-side recurrent perception framework that equips pretrained VLMs with adaptive visual depth. LoopLook recurrently revisits a middle-to-late window of the vision encoder, injecting question representations at each loop to guide visual refinement. A learned stopping mechanism adaptively determines the recurrent depth, while a token-wise Mixture-of-Looks (MoL) combines representations from different loops, allowing each visual token to selectively integrate information acquired across recurrent steps. Across four backbones and eight benchmarks, LoopLook consistently improves performance over the baseline, yielding gains of up to 8.6% on MMStar and 8.1% on HallusionBench. Our analysis shows that LoopLook progressively refines visual attention and adaptively allocates depth according to task difficulty. It also better preserves task-relevant visual information under reduced input resolution, while increasing time to first token (TTFT) by only 5.1-6.5%. By bringing recurrence from reasoning back into perception, LoopLook transforms visual perception from a one-shot glimpse into an adaptive process of recurrent refinement.

</details>


### [18] [WovenAnchor Matcher: Specialized Intra- and Inter-Image Context Modeling for Feature Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30799)
*Zhiyang Li,Ruijiang Jin,Thibaut Klenke,Yusuke Sekikawa,Nakamasa Inoue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WAM：通过角色专门化的上下文聚合（WovenMamba 做图内传播、AnchorCrossAttention 做图间检索）提升半稠密局部特征匹配，MegaDepth 上 66.1 pose AUC@5°，较 JamMa 提升 2.0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半稠密匹配在粗到细对应估计前统一聚合上下文，但图内聚合与图间聚合职责不同：图内需在单图内传播空间证据并保留结构化二维依赖；图间需从潜在对应区域收集信息并抑制无关位置噪声。作者认为应针对二者设计专门算子。

Method: 提出 WovenAnchor Matcher（WAM）框架，含两个互补算子：(1) WovenMamba 以"先水平后垂直"的状态空间传播实现图内聚合，垂直更新作用于已水平上下文化的特征，从而形成结构化二维感受野偏置；(2) AnchorCrossAttention 实现图间聚合，用 cross-attention 在图像对的潜在对应区域间交换上下文，交换在局部代表性锚点上进行，锚点先聚合各窗口内的特征证据。

Result: MegaDepth 上达到 66.1 pose AUC@5°，相同评测设置下较 JamMa 基线提升 2.0 AUC 点。消融显示移除或替换任一专门化组件均导致精度下降，表明图内传播与图间检索受益于角色特定的聚合设计。

Conclusion: 按角色分工设计上下文聚合是有效且必要的：结构化二维图内传播与锚点式图间检索互补，共同提升半稠密匹配精度。代码与预训练权重已开源。

Abstract: Semi-dense local feature matching commonly aggregates contextual information before coarse-to-fine correspondence estimation. However, intra-image and inter-image aggregation serve different roles: the former should propagate spatial evidence within each image while retaining structured two-dimensional dependencies, whereas the latter should gather information from plausible corresponding regions while limiting noise from unrelated locations. We propose WovenAnchor Matcher (WAM), a semi-dense matching framework based on role-specialized context aggregation. WAM introduces two complementary operators. WovenMamba performs intra-image aggregation through horizontal-then-vertical state-space propagation, allowing vertical updates to operate on horizontally contextualized features and thereby encouraging a structured two-dimensional receptive-field bias. AnchorCrossAttention performs inter-image aggregation by using cross-attention to gather context from plausible corresponding regions across the image pair. It performs this exchange over local representative anchors that aggregate feature evidence within each window. On MegaDepth, WAM achieves 66.1 pose AUC@5$^\circ$, improving on the JamMa baseline by 2.0 AUC points under the same evaluation setup. Ablations show that removing or replacing either specialized component reduces accuracy, providing evidence that intra-image propagation and inter-image retrieval benefit from role-specific aggregation designs. Code and pretrained weights are available at https://github.com/mmaiLab/WovenAnchorMatcher.

</details>


### [19] [RaSFNet: Ray-aligned and Scan-aware Fusion Network for LiDAR Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31186)
*Chuang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RaSFNet，将点云几何与LiDAR扫描特性融入投影式语义分割：用RAGDE在射线对齐坐标系下编码frustum内点分布，用STAM按方位角采样模式组织frustum特征并用Mamba建模跨扫描区域长程依赖，用RCPFF以共享距离信息约束点-投影特征融合；在SemanticKITTI与nuScenes验证集上分别取得70.2%与77.7% mIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投影式方法存在三类问题：(1) 投影时多点聚合使frustum内几何差异被抹平；(2) 局部特征提取难以建模跨扫描区域的空间关系；(3) 投影空间邻接不等于3D空间邻近，点特征与投影特征交互时会混入不同距离范围的特征，削弱语义可分性。

Method: 核心为三个模块：RAGDE（Ray-Aligned Geometric Distribution Encoding）在射线对齐坐标系下刻画每个frustum内的点分布；STAM（Scan-Topology-Aware Mamba）依据LiDAR方位角采样模式组织frustum特征序列，捕捉跨扫描区域的长程依赖；RCPFF（Range-Constrained Point-Frustum Fusion）利用共享的距离(range)信息指导点与frustum表示间的特征交换，保持空间对应关系。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI验证集取得70.2% mIoU，在nuScenes验证集取得77.7% mIoU，与所对比方法相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 将LiDAR几何分布与扫描拓扑先验显式引入投影式分割框架，并借助距离约束的融合机制，可有效缓解投影聚合的信息损失与跨距离特征混淆，从而提升点云语义分割性能。

Abstract: LiDAR point cloud semantic segmentation is a fundamental component of 3D perception for autonomous driving and robotics. Projection-based methods map 3D point clouds into 2D representations for feature extraction using 2D networks. However, aggregating multiple points during projection can obscure geometric differences within each frustum, while local feature extraction limits the modeling of spatial relationships across scanning regions. Moreover, proximity in the projected representation does not necessarily imply proximity in 3D space, and interactions between point and projected features may mix features from different ranges, reducing semantic discriminability. To address these challenges, we propose the Ray-aligned and Scan-aware Fusion Network (RaSFNet), which incorporates point cloud geometry and LiDAR scanning characteristics into projection-based semantic segmentation. Specifically, the Ray-Aligned Geometric Distribution Encoding (RAGDE) module characterizes point distributions within each frustum using a ray-aligned coordinate system. To model spatial context, a Scan-Topology-Aware Mamba (STAM) block organizes frustum features according to the LiDAR azimuth sampling pattern and captures long-range dependencies across scanning regions. In addition, a Range-Constrained Point-Frustum Fusion (RCPFF) mechanism uses shared range information to guide feature exchange between point and frustum representations while maintaining spatial correspondence. RaSFNet achieves mIoU scores of 70.2% and 77.7% on the SemanticKITTI and nuScenes validation sets, respectively, indicating competitive segmentation performance compared with the evaluated methods.

</details>


### [20] [HiSTEVE 1.0: Harnessing Human-Interface VLA Agents for Open-World Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31162)
*Zecong Tang,Zian Guan,Yuncheng Guo,Zhanqiu Zhang,Yiwen Guo,Bo Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 面向Minecraft等开放式游戏，提出分层VLA智能体HiSTEVE，用VLM规划器+结构化记忆做orchestration、单一VLA做原生键鼠视觉运动控制，并引入regime-specific RMSNorm分支与独立鼠标token嵌入缓解GUI/非GUI干扰，在MineStudio Bench达85.0%、OpenHA平均37.4%，并能从零合成铁镐完成长程任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建可在开放式游戏中像人类玩家一样行动的虚拟具身智能体，需要同时具备长时程自主性与可靠的原生界面控制。现有智能体往往割裂二者：或只做原生视觉运动控制而缺乏长程自主，或依赖环境API/预定义技能做高层规划。Minecraft作为程序化生成开放世界，是研究二者融合的天然测试台。

Method: 提出HiSTEVE分层VLA智能体，配以能力感知的agent harness。编排层：harness含结构化记忆跟踪进度，VLM规划器在VLA能力范围内选择子任务；并引入policy-coupled任务完成状态检测器，基于VLA策略上下文信号判定候选完成。控制层：单一VLA以纯视觉感知+原生键鼠动作执行全部子任务；为缓解异构GUI与非GUI交互干扰，采用regime-specific RMSNorm分支与独立鼠标移动token嵌入。训练上通过世界知识注入、模仿学习，以及带环境反馈的强化学习提升可靠性与恢复能力。

Result: 达到SOTA原子任务性能：MineStudio Bench 85.0%，OpenHA平均成功率37.4%；并像人类玩家一样从零完成合成铁镐等长程任务。

Conclusion: 将长程自主编排与原生接口VLA控制统一于单一分层框架，是构建开放式游戏通用具身智能体的可行路径；能力边界约束下的规划与策略耦合的完成判定对可靠性至关重要。

Abstract: Building virtual embodied agents that operate in open-ended games like human players is a long-standing goal requiring both long-horizon autonomy and reliable native-interface control. As a procedurally generated open world, Minecraft provides a natural testbed for studying the integration of these capabilities. Existing agents typically provide either native visuomotor control without long-horizon autonomy or high-level planning executed through environment APIs or predefined skills. To bridge this gap, we propose HiSTEVE, a hierarchical VLA agent with a capability-aware agent harness. At the orchestration level, we equip the harness with structured memory for tracking progress and a VLM planner for selecting subtasks within the VLA's capabilities. To reliably determine subtask completion, we further introduce a policy-coupled task completion state detector that conditions on VLA policy context to signal candidate completion. At the control level, a single VLA executes all subtasks with vision-only perception and native keyboard-and-mouse actions. To mitigate interference between heterogeneous GUI and non-GUI interactions, we introduce regime-specific RMSNorm branches and separate mouse-movement token embeddings. We also train the VLA through world knowledge injection, imitation learning, and reinforcement learning with environment feedback to improve reliability and recovery. HiSTEVE achieves state-of-the-art atomic-task performance, scoring 85.0% on MineStudio Bench and a 37.4% average success rate on OpenHA, while also completing long-horizon tasks such as crafting an iron pickaxe from scratch like human players.

</details>


### [21] [I$^2$-KAN: Interpretable Intra- and Inter-Modal Multi-Pathway Representation Learning for Multimodal Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31087)
*Xiaoting Xie,Feng Qiao,Xianwei Zhang,Hailong Chen,Kaiwen Zheng,Joemon M. Jose,Xuri Ge,Yujun Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可解释多通路框架I²-KAN，将模态内与成对模态间信息分置于独立结构通路，通过TDL-GDB精炼各通路表示并采用结构化KAN融合，揭示模态效用依赖交互：单模态独立预测效用低并不意味着交互条件下效用低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有融合方法将模态专属表示与跨模态交互混合为联合表示，难以区分预测效用来自模态本身还是来自其与其他模态的交互；且模态的独立预测效用可能无法真实反映其交互条件下的预测效用。

Method: I²-KAN：显式保持模态内与成对模态间信息于相互独立的结构通路；每条通路经任务解耦线性门控去噪瓶颈（TDL-GDB）学习紧凑且任务相关的表示；随后采用结构化Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（KAN）融合，保留显式通路关联并支持多层次解释。

Result: 在CMU-MOSI、CMU-MOSEI与CH-SIMS v2上取得强劲整体性能；消融与可解释性分析在三个基准上一致表明：独立预测效用低并不意味着交互条件下预测效用低，孤立看似弱的模态仍可通过成对交互提供信息。

Conclusion: 多模态效用具有交互依赖性，应从独立效用与交互条件效用两个视角共同刻画模态效用；结构化分离通路+可解释融合是刻画该性质的有效途径。

Abstract: Multimodal sentiment analysis (MSA) aims to infer sentiment by integrating linguistic, acoustic, and visual information. However, many fusion methods integrate modality-specific representations and cross-modal interactions into joint representations, making it difficult to determine whether predictive utility arises from a modality itself or from its interactions with others. More fundamentally, a modality's standalone predictive utility may not faithfully reflect its interaction-conditioned predictive utility. To address this issue, we propose I$^2$-KAN, an interpretable multi-pathway framework that explicitly maintains intra-modal and pairwise inter-modal information in separate structural pathways. Each pathway is refined by a Task-Decoupled Linear-Gated Denoising Bottleneck (TDL-GDB) to learn compact and task-relevant representations, followed by structured Kolmogorov-Arnold Network fusion that retains explicit pathway associations and supports multi-level interpretation. Extensive experiments on CMU-MOSI, CMU-MOSEI, and CH-SIMS v2 demonstrate strong overall performance. Ablation and interpretability analyses consistently show across the three benchmarks that low standalone predictive utility does not necessarily imply low interaction-conditioned predictive utility: a modality that appears weak in isolation can remain informative through pairwise interactions. These findings highlight the interaction-dependent nature of multimodal utility and suggest that modality utility should be characterized from both standalone and interaction-conditioned perspectives.

</details>


### [22] [A General-Purpose Dual-Path Interaction Framework for Multi-View Medical Image Segmentation: Alleviating Data Volume Dependency in Small-Sample Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31065)
*Shuhuan Wang,Yuanyuan Cui,Pengju Wang,Huimin Guo,Ling Yue,Yin Gu,Yanchao Zhang,Wei Feng,Yunhe Liu,Wei Qian,Mingjun Xu,Shuang Liu,He Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出通用即插即用的双路径交互框架DPIF，通过跨视图分布对齐、条件互信息引导的特征解耦与持续同调拓扑正则，缓解多视图医学图像分割的标注依赖，在40%-60%数据量下达到全量单视图模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分割依赖大量标注数据，低样本场景下性能显著下降；现有半监督/弱监督方法依赖特定网络结构，传统多视图辅助分割要求严格配对样本，限制临床应用。因此需要与任意编码器-解码器网络兼容、且不要求样本级配对的多视图框架。

Method: DPIF包含三个模块：(1) 基于Sinkhorn最优传输的跨视图分布对齐，在无样本级配对下建立全局语义对应；(2) 条件互信息引导的特征解耦，仅在共享语义空间内进行双向知识迁移，抑制视图特有噪声；(3) 基于持续同调（persistent homology）的拓扑正则，将解剖不变性编码为无监督先验以缓解过拟合。

Result: 在私有 multicenter 超声心动图数据集及两个公开数据集上，使用24个基线模型验证：DPIF增强模型在同数据量下优于单视图基线；多数双路径变体仅用40%-60%数据即达到全量数据单视图模型的性能。

Conclusion: DPIF为数据稀缺条件下的多视图医学图像分割提供了一种通用、可扩展的解决方案，具备即插即用特性与对网络结构、样本配对的低依赖。

Abstract: The performance of medical image segmentation depends on a large amount of annotated data, and most segmentation models experience a significant drop in performance in low-sample-size scenarios. Existing semi-supervised and weakly supervised methods rely on specific network architectures, while traditional multi-view-assisted segmentation methods require strictly paired samples, which limits their clinical applications. To address these issues, we propose DPIF, a general-purpose plug-and-play dual-path interaction framework compatible with arbitrary encoder-decoder segmentation networks, which leverages cross-view anatomical complementarity to reduce annotation reliance. DPIF first performs Sinkhorn optimal transport-based cross-view distribution alignment to establish global semantic correspondence without sample-level pairing. It then adopts conditional mutual information-guided feature decoupling to enable bidirectional knowledge transfer only within the shared semantic space, suppressing view-specific noise. Finally, persistent homology-based topological regularization encodes anatomical invariants as unsupervised priors to mitigate overfitting. Experiments were conducted using 24 baseline models on a private multicenter echocardiography dataset and two public datasets. The results show that DPIF-enhanced models outperform single-view baseline models when trained on comparable amounts of data. Notably, most dual-path variants trained on 40\% - 60\% of the data achieve performance comparable to that of single-view models trained on the full dataset. This demonstrates that DPIF provides a general-purpose and scalable solution for data-scarce multi-view medical image segmentation problems.

</details>


### [23] [Reading, Writing, Sharing, and Interacting with the World through Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31668)
*Shengqiong Wu,Meng Luo,Bobo Li,Ziwei Liu,Shuicheng Yan,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Philip Torr,Hao Fei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 一篇以"世界建模"为统一视角的视频基础模型与视频世界模型综述，将观测视频视为潜在时序世界的部分有损投影，围绕读、写、共享、交互四种操作组织文献，并指出研究重心从片段级识别与感知保真转向持久状态、可控演化、物理因果有效性与基于交互的评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频基础模型侧重理解与生成，视频世界模型侧重刻画世界动态，二者目标不同但共同关心"视频观测与演化世界的关系"。现有综述缺乏能同时统摄二者的框架，且常混淆"序列时序连贯"与"世界状态一致"。

Method: 提出统一透镜：将观测视频建模为潜在时序世界的部分、有损、时序有序投影；按四种互补操作组织文献——读（从视频推断世界属性）、写（由指定或学习到的世界条件合成视频）、共享（以共同建模机制整合理解与生成）、交互（在动作与演化观测下预测并更新世界状态）；并在任务、表示、模型、数据集与基准层面进行梳理。

Result: 给出四操作分类体系与跨任务/表示/数据/基准的系统对照；明确区分观测或生成序列的时序连贯性与潜在世界状态一致性；梳理出研究重心的迁移趋势。

Conclusion: 世界建模可作为统一视频理解与生成的框架，研究趋向持久状态、可控演化、物理与因果有效性及交互式下游评测；并提出由此衍生的开放挑战。

Abstract: Video foundation models have advanced rapidly in understanding and generating visual sequences, while video world models seek to capture the underlying dynamics of the world they depict. Despite their different objectives, both concern the relationship between video observations and an evolving world. This survey adopts world modeling as a unifying lens for examining these developments. Viewing an observed video as a partial, lossy, and temporally ordered projection of a latent temporal world, we organize the literature around four complementary operations: reading infers world properties from observed video; writing synthesizes video from specified or learned world conditions; sharing integrates understanding and generation through common modeling mechanisms; and interacting predicts and updates world states under actions and evolving observations. This perspective distinguishes temporal coherence in observed or generated sequences from consistency in the underlying world state. Across tasks, representations, models, datasets, and benchmarks, we trace a shift in emphasis from clip-level recognition and perceptual fidelity toward persistent state, controllable evolution, physical and causal validity, and evaluation grounded in interaction and downstream decisions, and we distill the open challenges that follow.

</details>


### [24] [WorldForge V2: Eliciting Camera-Trajectory Alignment in Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31640)
*Chenxi Song,Yanming Yang,Tong Zhao,Ruibo Li,Joey Tianyi Zhou,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldForge V2 提出训练无关的引导框架与 Trajectory LoRA，在预训练视频扩散模型上实现精确相机控制，同时保持场景外观与物体运动的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视频扩散模型蕴含丰富的3D/4D先验，但用于空间任务时受限于相机控制不精确、时空不一致、相机运动与场景动态纠缠；现有微调与后处理warping方法易产生伪影、泛化差或计算代价高。

Method: 1) 训练无关引导框架：用warped reference沿目标相机轨迹迭代精化生成；2) 通过光流分析识别与运动相关的latent通道进行选择性引导，分离运动控制与外观；3) 双路策略比较有引导/无引导预测以校正不完美参考带来的伪影；4) v-cache用前一步去噪预测近似校正，省去额外模型评估；5) 将运动定位从latent通道扩展到网络block，提出Trajectory LoRA聚焦于对轨迹引导最敏感的block，并采用两阶段训练让适配器学习去噪引导生成中遇到的状态。

Result: 仅需极少训练数据（单一训练场景即可泛化到未见场景）；在多个数据集上定量与定性评估均达到相机控制视频生成的最优生成质量与轨迹精度。

Conclusion: 通过选择性latent引导、双路校正与block级Trajectory LoRA，可在保留预训练视频模型生成能力的前提下实现可靠的相机控制，兼顾效率与泛化性。

Abstract: Video diffusion models have learned rich 3D/4D priors, making them promising for scene generation and video re-rendering. However, their use in spatial tasks is limited by imprecise camera control, spatial and temporal inconsistencies, and entangled camera motion with scene dynamics. Existing approaches, such as task-specific fine-tuning and post-process warping, often introduce visual artifacts, generalize poorly, or incur high computational costs. We propose WorldForge V2 to achieve accurate camera control while preserving the generative capabilities of pretrained video models. We first develop a training-free guidance framework using warped references to iteratively refine generation along a target camera path. Optical-flow analysis identifies motion-related latent channels for selective guidance, helping separate motion control from appearance. A dual-path strategy further compares guided and unguided predictions to correct artifacts from imperfect references. Together, these components guide viewpoint changes while preserving scene appearance and object motion. To reduce refinement costs, v-cache approximates the correction using the preceding denoising prediction, avoiding an additional model evaluation. We further extend motion localization from latent channels to network blocks and introduce Trajectory LoRA, which concentrates adaptation on blocks most responsive to trajectory guidance. A two-stage training strategy teaches these adapters to denoise the states encountered during guided generation, using limited supervision effectively. This enables reliable camera control with very little training data, with a single training scene already supporting generalization to unseen scenes. Quantitative and qualitative evaluations across multiple datasets show that our method achieves state-of-the-art generation quality and trajectory accuracy in camera-controlled video generation.

</details>


### [25] [F2G-Pose: Geometry-Aware Foundation Feature Lifting for Direct RGB-D Category-Level Object Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31623)
*Jinyu Zhang,Haitao Lin,Jiashu Hou,Xiangyang Xue,Yanwei Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: F2G-Pose 是一个实时单次前馈的类别级 RGB-D 物体位姿估计框架，可将视觉基础特征提升至点云并结合 DGCNN 与 MoE Transformer，一次前向同时输出位姿、度量尺寸与相机系形状，在 SOPE/ROPE 上达 SOTA，pose-only 推理 28 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类别级位姿估计因类内差异、对称性与部分观测而高度歧义；现有方法难以兼顾可靠性：基于中间结构的方法易误差传播，扩散模型推理延迟高且需复杂事后候选提取。

Method: 单次前向直接回归：将稠密视觉基础特征（frozen RADIO backbone）提升到部分点云上；用 DGCNN tokenization 编码局部几何；用 Mixture-of-Experts Transformer 融合物体级结构；联合预测尺寸并做相机系形状补全，以稠密几何监督保证度量一致性；任务网络仅在带位姿标注的合成 SOPE 数据上训练。

Result: 在 SOPE 与 ROPE 上取得 SOTA 精度，pose-only 推理 28 FPS；在 HouseCat6D 上展示强相机系几何能力，在 HANDAL 上展示真实物体迁移；延迟、鲁棒性、形状补全及 GenPose++ 候选行为分析验证效率与可靠性，真实机器人操作实验验证部署潜力。

Conclusion: 以单次前馈替代中间结构/扩散范式，可在实时条件下同时获得高精度位姿、度量尺寸与形状补全，具备良好的泛化与真实部署潜力。

Abstract: This paper studies category-level RGB-D object pose estimation, which recovers an object's 3D rotation and translation without instance-specific CAD models or reference views. This task is highly ambiguous due to intra-class variations, symmetries, and partial observations. Current solutions struggle to balance reliability and efficiency: methods based on intermediate structures are prone to error propagation, while diffusion models suffer from high inference latency and complex post-hoc candidate extraction. To address this, we present F2G-Pose, a real-time direct, single-pass framework that predicts category-level pose, metric size, and camera-frame completed shape from a single RGB-D observation in one feed-forward pass. F2G-Pose lifts dense visual foundation features to partial point clouds, encodes local geometry with DGCNN tokenization, and integrates object-level structure with a Mixture-of-Experts Transformer. Joint size prediction and camera-frame shape completion encourage metric consistency through dense geometric supervision. With its task-specific network trained only on pose-annotated synthetic SOPE data and its frozen RADIO backbone externally pretrained, F2G-Pose achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on SOPE and ROPE while running at 28 FPS for pose-only inference, and further demonstrates strong camera-frame geometry on HouseCat6D and real-object transfer on HANDAL. Analyses of latency, robustness, shape completion, and GenPose++ candidate behavior further demonstrate the efficiency and reliability of F2G-Pose, with real-world manipulation experiments demonstrating deployment potential under segmented RGB-D inputs. The project page, code, and pretrained models are made available at https://jrryzh.github.io/F2G-Pose/.

</details>


### [26] [Residual Calibration via Local Feature-Space Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30652)
*Xihao Wang,Chengyao Yu,Ruixing Ming*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出后验残差校准框架 LAPE，以全局校准器输出为锚点，利用特征空间中邻近校准样本的非参数局部证据精化预测，并通过 CFAF 扩展到多视图输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络置信度估计不可靠，需后验校准；现有后验方法多依赖分数变换，主要改善边际校准，难以捕捉特征空间中局部区域的空间变化性误校准。

Method: LAPE 将全局校准器输出作为稳定锚点，结合邻域校准样本的非参数局部证据对单条预测进行残差式精化；作为即插即用模块，可复用构建全局校准器的校准数据并利用特征空间几何信息；进一步提出级联特征增强融合（CFAF）以支持多视图输入。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法可提升多种现有基线的校准质量，并在不同设置下保持高效与鲁棒。

Conclusion: 局部自适应残差校准是改进后验校准有效且通用的思路，兼具即插即用性与多视图可扩展性。

Abstract: Deep neural networks often struggle to provide reliable confidence estimates, making post-hoc calibration essential for reliable decision-making. Existing post-hoc methods largely rely on score-based transformations, which improve marginal calibration but often fail to capture spatially varying miscalibration in local regions of the learned feature space. To address this gap, we propose Local Adaptive Probability Estimation (LAPE), a post-hoc residual calibration framework. Specifically, LAPE treats the global calibrator output as a stable anchor and refines the individual prediction by leveraging non-parametric local evidence from neighboring calibration samples. As a plug-and-play refinement module, LAPE effectively utilizes the information about feature-space geometry and can reuse the calibration data that are used to construct the global calibrator. Furthermore, we extend LAPE to the setting with multi-view inputs through Cascade Feature Augmentation Fusion (CFAF). Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed methods can improve the calibration quality of various existing baselines and remain efficient and robust across different settings.

</details>


### [27] [CODI: Improving Self-Supervised Vision Transformers with Cross Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30639)
*Siran Dai,Qianqian Xu,Peisong Wen,Yang Liu,Xiaochun Cao,Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究自监督ViT中图像级与patch级表征的失配问题，提出Cross Distillation（CODI），在不改变架构的前提下，将少量教师class token分布混入每个教师patch token目标，使被掩码patch获得与注意力无关的全局语义校正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督ViT能学到强图像级表征，但patch级表征在密集预测中不可靠。作者发现class token将全局语义集中在少数代表性patch上，而密集预测错误常出现在低注意力区域。

Method: 局部梯度分析：沿[CLS]注意力读出路径，图像级自蒸馏梯度由[CLS]到patch的注意力质量加权。据此提出CODI，将少量教师class-token分布混入每个教师patch-token目标，保持标准自蒸馏架构不变。

Result: 在对齐的预训练设置下，密集预测与图像级基准实验中，CODI同时提升了密集预测和图像级性能。

Conclusion: CODI通过跨级蒸馏为目标patch注入全局语义，缓解了图像级与patch级表征失配，且无需修改模型架构，是一种简洁有效的改进方案。

Abstract: Self-supervised Vision Transformers learn strong image-level representations, but their patch-level representations can be unreliable for dense prediction. We study this mismatch and find that the class token concentrates global semantics on a small number of representative patches, while dense prediction errors often occur on low-attention regions. We provide a local gradient analysis showing that, along the [CLS] attention readout, image-level self-distillation gradients are weighted by [CLS]-to-patch attention mass. To provide masked patches with global semantics without changing the architecture, we propose Cross Distillation (CODI), which mixes a small amount of the teacher class-token distribution into each teacher patch-token target. CODI gives every masked patch an attention-independent semantic correction while preserving the standard self-distillation architecture. Experiments on dense and image-level benchmarks show that CODI improves both dense and image-level performance under aligned pretraining settings. Code is available at https://github.com/EldercatSAM/CODI.

</details>


### [28] [MoRE: Motion-Regularized Encoding for Articulated 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30337)
*Guantian Zheng,Shan Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoRE 提出一种运动正则化的关节编码，用坐标对齐关节线（轴分类 + 两个轴正交 Plücker 矩系数）表示关节，并通过可微微分期望将属性重建与其诱导的运动耦合，实现单图铰接物体生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图生成铰接物体需同时恢复外观与驱动部件运动的机构；仅对关节属性做紧凑编码并不能保证重建结果保留相应运动，因此需要让关节表示学习受运动一致性约束。

Method: 关节线采用坐标对齐表示：轴类型分类加两个轴正交 Plücker 矩系数，消除沿轴原点选择的任意性。表示学习中，用解码出的关节假设移动对应三维锚点，并将其运动与标注关节的运动比较；以对关节类型和轴方向的可微微分期望，把属性重建与其诱导运动耦合。图像条件流模型学习得到的功能潜变量，多状态自适应高斯模型提供外观。推理时用恢复的部件分配与关节驱动同一组 rest-pose 高斯，无需逐状态重新生成。

Result: 在 PartNet-Mobility 与 ACD 上，部件包围盒一致性、铰接状态几何与视觉相似度均优于对比基线；关节预测相比 ArtiLatent 降低轴、原点与范围误差。消融实验支持关节线编码设计，并表明运动监督降低了锚点运动误差。

Conclusion: 以运动监督正则化关节属性编码可有效提升单图铰接物体生成的关节精度与几何/视觉质量，且单组 rest-pose 高斯即可支持多状态铰接渲染。

Abstract: Generating an articulated object from a single image requires recovering both its appearance and the mechanism governing its parts. A compact encoding of joint attributes does not by itself ensure that reconstruction preserves the resulting motion. We present MoRE, Motion-Regularized Encoding for articulated 3D generation. MoRE represents coordinate-aligned joint lines with axis classification and two axis-orthogonal Plücker moment coefficients, removing the arbitrary choice of an origin along the axis. During representation learning, decoded joint hypotheses are used to move corresponding three-dimensional anchors, and their motions are compared with those of annotated joints. A differentiable expectation over joint types and axis directions couples attribute reconstruction to the motion it induces. An image-conditioned flow learns the resulting functional latents, while a multi-state-adapted Gaussian model supplies appearance. At inference, recovered part assignments and joints articulate a single set of rest-pose Gaussians without regenerating the object for each state. Experiments on PartNet-Mobility and ACD show stronger part-box agreement, articulated-state geometry and visual similarity than the compared baselines, while joint prediction reduces axis, origin and range errors relative to ArtiLatent. Controlled ablations support the joint-line encoding and show that motion supervision reduces anchor-motion error. Code and pretrained checkpoints will be released in the future.

</details>


### [29] [LACToS: Local Anomaly Detection via Conditional Typicality of Superpixels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31538)
*Jihoon Hong,Alireza Kheirandish,Rohan Sanda,Sara Fridovich-Keil*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于统计典型性（typicality）的局部OOD检测方法LACToS，通过超像素分组与高效代理估计提升多模态inlier下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有局部异常检测方法多针对工业缺陷检测场景设计，在其他场景（尤其inlier分布呈多模态时）性能显著下降，需考虑inlier复杂性。

Method: 基于统计典型性的原则性局部OOD检测框架，并引入两项使其实用的创新：(i) 将像素分组为超像素；(ii) 使用计算高效的代理来估计典型性。

Result: 在医学影像、人脸和X射线安检三类应用基准上评估，LACToS相比已有方法表现更稳健。

Conclusion: 统计典型性结合超像素与高效代理估计可有效应对多模态inlier下的局部OOD检测，跨领域泛化性优于现有方法；代码已开源。

Abstract: Local anomaly detection is the task of identifying spatially confined anomalous or out-of-distribution (OOD) regions within an otherwise in-distribution (ID) image. Most existing methods are designed for a specific context, with a strong focus on industrial defect detection, where they achieve excellent performance on well-established benchmarks. However, we observe that these methods can exhibit significant performance drops in other contexts, especially when the inlier distribution is multimodal. To account for this inlier complexity, we propose LACToS, a principled local OOD detection method based on statistical typicality and made tractable through two innovations: (i) grouping pixels into superpixels and (ii) using a computationally efficient proxy to estimate typicality. We evaluate LACToS across three diverse applications spanning medical imaging, human faces, and X-ray security screening. LACToS performs more robustly than prior methods across these benchmarks. The code is released here: https://github.com/voilalab/LACToS.

</details>


### [30] [PiHand: Latent Hand-Object Interaction Priors for Physics-Based Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31299)
*Niklas Berndt,Xianghui Xie,Enes Duran,Yixiao Wang,Gerard Pons-Moll*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PiHand 提出一种物理落地、交互感知的灵巧手-物体运动合成框架：先训练追踪动捕演示的专家策略，再用目标条件 VAE 蒸馏为统一的手-物体交互先验，并在其潜空间中做有界残差探索求解多样操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成物理可信的灵巧手-物体交互对 VR/AR、游戏与机器人很重要，但难点在于需同时满足细微接触动力学、多样物体几何与目标轨迹，并保持自然抓取。现有方法要么用动捕轨迹追踪但难以迁移到新物体，要么仅依赖单手生成先验、无法刻画手-物体耦合运动，导致接触保真度与样本效率不足。

Method: 三阶段：1）在仿真中训练追踪动捕演示的专家策略；2）用目标条件 VAE 将专家策略蒸馏为统一的手-物体交互先验；3）在该潜空间中通过有界残差探索求解多样灵巧操作任务。仅使用单一奖励、无需复杂课程学习。

Result: 在 GRAB 和 DexYCB 上获得可泛化到未见物体与轨迹的通用桌面操作策略；同一先验可迁移到物体接取与双手交接这两个训练数据中罕见的任务；所有任务上各项指标均一致优于基线。

Conclusion: 将专家策略蒸馏为交互先验并在潜空间有界探索，可在简单奖励下实现高保真、可泛化、样本高效的灵巧手-物体运动合成，并具备跨任务迁移能力；将公开处理后的数据与模型权重。

Abstract: Synthesizing physically grounded dexterous hand-object interactions is important for VR/AR, gaming, and robotics, yet remains challenging because subtle contact dynamics, diverse object geometries, and target trajectories must be jointly respected while maintaining natural grasps. Existing methods either track mocap trajectories with limited transfer to novel objects, or rely on hand-only generative priors that fail to capture coupled hand-object motion, sacrificing contact fidelity and sample efficiency. To tackle these challenges, we introduce PiHand, a Physically grounded Interaction-aware Hand motion synthesis framework. PiHand first trains expert policies that track mocap demonstrations in simulation, distills them into a unified hand-object interaction prior using a goal-conditioned VAE, and finally solve diverse dexterous manipulation tasks through bounded residual exploration in its latent space. With only a single reward and no complex curriculum training, this yields general tabletop manipulation policies that generalize to unseen objects and trajectories on GRAB and DexYCB. The same prior transfers to object catching and bimanual handover, two tasks that are rarely seen in its training data. Across all tasks, PiHand consistently outperforms baselines in all metrics. We will release the processed data and model checkpoints.

</details>


### [31] [Retrieval-Augmented Online Textual Memory for Episodic Memory Video Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31284)
*Raffaele Cali,Giuseppe Lando,Rosario Forte,Antonino Furnari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在固定在线文本记忆（第一人称视频→时间锚定字幕）的前提下，系统研究检索在情景记忆视频问答中的作用，发现"父片段检索"（子块匹配、父字幕入上下文）以约一小部分文本记忆达到56.4%准确率；且更大检索预算提升Hit@K/Recall@K却可能降低准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时第一人称视频需将视觉流压缩为可高效检索、又保留答题证据的记忆表示；已有在线文本记忆框架中，检索环节的设计（检索单元、查询形式、上下文预算、重排）尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 以QAEgo4D-Closed多选题问答为评测，保持底层生成记忆不变、仅将检索作为唯一变量，比较不同检索单元、查询表述、上下文预算与重排策略。

Result: 最优配置Parent（先匹配细粒度字幕子块，再向推理器提供其完整父字幕）取得56.4%准确率，与强全记忆与检索基线相当，而答题时仅暴露少量文本记忆；分析显示增大检索预算虽提升时间Hit@K与Recall@K，却因引入视觉上可信的干扰项与不确定字幕证据而可能降低准确率。

Conclusion: 检索增强的第一人称记忆不应只优化证据召回，还需兼顾检索上下文的可靠性、具体性与答案判别性；应发展超越原始字幕检索的证据感知、结构化情景记忆。

Abstract: Long egocentric videos pose a central challenge for multimodal language models: the visual stream must be compressed into a memory representation that can be searched efficiently while still preserving the evidence needed for question answering. Building on an online textual-memory framework in which first-person videos are converted into temporally grounded captions, this work systematically studies the role of retrieval in episodic-memory video question answering. We evaluate how different retrieval units, query formulations, context budgets, and reranking strategies affect multiple-choice answering on QAEgo4D-Closed, isolating retrieval as the main variable while keeping the underlying generated memory fixed.
  Our best configuration, Parent, matches fine-grained caption subchunks, then provides their full parent captions to the reasoner. It achieves 56.4 % accuracy, matching strong full-memory and retrieval baselines while exposing only a small fraction of the textual memory at question time. More importantly, our analysis shows that better temporal coverage does not necessarily translate into better answers: increasing the retrieval budget improves temporal Hit@K and Recall@K, but can reduce accuracy by introducing visually plausible distractors and uncertain caption evidence. These findings suggest that retrieval-augmented egocentric memory should not be optimized only for evidence recall, but also for the reliability, specificity, and answer-discriminative value of the retrieved context. The study motivates more structured episodic memories that move beyond raw caption retrieval toward evidence-aware representations for long-form egocentric reasoning.

</details>


### [32] [Lightweight Frame Compensation for Real-Time Polyp Segmentation under Frame Skipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31228)
*Weihao Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出77K参数的即插即用轻量帧补偿模块LFC，插入冻结的分割骨干前，用三帧窗口经时空注意力融合为单张补偿帧，仅用伪标签训练，在保持2.05X–2.55X吞吐增益的同时恢复跳帧导致的精度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时结肠镜息肉分割需在严格延迟预算下部署高精度模型；跳帧推理（每K帧推理一次，其余帧复用或插值）可提升吞吐，但带来运动模糊与病灶错位，损害时间一致性。

Method: LFC为77K参数的可插拔模块，置于冻结分割骨干之前；在3帧窗口上通过时空注意力将连续帧融合为一张补偿帧；训练不依赖专家标注，仅使用由不同随机种子模型生成的伪标签。并在CNN、金字塔Transformer、全局注意力Transformer三类骨干上评估时间稳定性。

Result: 在三个息肉视频基准上，LFC恢复直接跳帧造成的大部分精度损失，部分设置下达到逐帧推理精度，同时保持2.05X–2.55X吞吐增益；三类骨干因局部/全局聚合方式不同呈现不同的跳帧敏感谱，LFC增益大小与该谱一致，对高容量Transformer骨干增益最大（其静态精度最佳但跳帧下降最严重）。

Conclusion: LFC为实时息肉分割提供轻量、可插拔、易部署的方案；骨干架构的局部—全局聚合方式决定了跳帧敏感性与补偿收益。

Abstract: Real-time polyp segmentation in computer-assisted colonoscopy is hampered by the difficulty of deploying high-accuracy models under strict latency budgets. Frame skipping, which performs inference every K frames and reuses or interpolates the rest, improves throughput but introduces motion blur and lesion misalignment that degrade temporal consistency. To close this gap, we propose the Lightweight Frame Compensator (LFC), a 77K-parameter plug-and-play module inserted before a frozen segmentation backbone. Operating on a three-frame window, LFC fuses consecutive frames via spatio-temporal attention into a single compensated frame, and is trained only on pseudo-labels generated by a differently seeded model, without any expert annotation. Experiments on three polyp video benchmarks show that LFC recovers most of the accuracy degraded by direct frame skipping and, in several settings, reaches the accuracy of frame-by-frame inference, while retaining a 2.05X--2.55X throughput gain. We further evaluate the temporal stability of three backbones spanning CNN, pyramid Transformer, and global-attention Transformer architectures, revealing a spectrum of sensitivity to frame skipping that arises from how locally or globally each architecture aggregates misaligned input. The compensation gain of LFC follows this spectrum: it is largest for high-capacity Transformer backbones, which achieve the best static accuracy but suffer the most under skip-frame inference. LFC thus offers a lightweight, pluggable, and deployment-friendly route to real-time polyp segmentation.

</details>


### [33] [GLOW-FM: Efficient Generative Low-Light Image Enhancement with One-Step Latent Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31222)
*Ariel Lapid,Yuval Haitman,Reuven Peretz,Arnon Netzer,Idit Diamant*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GLOW-FM：在冻结Tiny-VAE潜空间中进行一步式条件流匹配，利用Terminal Velocity Matching实现单次函数评估的生成式低光增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散式生成低光增强感知质量好，但迭代采样计算开销大；此前的效率改进仍难在单次函数评估与低总算力下应对多样真实低光场景。

Method: 提出GLOW-FM一步式潜空间流匹配框架：在冻结Tiny-VAE的潜空间中执行条件传输，并采用Terminal Velocity Matching（TVM）实现单步增强；与替代的一步目标进行匹配对比，并将TVM首次适配于图像复原/LLIE。

Result: 在具有挑战性的真实基准上取得有竞争力的图像质量与最优的跨数据集泛化能力，小模型变体仅需1次函数评估、34 GFLOPs。

Conclusion: 单步推理与紧凑潜空间建模使生成式LLIE更接近实际部署。

Abstract: Low-light image enhancement (LLIE) aims to recover well-exposed and perceptually faithful images from severely degraded observations. Recent diffusion-based generative methods achieve strong perceptual quality by leveraging powerful image priors, but their iterative sampling incurs substantial computational cost, limiting practical deployment. Despite recent efficiency gains in generative LLIE, high-quality enhancement with a single function evaluation and low overall computation remains challenging, particularly under diverse real-world low-light conditions. We introduce GLOW-FM, a one-step latent flow-matching framework for efficient generative LLIE. GLOW-FM performs conditional transport in the latent space of a frozen Tiny-VAE and leverages Terminal Velocity Matching (TVM) to enable enhancement with a single function evaluation. Matched comparisons with alternative one-step objectives support TVM as an effective design choice, and, to the best of our knowledge, this is its first adaptation to image restoration and specifically to LLIE. Extensive experiments on challenging real-world benchmarks demonstrate that GLOW-FM achieves competitive image quality and state-of-the-art cross-dataset generalization while requiring only a single function evaluation and 34 GFLOPs for the small variant. These findings highlight the potential of GLOW-FM to bring generative LLIE closer to practical deployment through single-step inference and compact latent modeling. Code will be made publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [34] [A Visual Grounding Benchmark for Retinal OCT with Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31205)
*Ronald Fecso,José Morano,Botond Fazekas,Paul Widmann-Sedlnitzky,Dorota Himel,Laetita Hinterhuber,Virginia Mares,Andreas Pollreisz,Hrvoje Bogunović*

Main category: cs.CV
