TL;DR: 该文提出首个视网膜OCT生物标志物视觉定位基准RetinaGroundBench（815张B-scan、4,537个框、13种标志物、8种疾病），并揭示27个开源MLLM定位能力普遍不足，进而提出OCT专用微调模型RetGrounder。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM虽能回答医学图像临床问题，但可信使用需视觉定位（visual grounding），即模型应定位其所报告的病灶而非依赖虚假捷径。然而MLLM能否在眼科最常用的OCT影像中实现临床发现的视觉定位，仍基本未被探索。

Method: 构建RetinaGroundBench：815张B-scan由眼科医生穷尽式标注，含4,537个边界框，覆盖8种疾病的13种生物标志物。针对模型常将单一病灶碎片化为多个局部预测的问题，提出AggIoU指标，评估重叠预测框的并集是否覆盖标注区域。评测27个开源MLLM（0.8–38B）。并开发RetGrounder：一个采用OCT专用策略、融合定位与文本信息微调的8B MLLM。

Result: 视觉定位仍基本未解决：最佳通用模型宏F1仅23.7%，召回率持续偏低，尽管其在生物标志物存在性问答上的平衡准确率可达73%。RetGrounder虽仅8B，却将定位F1提升至33.9%，相对提升43%。

Conclusion: RetinaGroundBench为MLLM用于OCT诊断的进展测量提供了参考基准，表明定位能力与存在性判别之间存在显著差距，专用微调策略可有效缩小该差距。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can answer clinical questions about medical images, but trustworthy use requires visual grounding: models should localize the pathology they report rather than rely on spurious shortcuts. Whether MLLMs can visually ground clinical findings in retinal optical coherence tomography (OCT), the most widely used imaging modality in ophthalmology, remains largely unexplored. To address this gap, we introduce RetinaGroundBench, the first benchmark for biomarker visual grounding in retinal OCT. It comprises 815 B-scans exhaustively annotated by ophthalmologists with 4,537 bounding boxes covering 13 biomarkers across 8 diseases. Because MLLMs often fragment a single lesion into multiple partial predictions, we propose AggIoU, a metric that evaluates whether the union of overlapping predicted boxes covers the annotated region. Across 27 open-source MLLMs (0.8-38B parameters), we find that visual grounding remains largely unsolved: the best general-purpose model achieves a macro F1 of just 23.7%, with consistently low recall, despite reaching up to 73% balanced accuracy on biomarker presence questions. To narrow this gap, we developed RetGrounder, an MLLM fine-tuned using an OCT-specific strategy integrating grounding and textual information. Despite its relatively small size (8B), RetGrounder improves grounding F1 to 33.9%, a 43% relative improvement. Our benchmark provides a reference point for measuring progress in the field of OCT diagnosis with MLLMs.

</details>


### [35] [DRAFT: Decoding and Reconstructing 3D Room Layouts from fMRI and Camera Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31108)
*Chi-Wei Lee,An-Kai Chen,Cheng-Han Chiang,Yi-Chun Chang,Po-Chih Kuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DRAFT方法，仅用fMRI信号与相机轨迹在无视觉帧条件下重建未见房间的3D布局，布局相关度r达0.480，优于仅用相机路径（0.446）及七种fMRI解码器（≤0.429），并可作为即插即用模块提升所有解码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有fMRI解码工作多聚焦于图像与3D物体重建，而从移动视角恢复房间的度量几何仍为空缺。核心难点在于快速视觉变化与模糊fMRI信号之间的时间不匹配，本文首次提出该任务以填补这一空白。

Method: DRAFT（Decoding and Reconstructing 3D room lAyouts from fMRI and Trajectories）：从早期视觉皮层提取紧凑的布局表征，并将其神经编码与由相机运动推导的几何自由空间相融合；评估指标为预测与光线投射对数深度间的Pearson相关（layout r）。

Result: 在10个留出场景、20名被试上，DRAFT的layout r为0.480，超过仅相机路径（0.446）与七种fMRI解码器（最高0.429）；作为即插模块无需重训练即可将七种解码器的layout r从0.171–0.429提升至0.487–0.493。

Conclusion: 脑活动与相机运动的结合能够恢复未见房间的度量布局，是迈向重建人所见3D空间的第一步。

Abstract: While functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) decoders reconstruct images and 3D objects, recovering the metric geometry of rooms from a moving viewpoint remains an open challenge. We introduce, to our knowledge for the first time, the task of reconstructing 3D layouts of held-out scenes using only the known camera trajectories and the fMRI signals of participants watching indoor walkthroughs, without any visual frames. The task is hard because of the temporal mismatch between rapid visual changes and blurred fMRI signals. Our method, DRAFT (Decoding and Reconstructing 3D room lAyouts from fMRI and Trajectories), extracts a compact layout representation from the early visual cortex and fuses this neural code with the geometric free space derived from camera motion. To evaluate it, we propose layout r, the Pearson correlation between predicted and ray-cast log depths. On ten held-out scenes (20 participants), DRAFT reaches a layout r of 0.480, outperforming the camera path alone (0.446) and seven fMRI decoders (at most 0.429). DRAFT also works as a plug-in that improves all seven decoders without retraining, raising their layout r from 0.171-0.429 to 0.487-0.493. Brain activity and camera motion can thus recover the metric layout of unseen rooms, a first step toward reconstructing the 3D spaces people see.

</details>


### [36] [GigaBench: Benchmarking Learnable Representations for Gigapixel Image Compression and Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31644)
*Matteo Fusconi,Alex Costanzino,Pierluigi Zama Ramirez,Luigi Di Stefano*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 GigaBench，首个面向十亿像素级图像的可学习表示（LRs）基准与实验评估，覆盖多领域图像及压缩、超分任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可学习表示（LRs）因紧凑性、分辨率无关性和可直接神经处理而受关注，同时十亿像素图像在科学、文化与工业领域日益普及，但缺乏在超大规模图像上系统评估 LRs 的基准。

Method: 构建 GigaBench，汇集跨领域采集与合成渲染的图像，覆盖图像压缩与超分辨率任务；将隐式、混合与显式三类代表性 LRs 适配到消费级硬件上可靠运行于十亿像素规模，并从重建质量、模型大小、压缩与推理时间等维度进行系统评测。

Result: 在统一设置下跨方法得到一致的趋势性结论，并据此提出可操作的后续研究建议。

Conclusion: GigaBench 为十亿像素图像上的可学习表示提供了首个标准化基准与实证参考，揭示方法间共性与差异，指导未来研究方向。

Abstract: Learnable Representations (LRs) can fit parametric families of functions to diverse kinds of signals. These emergent neural-native data formats are gathering interest in the scientific community due to their compactness, resolution-agnosticism and amenability to direct neural processing. On the other hand, gigapixel images are becoming increasingly widespread across a variety of scientific, cultural, and industrial domains. In this paper, we present GigaBench, the first benchmark and experimental evaluation focused on deploying LRs with gigapixel imagery. GigaBench includes images collected, acquired, and synthetically rendered across diverse domains, and addresses image compression and super-resolution. We adapt a representative set of LRs, spanning Implicit, Hybrid, and Explicit methods, so as to operate reliably at the gigapixel scale on consumer hardware. Through systematic evaluation across reconstruction quality, model size, compression and inference times, we identify consistent trends across methods and suggest actionable guidance for future investigation.

</details>


### [37] [Squaring-the-Sphere: Distortion-Free and Seamless Panorama Generation via CubeAtlas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31565)
*Xuhui Zuo,Chenjie Cao,Tengfei Wang,Junta Wu,Yisu Zhang,Chunchao Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CubeAtlas表示与Squaring-the-Sphere框架，将立方体贴图六面展开为单一紧凑画布，配合布局感知稀疏注意力，用预训练扩散Transformer单次联合生成360度全景图，在几何保真、跨视角一致性和高分辨率质量上优于ERP与立方体贴图方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 360度全景生成需同时兼顾局部透视保真与全球球面连续性。现有表示存在权衡：ERP可无缝建模但畸变严重且与预训练生成器存在域差；立方体贴图保留透视几何但面间不一致、接缝明显。

Method: 1) 新表示CubeAtlas：将六个立方体面展开到单张紧凑画布，把方向对齐、旋转后的极区与赤道面并排排布，既保留透视几何又编码跨面关系；2) 无需修改骨干或位置编码，预训练扩散Transformer单次pass联合建模全景；3) 布局感知稀疏注意力掩码，保留全景连续性所需交互、剔除几何无关token对；4) 免训练超分技术及融合方案，在无畸变透视空间增强细节后再合成最终ERP全景。

Result: 在多个基准上，相较先前方法提升了几何保真度、跨视角一致性与高分辨率视觉质量；在token数与ERP相当的情况下取得优于ERP建模的质量，且注意力计算量更低。

Conclusion: CubeAtlas以结构化展开布局弥合立方体贴图与预训练模型的域差，实现单次pass无缝全景生成，并通过稀疏注意力降低计算；结合透视空间超分融合获得高分辨率ERP全景。

Abstract: Generating 360-degree panoramas requires both local perspective fidelity and global spherical continuity. Existing representations face a trade-off: equirectangular projection (ERP) enables seamless panorama modeling but suffers from severe distortion and a domain gap to pre-trained image generators, whereas cubemaps preserve perspective geometry but often produce inconsistent faces and visible seams. We present Squaring-the-Sphere, a panoramic image generation framework built on a novel representation CubeAtlas, which unfolds all cubemap faces into a single compact canvas. By arranging orientation-aligned, rotated polar regions alongside the equatorial faces, this representation preserves the perspective geometry of cubemaps while organizing the cross-face relationships required for seamless panorama assembly. It further enables a pretrained diffusion transformer to model the complete panorama jointly in a single pass, without altering the backbone architecture or positional encoding. While CubeAtlas has a token count comparable to ERP, its structured layout makes many dense attention interactions unnecessary. We therefore introduce a layout-aware sparse attention mask that preserves the interactions required for panorama continuity while removing geometrically irrelevant token pairs. As a result, CubeAtlas achieves better quality than ERP modeling with lower attention computation. Furthermore, we introduce a training-free super-resolution technique and a fusion scheme that enhance fine details in distortion-free perspective space before achieving the final ERP panorama. Experiments on various benchmarks demonstrate that our method improves geometric fidelity, cross-view consistency, and high-resolution visual quality over previous methods.

</details>


### [38] [A Task-Centric Survey of Long-Document Visual Question Answering: Taxonomy, Evolution, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31385)
*Yufei Tong,Guanjie Cheng,Tianle Wu,Leilei Zhang,Hang Zheng,Peihan Wu,Jianwei Yin,Shuiguang Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 一篇以任务为中心的综述，将长文档视觉问答分解为文档解析、证据组织与检索、生成器组织与学习、评估四个组件，梳理各组件瓶颈如何塑造技术选择，并展望未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有综述多聚焦架构或技术路线，较少关注任务瓶颈如何影响技术选择与研究优先级；视觉文档理解对从富视觉文档中获取知识至关重要且研究兴趣日增，需要以任务为中心的视角进行系统梳理。

Method: 将长文档视觉问答拆解为四个组件：文档解析、证据组织与检索、生成器组织与学习、评估；对每个组件构建细粒度分类体系，追踪其动机、技术应对及跨组件关联；以显式检索为重点，同时覆盖直接阅读范式；并进行时间维度分析。

Result: 时间分析显示研究重心从多模态内容理解逐步扩展到充分且高保真的证据获取与持续的证据管理；同时刻画了各组件间动机—技术—连接的对应关系。

Conclusion: 提出未来方向：证据充分性、可验证与可纠正的理解、预算感知的协调，并展望持续文档理解。

Abstract: Visual document understanding is essential for acquiring knowledge from visually rich documents and is attracting growing research interest. Existing surveys emphasize architectures or technical approaches, with limited attention to how task bottlenecks shape technical choices and research priorities. We present a task-centric survey that decomposes long-document visual question answering (VQA) into four components: document parsing, evidence organization and retrieval, generator organization and learning, and evaluation. For each, we develop a fine-grained taxonomy and trace motivations, technical responses, and cross-component connections. We focus on explicit retrieval while covering direct reading. Our temporal analysis traces expanding priorities from multimodal content understanding to sufficient, high-fidelity evidence acquisition and sustained evidence management. We outline future directions in evidence sufficiency, verifiable and correctable understanding, and budget-aware coordination, with an outlook on continual document understanding.

</details>


### [39] [MVLoMa: Multi-View Local Feature Matching with Any Geometry Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31321)
*Xinyang Lu,Shengzhe Wei,Sheng Tang,Weiqi Zhang,Juntong Fang,Zesong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MVLoMa将稀疏局部特征匹配从图像对扩展到多视图星形图，通过冻结多视图几何模型注入跨视图几何上下文，单次前向完成中心图与所有邻居的匹配，在25视图星图上取得最优相对位姿AUC并比逐边匹配快2.20倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SfM与视觉定位通常将一张参考图与多个检索或共视邻居匹配，但现有局部特征匹配器仅面向图像对。独立地对每条中心-邻居边应用成对匹配器会重复参考侧计算，且无法利用其余观测提供的互补多视图几何证据。

Method: 提出稀疏星形图匹配器MVLoMa：先用冻结的多视图几何模型联合描述星形图内所有图像，将跨视图几何上下文注入局部描述子；再通过逐视图自注意力与双向中心-邻居交叉注意力，在单次前向中完成中心图对所有邻居的匹配。监督方面，正样本仅来自深度有效且互可见的稠密warp，并显式惩罚违反对极几何的高置信匹配。另测试DA3、π³、VGGT、VGGT-Ω四种冻结几何编码器。

Result: 在HyperSim、ScanNet++、Mip-NeRF 360、Tanks and Temples的25视图星形图上，三个阈值下均取得最优宏平均相对位姿AUC（58.65/85.41/90.99）；匹配器比24条中心-邻居边顺序评估快2.20倍；DA3-Giant在所有编码器消融指标上最佳。

Conclusion: 实验表明几何感知描述子、可见性过滤、几何负监督与联合多视图推理均有效，多视图星形图联合匹配在精度与效率上优于成对匹配的独立组合。

Abstract: Local feature matching is typically formulated for image pairs, although structure-from-motion and visual localization commonly match one reference image against many retrieved or covisible neighbours. Applying a pairwise matcher independently to these centre-neighbour edges duplicates reference-side computation and prevents the matcher from exploiting complementary multi-view geometric evidence from the remaining observations. To address these limitations, we introduce MVLoMa, a sparse star-graph matcher that extends LoMa from image pairs to multiple views. MVLoMa first uses a frozen multi-view geometry model to jointly describe all images in a star, then injects the resulting cross-view geometric context into local descriptors. It then matches the centre image to all neighbours in a single forward pass through per-view self-attention and bidirectional centre-neighbour cross-attention. For supervision, we derive positive correspondences only from depth-valid, mutually visible dense warps and explicitly penalize high-confidence matches that violate epipolar geometry. On 25-view star graphs from HyperSim, ScanNet++, Mip-NeRF 360, and Tanks and Temples, MVLoMa obtains the best macro-average relative-pose AUC among the compared methods at all three thresholds (58.65/85.41/90.99). Its matcher is 2.20 times faster than sequential evaluation of the 24 centre-neighbour edges. The same design supports four tested frozen geometry encoders -- DA3, $π^3$, VGGT, and VGGT-$Ω$ -- with DA3-Giant performing best on all reported encoder-ablation metrics. Controlled ablations further isolate the effects of geometry-aware descriptors, visibility filtering, geometric negative supervision, and joint multi-view inference.

</details>


### [40] [ReCoG: Relational Concept Graph for Training-Free Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31282)
*Changseon Yu,Hyeokjun Kweon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ReCoG，一种关系概念图用于免训练个性化检索增强，通过概念对间的有向判别边在推理时进行显式两两比较以消歧，并引入 FINGER 基准测试易混淆概念集，取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于检索器的免训练个性化方法将各概念孤立处理，检索到的描述性证据看似合理但缺乏判别性，尤其在个性化概念集包含高度混淆的细粒度类别时失效。作者认为个性化本质上是关系性的：正确识别依赖于概念与其最近邻替代项之间的具体差异。

Method: ReCoG 将概念表示为图节点，并附加有向边显式刻画概念对之间的判别线索（由冻结 VLM 在构建数据库时引出）。推理阶段先检索候选短名单，再在相应关系边条件下进行显式两两比较，选择对竞争候选持续获胜的概念。同时构建 FINGER 基准用于易混淆概念集的个性化识别评测。

Result: 在 FINGER 上显著优于先前方法，在混淆程度最高的设定下增益最大；在 captioning 与 VQA 等既有基准上亦取得一致最优性能，表明关系证据在细粒度设定之外同样有益。

Conclusion: 以概念对关系边与成对比较替代孤立检索，是可提升免训练个性化 VLM 判别能力的有效范式，且具有跨任务通用性。

Abstract: Training-free personalization adapts a frozen vision-language model (VLM) to recognize user-defined concepts from only a few exemplars by retrieving concept entries at inference time. While recent retriever-based methods enrich each concept with descriptive evidence, they largely treat concepts in isolation, so the retrieved information can be plausible yet non-discriminative, especially when the personalized concept set contains highly confusable, fine-grained categories. We argue that personalization is inherently relational: correct recognition depends on the specific distinctions between a concept and its nearest alternatives. To this end, we propose ReCoG, a Relational Concept Graph for retrieval-augmented personalization. ReCoG represents concepts as nodes and augments them with directed edges that explicitly capture discriminative cues between concept pairs, elicited by a frozen VLM during database construction. At inference, ReCoG retrieves a shortlist and resolves ambiguity via explicit pairwise comparisons conditioned on the corresponding relational edges, selecting the concept that consistently wins against competing candidates. We also introduce FINGER benchmark to test personalized recognition on confusable concept sets. Across FINGER, ReCoG substantially outperforms prior methods, with the largest gains in the most confusable regimes. ReCoG also achieves consistently best performance on established personalization benchmarks including captioning and VQA, confirming that relational evidence is broadly beneficial beyond fine-grained settings. Project Page: https://csy-98.github.io/ReCoG/

</details>


### [41] [Refining Open-Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation via Regional Semantics and Visual Prototypes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30623)
*Aijing Yu,Zhengbo Wang,Lijun Sheng,Jian Liang,Xiao-Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 调整CLIP视觉编码器的注意力层以获得更具区域语义的选择性注意力特征，并引入视觉原型缓解模态鸿沟，提升开放词汇分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP在图像级开放词汇分类上表现优异，但密集预测需像素级区域-文本对齐，原模型直接迁移会损失区域语义并受模态鸿沟负面影响。

Method: 1) 修改CLIP原生视觉编码器内的注意力层，采用新的注意力计算方式获得选择性注意力特征，保留更多区域语义信息；2) 引入额外视觉原型，精化仅基于文本嵌入的原预测器，具体将预测置信度高的patch特征构成辅助的基于记忆的分类器；3) 集成可优化特征提取器并采用指数滑动平均（EMA）更新策略提升预测质量。

Result: 在5个语义分割数据集上的大量实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过注意力层改造与视觉原型辅助分类器，可有效将CLIP适配到密集预测任务并缓解模态鸿沟。

Abstract: Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training (CLIP) has shown superior performance in the image-level open-vocabulary classification task, benefiting from its powerful alignment of visual features and text embeddings. However, dense prediction tasks require models to perform pixel-level predictions, necessitating modifications to CLIP for region-text alignment. In this paper, we adjust the attention layers within the vanilla visual encoder of CLIP. With a novel attention calculation method for selective attention features, the model retains more regional semantic information. To further mitigate the negative impact of the modality gap, we introduce additional visual prototypes to refine the original predictor solely based on text embeddings. Specifically, patch features with confident predictions are selected to form an additional auxiliary memory-based classifier for more accurate prediction. We further integrate an optimizable feature extractor with an exponential moving average updating strategy to enhance prediction quality. Extensive experiments on $5$ semantic segmentation datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of our method.

</details>


### [42] [KinoUAV: A Benchmark for Safe and Stable UAV Vision-Language Navigation with Kinodynamic Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30609)
*Chenyao Yang,Peijin Wang,Yingchao Feng,Wenhui Diao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出KinoUAV运动动力学基准（数据集+KinoUAV-Agent），显式引入运动动力学建模与安全距离，实现无人机安全稳定导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机导航方法未显式结合运动动力学建模与安全距离进行轨迹生成，导致偏航角速度绝对值偏高、碰撞频繁、导航成功率低，安全性与运动稳定性受限。

Method: 1）构建分层运动动力学轨迹生成流程，以加速度动作驱动无人机，基于全局规划的航点链分段生成轨迹，并施加速度、加速度、加加速度约束；2）在数据集中引入方向自由空间距离（DFD）标签，描述可安全运动的间隙；3）KinoUAV-Agent预测DFD并据此生成安全动作，实现感知与动作决策解耦；4）设计Cruise-Seek任务（先进入目标区域再搜索目标）。

Result: 在KinoUAV上，KinoUAV-Agent优于现有方法，取得最高成功率，同时保持较低的绝对偏航角速度与碰撞率。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明运动动力学建模对实现安全、稳定的无人机导航具有有效性。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) have recently emerged as important remote sensing platforms, and UAV navigation has shown great potential in remote sensing applications. However, existing methods do not explicitly incorporate kinodynamic modeling or safety distances into trajectory generation, resulting in high absolute yaw rates, frequent collisions, and low navigation success rates, which limit safety and motion stability. To address this challenge, we introduce KinoUAV, a kinodynamic benchmark comprising a dataset and KinoUAV-Agent. First, we develop a hierarchical kinodynamic trajectory generation pipeline that drives UAV through acceleration actions. It uses waypoint chains from global planning and generates trajectories segment by segment under velocity, acceleration, and jerk constraints, reducing oscillations and improving trajectory stability. Furthermore, we introduce directional free-space distance (DFD) labels into the constructed dataset to describe the available clearance for safe motion decisions. The proposed KinoUAV-Agent predicts DFDs and uses them to generate safe actions, decoupling perception from action decision-making. This design enables autonomous obstacle avoidance and improves navigation safety. The benchmark incorporates the proposed Cruise-Seek task, in which the UAV first enters the target region and then searches for the target, reducing the difficulty of target search and improving navigation success. Experiments on KinoUAV show that KinoUAV-Agent outperforms existing methods, achieving the highest success rate while maintaining lower absolute yaw rates and collision rates. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of kinodynamic modeling for safe and stable UAV navigation.

</details>


### [43] [HimmBO: High-Dimensional Interactive Model-Merging Bayesian Optimization for Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30875)
*Jinyuan Mao,Zhihao Shen,Chenxi Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HimmBO：两阶段偏好驱动适配器合并方法，先用预生成图像的level-set估计恢复活跃适配器集合（无需生成图像），再在低维子空间优化合并系数，两阶段均用风格感知核；在模拟用户实验中相似度最高，达到同等相似度的图像生成量不足此前工作的1/10，适配器增至1080个仍稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PBO方法仅能处理数十个预选适配器，且每轮交互都需昂贵的图像生成，难以扩展到10万+级适配器集合。

Method: 两阶段方法：筛选阶段通过对预生成图像做level-set估计恢复活跃适配器集合，不产生图像生成开销；精化阶段在恢复的低维子空间内优化合并系数；两阶段均采用风格感知核，使偏好信息可在视觉相似的适配器间迁移。

Result: 模拟用户图像匹配实验中，最终相似度高于所有对比方法，达到给定相似度所需图像生成量低于此前工作的1/10；集合扩大至1080个适配器时性能保持稳定，对比方法显著退化。

Conclusion: 通过筛选-精化分解与风格感知核，HimmBO将偏好驱动的适配器合并扩展到大规模集合并大幅降低图像生成成本，具备良好可扩展性。

Abstract: Fine-tuning has become a common way to customize text-to-image models, leading to community collections of over 100K lightweight adapters. Adapters sharing a base model can be merged with continuous coefficients. This turns a large collection into a high-dimensional continuous design space, which recent work explores interactively using preferential Bayesian optimization (PBO). However, existing approaches are limited to a few dozen preselected adapters and require expensive image generation in every interaction round. We introduce HimmBO, a two-stage method that scales preference-driven adapter merging to much larger collections while reducing image-generation cost. Its screening stage recovers the active adapter set through level-set estimation over pre-generated images, requiring no image generation. The refinement stage then optimizes merge coefficients in the recovered low-dimensional subspace. Both stages use a style-aware kernel, allowing preference information to transfer between visually similar adapters. In simulated-user image-matching experiments, HimmBO achieves higher final similarity than all comparisons while reaching a given similarity with under one tenth of the image generations required by prior work. Its performance remains stable as the collection grows to 1080 adapters, while competing methods degrade substantially.

</details>


### [44] [DRILL: Training World Models to Improve Policies, Not Predict Pixels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30814)
*Zhaolu Kang,Tailong Luo,Lei Wei,Guangyuan Dong,Sihan Liu,Yingjie He,Siheng Wang,Shuaibo Li,Rongchao Zhang,Zhen Tian,Richeng Xuan,Zhichao Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出用于VLA策略强化学习的世界模型以像素预测为目标存在目标错配，提出双层框架DRILL：直接以下游策略回报为目标更新世界模型，包含闭式代理版DRILL-VWI与全元梯度版DRILL-IMG，在两个VLA骨干与五个操作仿真器上显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练VLA策略时，世界模型被用来减少真实环境交互，但仍按"预测下一帧像素"训练。然而其真正作用是产生能改进策略的想象rollout学习信号，而非视觉保真的视频。作者发现该错配很显著：更低的像素MSE可能带来更差的策略梯度；而按策略效用训练的世界模型即使下一帧像素MSE高出17%，下游策略反而更好。

Method: 提出DRILL（Downstream-Return Imagination Learning Loop）双层框架：外层更新世界模型以最大化"在想象rollout上做一次内层GRPO更新后"的策略回报。两种实例化：(1) DRILL-VWI——闭式代理目标，无需额外仿真器rollout，按策略访问分布、GRPO组相对优势与模型置信度对预测误差重加权；(2) DRILL-IMG——完整元梯度方法，通过截断的Hessian-向量积对内层更新求导。理论上证明DRILL-VWI在score-compatibility与小残差假设下可恢复DRILL-IMG元梯度的局部单步分量。

Result: 在五个操作仿真器与两个VLA骨干上，DRILL-IMG相比WMPO平均成功率提升13.1个百分点，相比RLVR-World提升7.9个百分点；且与RLVR预训练互补而非竞争。

Conclusion: 用于VLA强化学习的视频世界模型可以按其产生的策略梯度来训练，而不只是按像素级预测来训练。

Abstract: World models are increasingly used to reduce environment interaction when post-training vision-language-action (VLA) policies with reinforcement learning. Yet they are typically trained to predict pixels one step ahead, even though their role here is not merely to render visually faithful videos but to produce learning signals from imagined rollouts that improve the policy. We show that this mismatch is substantial: lower pixel MSE can produce worse policy gradients, and a world model trained for policy utility can yield better downstream policies despite 17% higher pixel MSE on the next frame. We propose the Downstream-Return Imagination Learning Loop (DRILL), a bilevel framework that updates the world model to maximize the policy's return after an inner GRPO update on imagined rollouts. DRILL has two instantiations. DRILL-VWI is a closed-form surrogate, requiring no additional simulator rollouts, that reweights prediction errors by policy visitation, the group-relative GRPO advantage, and model confidence. DRILL-IMG is a full meta-gradient method that differentiates through the inner update using truncated Hessian-vector products. We show that DRILL-VWI recovers the local one-step component of the DRILL-IMG meta-gradient under score-compatibility and small-residual assumptions. Across five manipulation simulators and two VLA backbones, DRILL-IMG raises mean success rate by 13.1 points over WMPO and 7.9 points over RLVR-World, and complements RLVR pretraining rather than competing with it. More broadly, we believe video world models for VLA reinforcement learning can be productively trained by the policy gradients they produce, not only by pixel-level prediction.

</details>


### [45] [R4DGS: Referring Segmentation in 4D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30326)
*Bangpu Chen,Yaxuan Li,Shirui Peng,Xiangtian Si,Liuxin Chu,Xitong Cao,Hongbo Jin,Jiayu Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Referring 4DGS（R4DGS）任务与 R4D-Bench-QA 基准，并给出训练无关的统一框架 ReferGaussian，将 4DGS 低层动态高斯转化为持久、可语言寻址的实体，实现复杂自然语言指代下的动态场景时空定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 4D 高斯方法以渲染为导向，语义扩展多依赖场景特定语义优化，且在较简单的查询设定上评测，难以支持动态场景中复杂自然语言指代（时序变化、多目标、推理密集、零目标/干扰项）的接地需求。

Method: 构建带结构化查询标注的 R4D-Bench-QA 基准；提出 ReferGaussian 框架：将掩码支持的高斯实体组织为 EntityBank，利用多模态大模型（MLLM）将复杂查询分解为可跟踪的物体短语与结构化指代约束，在无需场景特定语义优化的情况下执行约束感知的接地推理。

Result: 在 R4D-Bench-QA 与 4D LangSplat 协议上，时序目标选择能力提升，并在无需场景特定语义训练的条件下取得具竞争力的时空接地性能。

Conclusion: 将 4DGS 从纯渲染表示拓展为可语言寻址的动态实体表示，结合 MLLM 查询分解与约束感知推理，可训练无关地完成复杂指代式 4D 接地；配套基准为该方向提供结构化评测基础。

Abstract: Dynamic representations based on 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS) have shown strong capabilities in reconstruction and rendering, yet current methods remain limited in supporting complex natural-language referring in dynamic scenes. Existing 4D Gaussian methods are primarily designed for rendering-oriented dynamic modeling, while current semantic extensions often rely on scene-specific semantic optimization or are evaluated on relatively simple query settings. In this paper, we study Referring 4D Gaussian Splatting (R4DGS), a task for grounding temporally varying, multi-target or reasoning-intensive, and zero-target or distractor expressions in dynamic 4D scenes. To support this task, we introduce R4D-Bench-QA, a benchmark with structured query annotations. We further present ReferGaussian, a unified framework built upon 4DGS that converts low-level dynamic Gaussians into persistent, language-addressable entities for training-free referring inference. ReferGaussian organizes mask-supported Gaussian entities in an EntityBank, uses Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to decompose complex queries into trackable object phrases and structured referring constraints, and performs constraint-aware grounding without scene-specific semantic optimization. Experiments on R4D-Bench-QA and the 4D LangSplat protocol demonstrate improved temporal target selection and competitive spatiotemporal grounding without scene-specific semantic training. Project resources are available at https://chenbangpu.github.io/R4DGS/.

</details>


### [46] [GraphCAD: Geometry-Aware Diffusion on Edge-Adjacency Graphs for CAD Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30974)
*Wenhao Zheng,Xianggen Liu,Li Yue,Jiancheng Shi,Jiancheng Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GraphCAD，以边邻接图(EAG)为中心的几何感知生成框架，用混合图扩散联合建模离散拓扑与连续几何，并构建EAGBench数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: B-rep中离散拓扑连通性与精确空间几何相互耦合，现有将CAD生成建模为序列问题的方法缺乏显式拓扑归纳偏置。

Method: 以Edge-Adjacency Graph为生成表示；采用混合图扩散同时建模EAG的离散与连续成分；引入几何感知去噪机制，将节点属性渲染为空间点集，经SE(3)等变骨干编码并融合回节点表示；构建大规模工程CAD数据集EAGBench。

Result: 可生成含50个以上面的复杂CAD模型；在几何保真度与分布对齐上相较序列基线达到SOTA。

Conclusion: 图结构表示与混合图扩散为CAD生成提供了有效的拓扑归纳偏置，几何感知去噪将生成过程锚定于物理几何，验证了图式建模范式的优越性。

Abstract: Generative modeling for Computer-Aided Design (CAD) remains fundamentally challenged by the need to capture the intricate interplay between discrete topological connectivity and precise spatial geometry inherent in Boundary Representations (B-reps). Existing generative approaches largely formulate CAD construction as a sequence modeling problem, but consequently lack an explicit topological inductive bias. In this work, we present GraphCAD, a geometry-aware generative framework that centers modeling on the Edge-Adjacency Graph (EAG), a novel graph-based CAD representation tailored for generative tasks. GraphCAD adopts a hybrid graph diffusion formulation to jointly model the discrete and continuous components of EAG and introduces a geometry-aware denoising mechanism that renders node attributes into spatial point sets. These point sets are encoded by an SE(3)-equivariant backbone and fused into the node representations, thereby grounding the generative process in physical geometry. To support this study, we construct EAGBench, a large-scale dataset of engineering CAD models. Extensive experiments show that GraphCAD can generate complex CAD models with more than 50 faces and achieves state-of-the-art performance in geometric fidelity and distributional alignment compared with sequence-based baselines.

</details>


### [47] [Latent Space Jam: Layout Guided Video Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30390)
*Asaf Joseph,Moshe Mandel,Ohad Fried*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种免训练的两阶段方法，利用预训练视频扩散模型实现基于粗布局的物体布局与运动控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为视频生成加入控制通常需要训练，会带来额外算力、数据或架构改动成本，作者希望无需训练即可实现可控视频生成。

Method: 两遍（pass）流程：第一遍通过提示引导的交叉注意力调控时空放置，生成粗略视觉引导；第二遍用该引导条件化同一模型，生成高质量且布局一致的视频。

Result: 支持多样轨迹与物体交互、时序一致性强；在重叠与轨迹精度以及视觉质量上，于静态、动态和多物体场景中一致优于先前工作。

Conclusion: 粗布局引导加预训练视频扩散模型的两阶段免训练框架即可实现协调运动与布局一致的视频控制，且无需额外训练。

Abstract: Adding control to video generation often requires training, which demands additional computational resources, data, or architectural changes. We propose a training-free approach that utilizes pretrained video diffusion models to control object layout and motion using coarse layouts. Our method operates in two passes: first, it steers spatiotemporal placement through prompt-guided cross-attention to produce a coarse visual guide; second, this guide conditions the same model to generate a high-quality, layoutconsistent video. The approach enables coordinated motion with strong temporal consistency, supporting diverse trajectories and object interactions without additional training. Extensive experiments show that our method consistently outperforms prior work in overlap and trajectory accuracy, as well as visual quality across static, dynamic, and multi-object scene settings.

</details>


### [48] [ICGF-CXRMate: Image-Conditioned Clinical Context Compression and Gated Fusion for Chest X-ray Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30928)
*Vu Minh Tran,Thuc Huynh,Son Thanh Luu,Doanh C. Bui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ICGF-CXRMate框架，用图像条件化查询将异构患者数据压缩为固定数量的临床上下文token，并通过门控交叉注意力融入视觉流，在提升胸片报告质量的同时缩短解码提示长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辅助患者数据可改善胸片报告生成，但将异构临床信息与视觉token直接拼接会导致解码提示长且长度多变，并向模型暴露冗余或与图像无关的上下文。

Method: 可学习查询先以当前胸片表示为条件，再对患者数据序列做注意力，压缩为固定数量的临床上下文token；通过门控交叉注意力选择性将其融入视觉流，同时将这些压缩token保留在解码器提示中作为直接临床通路。

Result: 在关联的MIMIC-CXR与MIMIC-IV-ED数据上，Stage 2相较CXRMate-ED将RadGraph-F1由24.00提升至28.86、CXR-BERT相似度由56.86提升至62.80、BLEU-4由5.14提升至7.96，平均提示长度由373.89降至288.37（降低22.9%）；经自批判序列训练后，Stage 3在8项指标中的6项优于CXRMate-ED。

Conclusion: 消融实验证实图像条件化压缩、门控融合与压缩临床token直接保留三者具有互补贡献，该框架可同时提升报告质量并控制提示长度。

Abstract: Auxiliary patient data can improve chest X-ray report generation, but directly concatenating heterogeneous clinical information with visual tokens produces long, variable-length decoder prompts and may expose the model to redundant or image-irrelevant context. We propose ICGF-CXRMate, an image-conditioned clinical context compression and gated fusion framework. Learnable queries are first conditioned on the current chest X-ray representations and then attend to the patient-data sequence, compressing it into a fixed number of clinical context tokens. Gated cross-attention selectively integrates these tokens into the visual stream, while the compressed tokens are retained in the decoder prompt as a direct clinical pathway. Experiments on linked MIMIC-CXR and MIMIC-IV-ED examinations show that ICGF-CXRMate improves report quality while reducing the average prompt length. In Stage~2, it improves RadGraph-F1 from 24.00 to 28.86, CXR-BERT similarity from 56.86 to 62.80, and BLEU-4 from 5.14 to 7.96 compared with CXRMate-ED, while reducing the average prompt length from 373.89 to 288.37 tokens, corresponding to a 22.9\% reduction. After self-critical sequence training, ICGF-CXRMate outperforms CXRMate-ED on six of eight report-quality metrics in Stage~3. Ablation studies confirm the complementary contributions of image-conditioned compression, gated fusion, and direct retention of the compressed clinical tokens.

</details>


### [49] [When Annotation Patterns Are Learned: Skin-Tone Dependence in Dermatology Concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30901)
*Y. Li,M. Bigliardi-Qi,S. Bedros,P. Bigliardi,N. Papanikolopoulos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文审计皮肤科概念数据集SkinCon的跨肤色标注可靠性，发现"肤色相关概念标注"偏差：深色皮肤上红斑标注率显著低（OR=0.17），色素沉着/减退标注率显著高（OR=6.2/4.5）；证据性概念模型会学习并复现该模式，且三种标签错误检测器在发现"皮肤科医生描述但SkinCon未标"的红斑时均不如疾病单任务基线，说明概念标注需按亚组审计后才能用于可信解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念瓶颈模型（CBM）依赖可解释的概念标注让临床医生检查与纠正诊断，但其可信度以概念标注可靠为前提；而广泛使用的皮肤科概念数据集SkinCon的标注可靠性尚未按肤色分组检验，存在潜在系统性偏差风险。

Method: 在同一疾病内、去除近重复样本后，按肤色比较各概念标注频率并计算优势比（OR）；用这些标注训练证据性概念模型（evidential concept model），检验其是否在未见图像上复现该模式；并比较三种标签错误检测器（基于自信学习、训练动态、模型自身证据不确定性）与仅疾病预测基线在识别"皮肤科医生描述为红斑但SkinCon标为缺失"病例上的表现。

Result: 深色皮肤上红斑标注显著更少（OR=0.17），色素沉着与色素减退标注显著更多（OR=6.2和4.5）；证据性概念模型习得并在未见图像上复现该肤色相关模式，因而可能顺应而非纠正潜在的红斑漏标；三种错误检测器在发现被遗漏红斑上均不如疾病单任务基线。

Conclusion: 概念标注存在按肤色系统变化的偏差，模型会继承该偏差，现有错误检测方法难以纠偏；因此基于概念的解释在投入使用前必须按各亚组分别审计概念标注质量。

Abstract: Concept bottleneck models (CBMs) let clinicians inspect and correct skin disease diagnoses through clinical concepts, but only if the concept annotations are reliable. We therefore audit the reliability of SkinCon, a widely used dermatology concept dataset, across skin tones. Within the same disease and with near duplicates removed, erythema is annotated far less often on dark than on light skin, with an odds ratio of 0.17. In contrast, hyperpigmentation and hypopigmentation are annotated more often, with odds ratios of 6.2 and 4.5, respectively. We refer to this pattern as tone-dependent concept annotation. An evidential concept model trained on these annotations learns the same pattern and reproduces it on unseen images. Such a model may agree with potential erythema omissions rather than contradict them. Consistent with this, three label error detectors underperform a disease-only baseline at finding erythema that dermatologist captions mention but SkinCon labels as absent. The three detectors are based on confident learning, training dynamics, and the model's own evidential uncertainty. Concept annotations should thus be audited within each subgroup before explanations built on them can be trusted.

</details>


### [50] [Verus-Insight: Towards Explainable Artifact Detection in AI-Generated Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30866)
*Zhuoran Zhang,Yang Shi,Qixun Wang,Tengfei Liu,Yuqi Tang,Xuanyu Zhu,Yizhuo Jia,Jingyun Hua,Shixuan Liu,Xianghua Ying,Haotian Wang,Yuanxing Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Wenjing Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文将AI生成视频检测形式化为“检查规划—证据验证—证据聚合”三阶段伪影检测流程，提出Verus-Insight模型与Verus-Bench细粒度基准，在细粒度伪影分析与视频级检测上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于伪影的检测器存在两类问题：要么无法识别细微伪影，要么虽给出正确来源判断却未真正定位其依据的伪影证据，缺乏对伪影证据的可信识别与利用。

Method: 将伪影检测结构化为三项互补能力：Inspection Planning（确定检查对象）、Evidence Verification（验证内容是否含真实生成伪影）、Evidence Aggregation（聚合已验证证据作出源判定）；据此构建Verus-Insight，采用人类锚定的全流程监督与渐进式能力训练，强化细粒度伪影检测与视频级检测；并提出含细粒度人类伪影标注的Verus-Bench基准。

Result: 在多个基准实验中，Verus-Insight在细粒度伪影分析与AI生成视频检测两方面均表现强劲，在还原人类可感知伪影上取得显著提升；分析表明三项能力互补但彼此独立，且模型能更可靠地将相关检查目标转化为有依据的伪影证据，同时不增加虚假伪影声明。

Conclusion: 以结构化伪影检测流程替代直接伪影分类，可同时提升检测性能与证据可信度；细粒度人类标注基准为评估伪影检测的证据忠实性提供了新途径。

Abstract: Recent advances in video generation have made synthetic videos increasingly realistic, placing greater demands on reliable AI-generated video detection. Existing artifact-based detectors typically identify generation artifacts as evidence for source prediction, yet they may either fail to recognize subtle artifacts or reach the correct source decision without faithfully identifying the evidence underlying it. In this work, we systematically investigate how to design and train effective artifact detectors and formulate artifact detection as a structured process comprising three complementary capabilities: Inspection Planning, which determines what should be examined; Evidence Verification, which verifies whether the inspected content exhibits genuine generation artifacts; and Evidence Aggregation, which integrates the verified evidence into the final source decision. Building on this formulation, we introduce Verus-Insight, trained with human-anchored full-process supervision and progressive capability training to strengthen both fine-grained artifact detection and video-level detection. We further introduce Verus-Bench, a benchmark with fine-grained human annotations of perceptible artifacts for evaluating artifact detection. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks demonstrate that Verus-Insight achieves strong performance in both fine-grained artifact analysis and AI-generated video detection, with substantial gains in recovering human-perceived artifacts. Further analyses reveal that the three capabilities are complementary yet distinct, and that Verus-Insight more reliably converts relevant inspection targets into supported artifact evidence without increasing spurious artifact claims.

</details>


### [51] [Cross-Spectral Feature Fusion for Multispectral Document Binarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30863)
*Yasas Savinda,Maheshi Buddhinee Dissanayake*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种多分支条件GAN，在编码器深度L=3处以空间与通道softmax门控进行跨光谱自适应融合，并用VIS-NIR波段对导出的边缘图作结构引导，在SMADI四波段（500/600/700/1000 nm）上实现优于固定融合基线的多光谱古籍二值化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 古籍二值化中褪色墨迹、渗透与背景退化在可见—近红外波段呈现波长相关差异，固定融合策略与通用注意力难以充分利用跨光谱互补信息，因此需探究自适应跨光谱融合与定向结构引导能否带来增益。

Method: 采用多分支cGAN处理四幅已配准SMADI波段；可见光、NIR与结构特征流在编码器深度L=3通过空间与通道softmax门控融合；由选定VIS-NIR波段对生成辅助边缘图提供结构引导；设计9种受控融合与结构配置，使用文档级五折交叉验证、DIBCO标准指标与Holm校正统计检验评估。

Result: F3+NIR边缘引导配置最优，平均F1=0.7916、p-FM=0.8044、IoU=0.6654；相对固定Mid AVG基线分别提升F1 +0.0269、p-FM +0.0360、IoU +0.0321，且经Holm校正后三项增益均统计显著；NRM与DRD均值亦下降，但校正后不显著。

Conclusion: 定向跨光谱结构引导可有效调控中间多光谱融合，在保留完整四波段观测的同时显著提升前景重叠类主指标，为多光谱古籍二值化提供可复现的融合—引导设计范式。

Abstract: Historical document binarization remains challenging when faded ink, bleed-through, and background degradation exhibit wavelength-dependent behavior across visible and near-infrared observations. This study investigates whether adaptive cross-spectral feature fusion and targeted structural guidance can improve multispectral document binarization beyond fixed fusion and generic attention mechanisms.
  Four registered SMADI bands at 500, 600, 700, and 1000 nm are processed using a multi-branch conditional generative adversarial network (cGAN). Visible, NIR, and structural feature streams are fused at encoder depth L = 3 using spatial- and channel-wise softmax gating, while auxiliary edge maps derived from selected VIS-NIR band pairs provide targeted structural guidance. Nine controlled fusion and structural configurations are evaluated using five-fold document-level cross-validation, DIBCO-standard metrics, and Holm-corrected statistical testing.
  The F3 + NIR edge-guided configuration provides the strongest overall performance among the evaluated models, achieving mean F1 = 0.7916, p-FM = 0.8044, and IoU = 0.6654. Relative to the fixed Mid AVG baseline, it improves F1 by 0.0269, p-FM by 0.0360, and IoU by 0.0321, with all three gains remaining statistically significant after Holm correction. Mean NRM and DRD are also reduced, although these reductions are not statistically significant after correction.
  Targeted cross-spectral structural guidance provides an effective mechanism for regulating intermediate multispectral fusion, improving primary foreground-overlap performance while retaining the complete four-band observation.

</details>


### [52] [FilmFlow: Agentic System for 3D Virtual Film Cinematography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30753)
*Yiming Zhang,Jiarui Guo,Haojia Wei,Yifei Liu,Yifan Yang,Hongjie Zhang,Xue Yang,Zhihang Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FilmFlow：将自然语言指令或参考视频转译为可执行相机轨迹的多智能体框架，并配套发布 CinemaBench 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂相机运动是视觉叙事的核心，但把电影化意图转化为可控、可执行的相机轨迹仍然困难，尤其面向动态 3D 环境。

Method: 由 Director 智能体通过组合并发原子操作（联合控制平移、旋转与构图）构建可执行相机计划；辅以三个专用智能体：Interpreter 提取场景接地（scene-grounded）的电影化意图，Scout 选取初始视点，Critic 评估渲染结果并给出视觉反馈以迭代优化计划。同时构建 CinemaBench：150 个 Blender 场景、每场景 5 条相机指令（共 750 个文本导向任务）及 60 段参考视频。

Result: 在多种基线中，FilmFlow 取得最强基准性能与最高用户偏好，体现出有效的空间推理、时序协调与视觉构图能力。

Conclusion: 该框架支持文本导向与视频参考导向两类相机生成任务，验证了智能体式多智能体协同在可执行 3D 电影摄影中的有效性。

Abstract: Complex camera motion is central to visual storytelling, yet translating cinematographic intent into controllable, executable camera trajectories remains challenging. We introduce FilmFlow, an agentic framework that converts natural-language instructions or reference videos into explicit camera trajectories for dynamic 3D environments. To generate these trajectories, a Director agent constructs executable camera plans by composing high-level motions from concurrent atomic operations that jointly control translation, rotation, and framing. The Director is supported by three specialized agents: an Interpreter that extracts scene-grounded cinematographic intent, a Scout that selects initial viewpoints, and a Critic that evaluates rendered results and provides visual feedback for iterative plan refinement. We also introduce CinemaBench, comprising 150 Blender scenes from self-created and licensed sources, with five camera instructions per scene for 750 text-directed tasks and 60 reference videos associated with a subset of scenes. CinemaBench supports evaluation of both text-directed camera generation and video reference-directed camera generation. Across a diverse set of evaluated baselines, FilmFlow achieves the strongest benchmark performance and the highest user preference, demonstrating effective spatial reasoning, temporal coordination, and visual composition for executable 3D cinematography.

</details>


### [53] [PhyEvolve: Multi-Agent State-Evolution Planning for Physics-Aware Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30717)
*Yang-Hao Zhou,Lixing Tan,Zhiqi Li,Guang Yang,Xiao Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhyEvolve：多智能体框架，通过结构化的状态演化规划增强提示词，无需重训练即提升文生视频的物理一致性与状态连续性，在四个基准上平均领先此前最优智能体基线7.9个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生视频模型视觉保真度提升明显，但仍难以理解用户提示中隐含的物理演化过程，导致生成视频缺乏连续的物理状态演化。

Method: 提出多智能体框架PhyEvolve，含三个智能体：Director（依据用户提示与可选首帧定位场景，将物理事件与观测变化解耦）、Physical Evolver（利用场景证据识别物理机制，构建贯穿视频的因果关联状态转移，无论是否可见）、Composer（用这些转移增强原始提示）。通过提示词替换直接作用于现有生成器，不重训练、不改模型。

Result: 在四个主要基准上取得SOTA，平均超越当前最优智能体基线7.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 显式的因果状态演化规划可通过自然语言提示增强，有效提升生成视频的物理一致性与状态连续性。

Abstract: Recent advances in text-to-video generation have substantially improved visual fidelity, yet existing models still struggle to understand the physical evolution implicit in user prompts, making it difficult to generate videos with continuous physical state evolution. Here, we propose PhyEvolve, a multi-agent framework that enhances natural-language prompts through structured state-evolution planning. PhyEvolve consists of three agents: a Director, a Physical Evolver, and a Composer. Given a user prompt and an optional first frame, the Director grounds the scene and disentangles physical events from observation changes. The Physical Evolver uses scene evidence to identify physical mechanisms then constructs causally linked state transitions that unfold throughout the video, whether visible or not. The Composer utilizes these transitions to enhance the original prompts. Without retraining or model modification, PhyEvolve works directly with existing generators through prompt replacement. Extensive experiments on four major benchmarks show that PhyEvolve achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming the cutting-edge prior agentic baseline by 7.9 percentage points on average. These results demonstrate that explicit causal state-evolution planning improves physical consistency and state continuity in generated videos through natural-language prompt enhancement.

</details>


### [54] [Beyond Watching and Searching: Benchmarking Agents for Video-centered Deep Research Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31158)
*Jiahao Meng,Qize Yang,Yuxuan Wang,Yunfei Chu,Wensi Dai,Jin Xu,Yunhai Tong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出"以视频为中心的深度研究报告生成"新任务，并发布技能式工作流、OmniDR-Bench基准与Omni Atomic Rubric Trees评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度研究智能体基准缺乏以源视频为核心的研究评估，而用户亟需定位视频关键时刻、核验论断、复现操作流程并据此决策。

Method: 提出技能式（skill-based）工作流；构建OmniDR-Bench（50个中英文任务、19个真实场景子类）；提出Omni Atomic Rubric Trees，每棵树含3个维度、23个共享方向及任务特定的原子二元判据；用Audited Rubric Construction（ARC）经独立证据收集、自动检查、行为校准与迭代人工评审构建评分树。

Result: 基准共6,414条判据；18种智能体配置中最佳系统得分63.3/100；现有智能体在内容覆盖广度、跨模态因果与逻辑分析、引用准确性上仍有不足；并分析了模型、harness、技能配置、视频类别与研究轨迹。

Conclusion: 该工作将视频源与多模态证据检索、可交互报告生成统一为可评估任务，为视频中心深度研究提供了基准与细粒度评估框架，并公开代码与数据。

Abstract: Deep research agents synthesize open-web evidence into long reports, but current benchmarks do not evaluate research centered on a source video. We introduce Video-Centered Deep Research Report Generation. Given a video and user request, an agent identifies research questions, retrieves external multimodal evidence, and produces an interactive report linked to relevant video moments. This task has direct practical value because it helps users locate relevant moments, verify claims, reproduce demonstrated procedures, and make informed decisions from video content. We present a skill-based workflow and OmniDR-Bench for this task. For evaluation, we propose Omni Atomic Rubric Trees (Omni-ARTs). Each tree contains three dimensions, 23 shared directions, and task-specific atomic binary criteria. The directions test audiovisual grounding, multimodal evidence use, and interactive presentation. Audited Rubric Construction (ARC) builds these trees through independent evidence collection, automated checks, behavioral calibration, and iterative human review. OmniDR-Bench contains 50 Chinese and English tasks across 19 realworld subcategories. It includes 6,414 criteria. Across 18 agent configurations, the best system scores 63.3 out of 100. Current agents still need broader content coverage, stronger causal and logical analysis across modalities, and more accurate citation support. We further analyze models, harnesses, skill configurations, video categories, and research trajectories. The code and data are released at https://github.com/marinero4972/OmniDR-Bench.

</details>


### [55] [Where Do Long Captions Fail? Position-Aware Diagnosis and Reinforcement Learning for Detailed Image Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31111)
*Yuanze Hu,Zhichao Yang,Junwei Jing,Xin Yang,Wenxuan Liu,Tinghai Zhang,Jia Fu,Hongzhi Zhang,Xiangyu Wu,Ruiming Tang,Tingting Gao,Han Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出位置感知的细粒度标题评估视角，发现长标题存在“中段无据具体化、尾段空泛化”两类失败模式，并据此设计PAGER位置感知奖励的GRPO训练目标以修复这些失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有详细图像标题研究多将问题视为"数量问题"（多提事实、少幻觉），但自回归VLM按序生成，后段可能在视觉 grounding 更弱或噪声更大时产生，因此需同时研究"提到了哪些事实"与"事实出现在哪里"。

Method: 构建位置感知的评分量表表示：将稠密人工参考分解为原子视觉主张，对生成标题逐条核验，并按四分位归纳支持、幻觉与泛化内容的组成。基于该诊断提出PAGER——GRPO训练目标，为支持性中段细粒度证据、含证据的尾部续写、主张覆盖率和内容感知长度控制设置独立奖励信号，形成"同一有序主张表示既定位失败又提供位置特定奖励"的闭环。

Result: 揭示两类性质不同的失败：中段倾向产生缺乏支持的具体化表述，尾段则滑向无证据的模糊语言而非简单累积幻觉；同参考下的人类对照不呈现该模式，额外人工审计验证了标签的视觉有效性。在详细标题基准上，PAGER提升位置级 grounding 与成对标题偏好，同时保持有竞争力的全局标题质量。

Conclusion: 长格式标题应超越"数量"视角，纳入事实出现位置的维度；位置感知的主张级评分既能诊断失败，也能直接转化为GRPO的位置特定奖励，实现诊断与修复的统一闭环。

Abstract: Detailed image captioning is usually framed as an amount problem: a model should mention more visual facts while avoiding hallucination. However, autoregressive VLMs generate captions as ordered sequences, and later generations may be produced under weaker or noisier visual grounding. We therefore argue that long-form captioning should be studied not only by what facts are mentioned, but also by where they appear. We introduce a position-aware rubric representation that decomposes dense human references into atomic visual claims, verifies generated captions against those claims, and induces a quartile-wise composition of supported, hallucinated, and generic content. This lens reveals two qualitatively different failures in long captions: models tend to produce unsupported specificity in the middle, while the final portion often escapes into vague, non-evidential language rather than simply accumulating more hallucination. A same-reference human control does not exhibit these patterns; additional human audits examine the visual validity of the induced labels. Motivated by this diagnosis, we propose PAGER (Position-Aware Grounded Evidence Reinforcement), a GRPO training objective with separate signals for supported fine-grained middle evidence, evidence-bearing tail continuation, claim coverage, and content-aware length control. The result is a closed loop: the same ordered claim representation identifies where long captions fail and supplies the position-specific reward needed to repair those failures. Experiments on detailed-caption benchmarks show that PAGER improves position-wise grounding and pairwise caption preference while preserving competitive global caption quality.

</details>


### [56] [Zero-Shot Domain Generalization via Domain Prompt Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30680)
*Shiming Chen,Shuhuang Chen,Salman Khan,Fahad Shahbaz Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DoP（Domain Prompt Learning），基于 CLIP 等大型视觉-语言模型，在无源域数据条件下实现零样本域泛化，通过文本与视觉双模态的提示学习适配未见目标域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有域泛化方法依赖源域数据学习域不变特征或提示，而大规模单/多源数据集的采集与标注往往不可行，限制了 DG 的实际可用性。

Method: 在测试时自适应框架下学习目标域知识：1) 文本域学习（TDL）在文本空间隐式学习多样化风格 token，用风格间多样性损失与风格内一致性损失模拟分布偏移；2) 视觉域学习（VDL）利用测试视觉数据以自监督方式进一步精炼 TDL 生成的与目标域相关的风格 token。

Result: 在 PACS、VLCS、OfficeHome、DomainNet 等主流 DG 基准及 ImageNet 上，DoP 持续优于 SOTA 域泛化方法。

Conclusion: DoP 表明在无源域数据设定下，借助视觉-语言模型的双模态提示学习可有效应对文本-视觉模态域偏移，实现零样本域泛化。

Abstract: Domain generalization (DG) aims to improve
  the generalization of the model to any unseen domains. Targeting this goal, existing DG methods mainly focus on learning domain-invariant features or prompts from the given source domains. However, these methods rely on source-domain data, and collecting and annotating large-scale single- or multi-source datasets is often infeasible, limiting the practical applicability of DG. To tackle this challenge, we propose {\underline{Do}}main \underline{P}rompt Learning (DoP), a method built on a large vision-language model (e.g., CLIP) for zero-shot domain generalization, which recognizes unseen classes in unseen target domains without access to source-domain data. DoP essentially learns the target domain knowledge via prompt both in text and visual modalities to overcome the domain shift in text-visual modalities under the test-time adaptation formulation. Specifically, DoP first takes text domain learning (TDL) to simulate various distribution shifts by learning diverse style tokens implicitly in the text space with inter-style diversity loss and intra-style consistency loss. Subsequently, DoP employs visual domain learning (VDL) to further refine the target-domain-relevant style token generated by TDL by making use of the test visual data with self-supervised learning. Extensive experiments on popular DG benchmark datasets (e.g., PACS, VLCS, OfficeHome, and DomainNet) and ImageNet show that our DoP consistently outperforms the state-of-the-art DG approaches. Codes are available at: https://github.com/shiming-chen/DoP.

</details>


### [57] [AgentDreamer: Agentic 3D Motion Reasoning for Text-Controllable Physics Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30679)
*Mijeong Kim,Suho Park,Junha Park,Bohyung Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 AgentDreamer 框架，借助现成智能体将文本指令转化为结构化语义运动先验（稠密 3D 轨迹），再通过优化可微 MPM 仿真器参数使物理仿真结果匹配该先验；并引入基于掩码文本恢复的信息论指标 Motion-IG 来评估文本-运动对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本驱动仿真的引导方式（如基于视频的引导）在语义对齐上不足，需要一种既能利用语言语义、又能保证物理可行性的显式 3D 运动规划方案，同时缺乏衡量文本-运动对齐的可靠度量。

Method: 1) 输入文本指令与场景几何；2) 由现成智能体将指令解析为结构化语义运动先验（指定运动对象、方向、时序）；3) 智能体以时间分块上的参数化函数表示该先验，生成稠密 3D 轨迹；4) 优化可微 MPM 仿真器参数，使 rollout 在场景动力学约束下最匹配该先验；5) 提出 Motion-IG 指标：基于掩码文本恢复，比较有/无仿真运动时恢复掩码运动相关内容的似然，度量运动贡献的额外信息。

Result: 实验表明，与基于视频的引导相比，AgentDreamer 提升了文本对齐度，生成的仿真更贴近所要求的物理行为。

Conclusion: 将语言语义引导与可微物理仿真通过显式 3D 轨迹先验相结合，可有效提升文本到物理仿真的对齐质量，Motion-IG 为文本-运动对齐提供了信息论视角的量化手段。

Abstract: We propose AgentDreamer, an agent-guided optimization framework that aligns text instructions with simulated motion through explicit 3D motion planning. Given a text instruction and scene geometry, an off-the-shelf agent translates the instruction into a structured semantic motion prior, specifying what should move, in which direction, and in what temporal order. The agent represents this prior using parametric functions over a series of temporal chunks, yielding dense 3D trajectories. We then optimize the parameters of a differentiable MPM simulator such that its rollout best matches the prior while remaining constrained by the scene dynamics. This allows the agent to provide semantic guidance from language, while the simulator grounds it in physically feasible motion. We further introduce Motion-IG, an information-theoretic metric for text-motion alignment based on masked text recovery. By comparing the likelihood of recovering masked motion-related content with and without the simulated motion, Motion-IG measures the information contributed by the motion beyond that available from the remaining text and scene context. Experiments show that AgentDreamer improves text alignment over video-based guidance, producing simulations that more closely follow the requested physical behavior.

</details>


### [58] [Class-Grounded Batik Captioning under Scarce Text Annotation: Label Retention and Source-Specific Error Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30661)
*Novanto Yudistira,Muhammad Dzaka Aufa Fadhillah,Irawati Nurmala Sari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究在缺乏专家文本标注的蜡染（batik）文化遗产图像上，采用"LoRA微调的DINOv2分类器 + LoRA微调并以4-bit NF4加载的Qwen2.5-VL-7B"构成的级联式图像描述流程，通过3×2评估（三种captioner条件×两种推理提示）考察模型适配与预测类别注入的效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文化遗产图像缺乏专家文本标注，而类别标签相对易得、可作为紧凑监督信号。作者希望验证：将分类器预测的类别标签注入多模态大模型，能否在不依赖大量文本标注的情况下保留身份信息，并厘清上游分类误差如何传导至下游描述。

Method: 构建分类器—描述器级联：LoRA适配的DINOv2做类别预测，Qwen2.5-VL-7B经LoRA微调、以4-bit NF4量化加载生成描述。设计3×2评估矩阵，交叉三种captioner条件（如无注入、真值注入、预测类注入）与两种推理提示。在150张描述评估图像上统计类别提及率；分类器另设测试，涵盖300张公开来源图像与33张本地图像，并报告支持类别的宏F1与平衡准确率。采用参考型描述指标与图文兼容性分数两种评价口径。

Result: 三种注入条件在150张图像上均达到0.9067的真值类别提及率。分类器整体池化准确率96.10%，公开来源300张为98.33%，本地33张仅75.76%；本地子集支持类宏F1为0.8493，平衡准确率0.8302。参考型指标与图文兼容性分数给出的模型排序不一致。定性样例显示既能保留正确预测的身份，也会将分类错误以流畅文本形式传播。

Conclusion: 类别条件化可有效保留标签信息，但会把上游分类误差转移进描述文本；在文本标注稀缺场景下，该级联方案可行但可靠性受分类器泛化（尤其本地域数据）制约。作者同时指出局限：分类器与描述器使用不同数据划分、训练—评估重叠未量化，且来源间准确率差异受标签语义与训练支持度影响，需谨慎解读。

Abstract: Class labels offer compact supervision for describing cultural-heritage images when expert text annotations are scarce. We investigate a batik-captioning cascade that integrates a LoRA-adapted DINOv2 classifier with Qwen2.5-VL-7B, adapted using LoRA and loaded in 4-bit NF4. A $3\times2$ evaluation combines three captioner conditions with two inference prompts to examine adaptation and predicted-class injection. Across 150 caption-evaluation images, all three injected conditions achieve a ground-truth class-label mention rate of 0.9067. The classifier's separate test yields 96.10% pooled accuracy, with 98.33% on 300 public-source images and 75.76% on 33 local images. Supported-class macro-F1 and balanced accuracy on the local subset are 0.8493 and 0.8302, respectively. The classifier and captioner use separate partitions, and their training--evaluation overlap remains unquantified. Differences in label semantics and training support also affect interpretation of the source-specific accuracy gap. Reference-based caption metrics and image--text compatibility scores produce different model rankings. Qualitative examples show both retention of correctly predicted identities and propagation of classification errors into fluent descriptions. These findings characterize how class conditioning preserves label information and transfers upstream errors into batik captions under scarce text annotation.

</details>


### [59] [FedBST: Bilateral Self-Training for Unsupervised Federated Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30634)
*Aijing Yu,Kuangpu Guo,Jian Liang,Xiao-Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FedBST，一个双侧自训练框架，同时利用高置信度样本与模糊（"腰部"）样本，通过不确定性感知置信惩罚（UACP）与视觉-文本原型集成，缓解严重非IID场景下无监督联邦CLIP分类的性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督联邦学习结合CLIP等视觉-语言模型可实现无标签分类，但在严重非IID偏移下性能显著下降。已有方法FedCoPL仅对高置信度预测进行伪标注，忽略了模糊样本的信息，造成数据利用不足与决策边界偏置。

Method: 提出FedBST双侧自训练框架：1）本地优化阶段引入不确定性感知置信惩罚（UACP），对模糊样本的过度自信预测施加惩罚以隐式提升其不确定性，避免错误伪标签；2）推理阶段采用视觉-文本集成策略，聚合不确定性感知的本地视觉原型并与文本原型集成，通过基于原型的校准实现轻量化的本地分布适配。

Result: 在标准联邦聚合协议下，FedBST相较现有方法取得一致性提升。

Conclusion: 充分利用高置信度与模糊样本的双侧自训练，配合原型级视觉-文本校准，可有效提升无监督联邦视觉-语言模型在非IID数据下的分类性能。

Abstract: Unsupervised federated learning with Vision-Language Models (e.g., CLIP) offers a promising solution for label-free classification, yet its performance often degrades under severe non-IID shifts. A pioneering method, FedCoPL, typically relies on self-training strategies that exclusively prioritize high-confidence predictions for pseudo-labeling. However, this selective approach neglects the information of ambiguous samples (often referred to as ``waist" samples), leading to the underutilization of data and biased decision boundaries. To address this limitation, we propose FedBST, a bilateral self-training framework that effectively exploits both high-confidence and ambiguous data. Specifically, for local optimization, we introduce uncertainty-aware confidence penalization (UACP). UACP targets ambiguous samples by penalizing overconfident predictions to implicitly promote higher uncertainty, thereby preventing erroneous pseudo-labeling. At inference, we employ a visual-text ensemble strategy. This approach aggregates uncertainty-aware local visual prototypes and ensembles them with textual prototypes, achieving lightweight adaptation to local distributions via prototype-based calibration. Experiments demonstrate that FedBST achieves consistent improvements over existing methods with standard federated aggregation protocols.

</details>


### [60] [Data-Driven Vision-Language Models for Remote Sensing: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31304)
*Yue Zhou,Shujun Zhao,Xue Yang,Ruigang Li,Tianwen Zhang,Mengcheng Lan,Chaofeng Chen,Lingfei Ma,Hongjie He,Jonathan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该综述聚焦视觉-语言模型（VLM）时代的遥感研究范式从"模型中心"转向"数据中心"，系统梳理遥感视觉-语言数据集的发展现状，并为数据构建、微调与评估提供全流程实践指南，呼吁社区共建任务覆盖广、地理分布广的遥感视觉-语言语料库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习时代遥感视觉模型的创新集中于网络结构设计与端到端训练；进入VLM时代后，受算力成本约束，主流做法转为用遥感垂域数据微调通用VLM而非改动核心架构，创新路径由模型中心转向数据中心。然而学界仍缺乏系统性、以数据为中心的综述，无法指导研究者针对不同应用场景构建高质量遥感视觉-语言数据集并据此开展有效微调与性能评估。

Method: 综述性研究：系统回顾面向VLM的遥感数据集发展现状，并给出覆盖"数据构建—微调—评估"全流程的实践指南。

Result: 梳理了遥感视觉-语言数据集的发展脉络与现状，形成面向大模型时代遥感研究者的可操作指南，涵盖数据集构建、模型微调与性能评估三个环节。

Conclusion: 旨在推动学界为遥感各细分领域构建更多样、更高质量的视觉-语言数据集，并最终汇聚为任务覆盖广、地理分布广的综合遥感视觉-语言语料库，为遥感通用人工智能奠定数据基础。

Abstract: During the deep learning era, innovations in remote sensing (RS) vision models primarily focused on optimizing network architectures for specific tasks and conducting end-to-end training. However, with the advent of the Vision-Language Models (VLMs) era, constrained by substantial computational resource demands, the mainstream research paradigm has shifted towards adapting general-purpose VLMs using RS vertical domain data for fine-tuning, rather than modifying their core architectures. This transition marks a change in the innovation pathway from model-centric to data-centric. Despite the increasing importance of data, the academic community still lacks a systematic, data-centric survey to guide researchers in constructing high-quality RS vision-language datasets for various application scenarios and in conducting effective model fine-tuning and performance evaluation based on these datasets. To address this gap, this paper presents a systematic review of the development status of RS datasets tailored for VLMs. It further provides a comprehensive practical guide for RS researchers in the large model era, covering the entire pipeline from data construction to fine-tuning and evaluation. This paper aims to encourage the academic community to build more diverse and higher-quality vision-language datasets for specialized RS sub-fields. Ultimately, the goal is to converge these efforts into a comprehensive RS vision-language corpus that offers extensive task coverage and a wide geographical distribution, thereby laying a solid data foundation for advancing towards general artificial intelligence in RS.

</details>


### [61] [Reinforcing intra-operative tumour localisation with weak supervision from pre-operative appearances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31253)
*Sidhart Sami,Ali Sharjeel,Sri Atluri,Miao Zhao,Caroline Roney,Eirini Marouli,Shaheer U. Saeed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出弱监督跨模态框架：用术前MR训练的肿瘤分类器为术中CT窗口提供弱信号，指导强化学习智能体定位肿瘤；合成迁移（CT→合成MR）在外部HCC队列上最优，且无需配对MR-CT训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 术中影像（如CT）上肿瘤可见性差，导致手术导航（HCC靶向）困难；带标注的术中数据集难以构建，而配准迁移术前勾画又受术中形变与缺乏配对术前-术中数据限制。

Method: 1) 在术前MR上训练肿瘤存在性分类器；2) 将其应用于术中CT的滑窗，尽管存在域间隙，含肿瘤窗口平均获得更高的肿瘤存在概率，形成弱跨域信号；3) 以该弱信号引导强化学习智能体在术中CT上定位肿瘤；4) 比较直接迁移、少样本自适应与合成迁移（用非配对MR与CT训练，先将CT翻译为合成MR再定位）三种策略。

Result: 在外部HCC队列上，合成迁移优于其他被测方法及仅用CT的基线。

Conclusion: 该框架可在无配对MR-CT训练图像条件下实现跨模态肿瘤定位。

Abstract: Limited tumour visibility on intra-operative images complicates surgical navigation, including liver hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) targeting on computed tomography (CT) imaging. This makes labelled datasets difficult to curate for deep learning, and transferring pre-operative delineations through registration remains challenging due to intra-operative deformation and lack of paired pre- and intra-operative data. We propose to transfer tumour appearances learned from pre-operative magnetic resonance (MR) images to guide localisation on intra-operative CT through weak supervision. A classifier is trained to classify tumour presence on pre-operative images. This is applied to windows on intra-operative images, and despite the domain gap, it assigns higher tumour-presence probabilities on average to tumour-containing windows. These weak cross-domain signals guide a reinforcement learning agent to localise tumours on intra-operative images. We compare direct transfer, few-shot adaptation and synthetic transfer (trained using unpaired MR and CT), which translates CT into synthetic MR before localisation. On an external HCC cohort, synthetic transfer outperformed other tested methods and CT-only baselines. Our framework enables cross-modality tumour localisation without paired MR-CT training images.

</details>


### [62] [Evaluation of Uncertainty-Aware Multi-Software Ensembles for Hippocampal Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31505)
*Gabriel Oliveira-Stahl,Anna Schroder,James Moggridge,Hamza A. Salhab,Caroline Micallef,Josephine Barnes,M. Jorge Cardoso,Carole H. Sudre,Matthew Grech-Sollars*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 评估五种异构海马分割方法的集成，发现InnerEye+FastSurfer+HippoSeg子集成兼顾精度与效率，且集成导出的不确定性可标记低置信病例并具有下游临床价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的自动海马分割有助于阿尔茨海默病、癫痫等神经疾病的诊断与监测，但现有众多自动方法临床采用受限；可靠的不确定性评估能提升信任度、促进临床转化。

Method: 在两个痴呆数据集和一个癫痫数据集上，对五种异构海马分割方法（InnerEye、ASHS、FastSurfer、HippoSeg、FreeSurfer）构建集成评估，并采用样本方差、互信息、预测熵三种方式进行集成导出的不确定性量化。

Result: 包含InnerEye、FastSurfer、HippoSeg的子集成兼具准确性与效率；基于样本方差、互信息和预测熵的不确定性量化可通过标记低置信病例减少不准确分割；不确定性显著相关于运动伪影严重程度，并对阿尔茨海默病分类有适度贡献。

Conclusion: 集成方法可在计算成本与性能间取得平衡，集成导出的不确定性可作为自动升级模糊病例供专家复核的机制，并具备下游临床实用性。

Abstract: Accurate hippocampal segmentation can be a useful tool for diagnosing and monitoring neurological conditions such as Alzheimer's disease and epilepsy. While numerous automated segmentation methods exist, their clinical adoption remains limited. Reliable uncertainty assessment can enhance trust and facilitate clinical translation. This study evaluates ensembles of five heterogeneous hippocampal segmentation methods - InnerEye, ASHS, FastSurfer, HippoSeg, and FreeSurfer - across two dementia datasets and one epilepsy dataset. The sub-ensemble containing InnerEye, FastSurfer, and HippoSeg emerged as both accurate and efficient, highlighting the feasibility of balancing computational cost and performance. Additionally, ensemble-derived uncertainty quantification with sample variance, mutual information, and predictive entropy is shown to reduce inaccurate segmentations by flagging low-confidence cases, potentially providing a mechanism for automatically escalating ambiguous cases for expert assessment. Lastly, this study explores the downstream clinical utility of ensemble-derived uncertainty, finding a significant correlation with motion artefact severity and a modest contribution to Alzheimer's disease classification.

</details>


### [63] [Ask About the Right Moment: Training-Free Object Identity Reasoning for Video-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31471)
*Di Yang,Xiaochang Li,Muyang Liu,Yufei Wang,Yanhai Xiong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出视频语言模型在多帧间无法保持物体身份，提出免训练的 Track, Then Bind：先用可提示视频跟踪器为每个候选物体分配轨迹编号，再由冻结VLM在证据可见帧上回答静态识别问题以绑定目标，最后从跟踪状态以文本推导答案。Qwen3-VL-32B下解出VET-Bench两项任务，并在Perception Test与TOC-Bench上大幅超越现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频中许多问题只围绕一个物体（杯球游戏中球最终在哪、哪张是Q），但Video-LM虽能在单帧识别物体，却无法在时间上保持其身份区分。实证显示：在真实shell-game视频上，8B至235B开放模型、thinking变体、强化学习工具代理及三种免训练工具代理均仅在随机水平十个百分点内。作者据此把问题拆为"语义身份绑定"与"时序身份维持"两个独立环节，并主张前者应在证据可见的瞬间确定，后者交由跟踪器承担。

Method: 免训练的 Track, Then Bind 流水线：(1) 可提示视频跟踪器跟踪所有候选物体并用轨迹编号绘制；(2) 冻结VLM回答关于识别性证据的静态问题，将目标绑定到某条轨迹；(3) 在文本层面由跟踪状态推导最终答案，读出不需模型重新解释原视频。全部基准共用同一配置。

Result: Qwen3-VL-32B下解出VET-Bench两项任务（微调轨迹模型为92%和90%，同一VLM对16样本投票仅30–32%）；在Perception Test最难的杯子游戏中达55.4%（最佳公开模型41.5%）；基于轨迹时间线的TOC-Bench达48.6%，同模型直接作答为37.1%。对照实验：把相同轨迹画在视频上再直接提问，在真实杯子游戏上比该代理低12–19个百分点。

Conclusion: 将身份绑定与时序维持解耦，并以"先跟踪、后绑定"的顺序实现，可在零训练条件下显著提升Video-LM对单物体时序问题的推理能力；显式轨迹状态比让模型在视频上"看轨迹再直接回答"更有效，说明关键在于读出阶段不重新解释原始视频。

Abstract: Many questions about a video concern one physical object. Where does the ball end up after the cups are shuffled? Which card is the queen? How often was the mug covered? Video-language models (Video-LMs) recognise such objects in single frames but do not keep them apart over time. On real shell-game videos, open models from 8B to 235B parameters, their thinking variants, a reinforcement-learned tool agent and three training-free tool agents all stay within ten points of chance. We separate semantic identity binding from temporal identity maintenance. An object's identity should be fixed at a moment when the evidence for it is visible, and carried afterwards by a tracker. \emph{Track, Then Bind} implements this principle without training. A promptable video tracker follows every candidate object and draws it with a track number. A frozen vision-language model answers a static question about the identifying evidence, which binds the target to a track. The answer is then derived from the tracked state in text, without asking the model to reinterpret the original video during read-out. A single configuration covers all benchmarks. With Qwen3-VL-32B it solves both VET-Bench tasks, where a fine-tuned trajectory model reaches 92\% and 90\% and the same VLM voting over 16 samples stays at 30--32\%. It answers 55.4\% of the hardest Perception Test cup games, where the best published model answers 41.5\%, and, using track-derived timelines, 48.6\% of TOC-Bench questions, against 37.1\% for the same model answering directly. Drawing the same tracks on the video and asking the question directly falls 12--19 points short of the agent on real cup games.

</details>


### [64] [BrickWright: Agentic Creation of Physically Feasible Brick Builds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31651)
*Ava Pun,Kevin Dai,Sally Chen,Maxwell Jones,Ruixuan Liu,Kangle Deng,Changliu Liu,Maneesh Agrawala,Deva Ramanan,Jun-Yan Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BrickWright 是一个让预训练编码智能体通过编写 BrickLayer 代码（含装配 API 与物理可行性验证器）从文本/图像生成物理可行 LEGO 拼搭的框架，在 512 条提示上实现 100% 物理可行率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿编码智能体已能进行 3D 建模，但构建面向真实世界装配的物体时仍易产生浮空、碰撞或部件不连通的无效装配。作者主张：只要为智能体提供装配与物理可行性验证工具，它们就能在遵循输入提示的同时生成物理可行的装配体。

Method: 聚焦 LEGO 积木领域，提出 BrickWright 框架，引导预训练智能体通过编写 BrickLayer 代码生成拼搭。BrickLayer 是 Python 工具包，包含两部分：(1) 符合人体工学的积木装配 API，相比直接使用原始 LDraw 可减半推理成本；(2) 验证器，对物理可行性（仅使用真实零件、无碰撞、无浮空、尽可能连通）提供反馈。物理可行性的定义为：仅使用真实零件、无碰撞或浮空部件，并在可能时形成连通结构。

Result: 基线智能体物理可行率仅 9%（GPT-6 Luna）至 69%（GPT-6 Astra）；经 BrickWright 引导的智能体在 512 条提示样本上达到 100% 可行率，同时提升轻量智能体的提示遵循度、维持强智能体的提示遵循度，并在文本到积木任务上超越专用模型。

Conclusion: 为智能体配备装配工具与物理可行性验证，足以让其产出既符合提示又物理有效的装配体；BrickWright 在 LEGO 拼搭任务上显著提升可行率并优于专用模型，代码已在项目主页公开。

Abstract: Frontier coding agents are becoming increasingly adept at 3D modeling. Yet, they still face challenges when building objects meant to be assembled in the physical world, often creating invalid assemblies with floating, colliding, or disconnected parts. We argue that providing agents with tools for assembling builds and verifying their physical feasibility is sufficient for them to produce physically valid assemblies that still adhere to input prompts. Focusing on the domain of LEGO brick builds, we present BrickWright, a framework for generating physically feasible brick builds from text or image prompts using pre-trained agents, where a build is physically feasible if it uses only real parts, has no colliding or floating parts, and forms a connected structure if possible. BrickWright guides agents to create builds by writing code with BrickLayer, a Python toolkit with two main components: an ergonomic brick assembly API that halves inference cost compared to working in raw LDraw, and a validator that provides feedback on physical feasibility. Whereas baseline agents achieve physical validity rates ranging from only 9% (GPT-6 Luna) to 69% (GPT-6 Astra), BrickWright-guided agents achieve 100% validity on a sample of 512 prompts, while improving prompt adherence for lightweight agents and maintaining it for stronger agents. Additionally, they outperform specialized models on the text-to-brick task. Code is available on our project page: https://avalovelace1.github.io/brickwright/

</details>


### [65] [DeXplicit: Making Physical State Explicit in Dexterous Manipulation World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30781)
*Adam Lee,Mutian Xu,Yukang Cao,Shulin Tian,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DeXplicit 是一个灵巧操作世界模型，先在度量 3D 空间中显式预测世界演化（物体中心运动与形变），再据此渲染视觉结果；在 HOT3D、OakInk2、PhysTwin 上显著降低运动点误差并提升物体保真度，且两步因果生成带来 57 倍视频合成加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在操作物体前需预判手部运动如何改变物理世界及结果场景外观。现有方法要么在视频生成模型中纠缠动作条件动力学、相机运动与视觉合成，要么用点轨迹表示动力学而缺乏显式物体结构，导致动力学学习隐式且低效、几何不可靠。

Method: 提出"显式 3D 优先"的解耦框架：给定初始场景与未来手部轨迹，用轻量 3D 动力学网络预测以物体为中心的运动与形变，得到结构化、几何可依托的未来世界状态；再以该 3D 预测作为脚手架，将外观沿预测运动传输并投影到未来视角，渲染器仅需补全不确定区域与新可见内容。该分解支持高效因果生成，每块仅需两步去噪，并以已生成历史为条件。

Result: 在 HOT3D、OakInk2、PhysTwin 上，相较 PointWorld，运动点误差降低 27–40%；在共享渲染器下，HOT3D 上物体保真度 IoU+ 提升 47%；两步因果生成相比 14B 双向配置在视频合成上实现 57 倍加速。

Conclusion: 将世界动力学显式建模于度量 3D 空间、再交由渲染补全视觉细节，是一种比端到端视频生成更结构化、几何更可靠且更高效的灵巧操作世界模型范式。

Abstract: Before acting on an object, a robot needs to anticipate how its hand motion will change the physical world and what the resulting scene will look like. Existing approaches typically entangle action-conditioned dynamics, camera motion, and visual synthesis within a video generation model, or represent dynamics as point trajectories without explicit object structure. We introduce DeXplicit, a dexterous manipulation world model that instead predicts how the world evolves explicitly in metric 3D before rendering its visual consequences. Given an initial scene and a future hand trajectory, a lightweight 3D dynamics network predicts object-centric motion and deformation, providing structured and geometrically grounded future world states. These predictions then serve as a strong scaffold for video generation: appearance is transported through the predicted 3D motion and projected into future views, allowing the renderer to focus on completing uncertain and newly visible content rather than implicitly learning world dynamics from pixels. This explicit-3D-first decomposition also enables efficient causal generation, requiring only two denoising steps per block while conditioning on generated history.
  Across HOT3D, OakInk2, and PhysTwin, DeXplicit reduces moved-point error by 27-40% relative to PointWorld and improves object fidelity IoU+ by 47% on HOT3D under a shared renderer. Our design with two-step causal generation achieves a 57x speedup from our 14B bi-directional configuration in video synthesis.

</details>


### [66] [Where Did the Light Come From? Diagnosing Cast-Shadow Reasoning in VLMs and Borrowing the Missing Shadow Prior from Image Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30740)
*Ryugo Morita,Shunsuke Kitada,Stanislav Frolov,Brian Bernhard Moser,Ko Watanabe,Riku Takahashi,Jinjia Zhou,Andreas Dengel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ShadowBench基准，从真实照片构建正确/移除/错误方向三种阴影状态，系统评测VLM对阴影方向的读取、缺失推断与物理一致性判断，发现VLM仅能读取可见阴影，推断与校验能力薄弱且CoT无效，借助图像生成模型（IGM）路由可显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阴影是光照、物体几何与场景结构交互的可观测结果，理解阴影需要读取方向、推断缺失方向、判断物理一致性。现有VLM是否具备这种光照理解尚缺乏系统评测，作者希望量化该能力缺口并探究改进路径（推理 vs. 知识）。

Method: 构建ShadowBench：对每个真实场景生成三种状态——正确阴影、去除阴影、人工标注的错误方向阴影，以此向VLM提出三类问题。评测19个开源VLM（2B–78B）及前沿闭源模型，并采用无偏二选一测试消除单图自然性问题的yes-bias；同时测试CoT效果，并提出将VLM经由IGM路由的混合方案。

Result: VLM读取可见阴影方向显著高于随机且随规模提升；但推断缺失阴影方向能力很弱；物理一致性判断跌至随机水平，仅Gemini-3.1-pro略高于随机且远低于人类；CoT无帮助。经IGM路由后，VLM的推断能力显著提升，并能标记其原本接受的错误方向阴影。

Conclusion: VLM与IGM在光照理解上存在明显差距，缺失的关键不是推理能力而是IGM已具备的知识；图像生成/重建目标可能是通往光照感知VLM的有效路径。投影响应方向只是光照理解的一个侧面。

Abstract: Shadows are observable consequences of latent physical causes, arising from the interaction between illumination, object geometry, and scene structure. A model that understands shadows should be able not only to read a shadow's direction, but also to infer where a shadow should fall when it is absent, and to judge whether a shadow is physically consistent with the scene. We introduce ShadowBench, built from real photographs, which poses these three questions to vision-language models (VLMs). From each scene, we build a correct-shadow, a shadow-removed, and a human-annotated wrong-direction state. Evaluating 19 open VLMs (2B to 78B) and frontier proprietary models, we find that VLMs read a visible shadow's direction well above chance and improve with scale, yet infer the absent direction only weakly and fall to chance at judging physical consistency even under a bias-free two-alternative test that removes the yes-bias of single-image naturalness questions. Only Gemini-3.1-pro exceeds chance on validity, and even then only modestly and far below humans. Chain-of-Thought (CoT) does not help. The missing ingredient is not reasoning but knowledge that image generation models (IGMs) already hold. Routing a VLM through an IGM lifts its inference well above its direct attempt and helps it flag the wrong-direction shadows it otherwise accepts. Cast-shadow direction is a single facet of illumination understanding, but the sharp gap it reveals between VLMs and IGMs suggests image-generation or reconstruction objectives as a route toward illumination-aware VLMs.

</details>


### [67] [SmolVLA-MoE: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts for Efficient Multi-Task Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30732)
*Shiyang Zhang,Miao Yang,Zhengda Li,Yang Shao,Ruifang Zhai,Hui Peng,Peng Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SmolVLA-MoE，将稀疏混合专家（MoE）引入轻量VLA模型的动作专家FFN层，在约5亿激活参数、同等推理效率下于LIBERO四套任务上达到84.25%平均成功率，优于SmolVLA与OpenVLA-7B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轻量VLA模型仅使用单个稠密动作专家处理所有任务，在受限算力预算下多任务能力受限，需要在不显著增加推理成本的前提下提升多任务表现。

Method: 将动作专家中的稠密FFN替换为多个并行专家FFN，并引入token级路由器实现稀疏激活，使每个token自适应选择最合适的专家；同时加入带可调权重的共享专家，以平衡预训练能力的继承与任务特定专家的专业化。

Result: 推理时仅激活单个专家FFN，含冻结VLM主干的总激活参数约500M，总存储参数约800M；在LIBERO四个任务套件上平均成功率84.25%，超过SmolVLA（82.75%）与OpenVLA-7B（76.5%），且推理效率相当。

Conclusion: 稀疏MoE可用于轻量VLA动作专家，在几乎不增加推理开销的情况下提升多任务成功率，说明参数扩展与稀疏激活是提升小规模VLA多任务能力的有效途径。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown promising results in multi-task robotic manipulation by leveraging visual and language understanding for action generation. However, existing lightweight VLA models employ a single dense action expert to handle all tasks, which limits their multi-task capability under constrained computational budgets. To address this issue, we propose SmolVLA-MoE, a novel framework that introduces sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) into the FFN layers of the action expert in VLA models. SmolVLA-MoE replaces dense FFN layers with multiple parallel expert FFNs and a token-level router, enabling each token to adaptively select the most suitable expert for action generation. We further introduce a shared expert with a tunable weight parameter to balance the inheritance of pre-trained capabilities and task-specific expert specialization. Through sparse activation, SmolVLA-MoE activates only a single expert FFN during inference, with a total activated parameter count of approximately 500M including the frozen VLM backbone, while benefiting from approximately 800M total stored parameters. We evaluate our method on four task suites of the LIBERO benchmark. SmolVLA-MoE achieves an average success rate of 84.25%, outperforming SmolVLA (82.75%) and OpenVLA-7B (76.5%), while maintaining comparable inference efficiency. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/dudu435/smolvla-moe.

</details>


### [68] [WildFakeToolBench: Benchmarking AI-Generated Image Detectors with Web-Sourced Image Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31152)
*Mingjie Li,Yukun Jiang,Yicong Tan,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 WildFakeToolBench 基准，从网络公开信息（含 LLM 辅助代码翻译）中构建 156 个可执行图像处理工具，系统性评估其对 6 种 AI 生成图像检测器的规避风险，结果显示单一工具攻击成功率达 25.25%–100%，八工具池子集可实现每个检测器超 80% 的最坏覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 生成图像日益普及，相关讨论广泛存在于公共论坛、软件仓库与摄影教程中；借助 LLM 辅助的代码翻译，缺乏专业知识和本地 GPU 条件的攻击者也能将此类公开信息转化为规避检测器的工具。现有评估缺乏针对这种"真实世界可得信息"风险的基准。

Method: 构建 WildFakeToolBench：从 142 个领域的 220 个公开来源中提炼出 156 个可执行图像处理工具，每个工具保留来源证据，并通过语法、执行与输出失真检查，且不使用被评估检测器的反馈。在来自 5 个生成器的 105 张生成图像上，用这些工具处理图像后测试 6 个 SOTA 检测器。

Result: 最强单一工具在各检测器上取得 25.25%–100% 的攻击成功率，均超过 6 个图像处理基线中最强者；从 8 工具池中取子集评估，所有检测器最坏情况覆盖率均超 80%。有效性呈稀疏性与检测器特异性：在候选工具上高覆盖并不意味可靠的一次性规避。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了网络来源信息所带来的现实规避风险，并表明可基于这些"in-the-wild"信息建立 AI 图像检测器鲁棒性评估框架。

Abstract: As AI generated images are getting popular these days, discussions on such generations are widely existed in current public forums, software repositories, and photography tutorials. Moreover, with the help of LLM-assisted code translation, malicious actors can adopt such information to evade detectors without specialized knowledge or local GPU execution of the resulting tools. To evaluate such risks, we introduce \textsc{WildFakeToolBench}, a benchmark suite of $156$ executable image-processing tools derived from $220$ public sources across $142$ domains.
