Each tool retains its source evidence and passes syntax, execution, and output-distortion checks without using feedback from the evaluated detectors. On $105$ generated images from five generators, we evaluate six state-of-the-art detectors with images processed by the tools in our collected bench to assess their effectiveness against such in-the-wild risks. The strongest individual tools achieve attack success rates of $25.25\%$--$100\%$ across detectors, exceeding the strongest of six selected image-processing baselines for each detector. Evaluating subsets of an eight-tool pool yields worst-case coverage above $80\%$ for every detector. Effectiveness is sparse and detector-specific: high coverage across candidates does not imply reliable one-shot evasion. These findings not only highlight the potential of such web-sourced risks, but also show the potential on establishing AI-image detector robustness evaluation framework based on those in-the-wild information.

</details>


### [69] [GM-GReFEL: Geometry-Guided Spatiotemporal State-Space Learning for Robust Dynamic Facial Expression Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31140)
*Yudhistira Arditya Pratama,Yi-Zeng Hsieh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GM-GReFEL：以线性复杂度的Modulated GroupMamba时空主干替换ViT，并加几何感知可靠性决策模块，用于无约束视频面部表情识别，在DFEW/MAFW/FERV39k上具竞争力且延迟接近ViT-B/16。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无约束视频面部表情识别存在两个耦合问题：自注意力代价随片段长度二次增长；众包标签噪声大。需要在保持效率的同时提升对噪声标签的鲁棒性。

Method: 主干为Modulated GroupMamba：六个通道组分别扫描（4组沿帧平面、2组沿时间），成本随序列长度线性增长O(N)；每层后由Channel Affinity Modulation门控重新混合各组。之上设GReFEL可靠性模块，以预测归一化熵作为混合权重，在分类器输出与可训练几何锚点投票间加权，网络不确定时更依赖面部几何。采用静态到动态权重迁移初始化，并用Triple Loss训练。

Result: 五折交叉验证：DFEW最佳折66.57%/76.46% UAR/WAR（落后最强对比方法≤0.3点），五折均值64.07%/74.54%；MAFW最佳折45.66%/62.27%（优于全部对比方法），五折均值38.90%/54.37%（低于最佳方法）；FERV39k五折均值42.30%/52.66%，具竞争力。DFEW配对t检验显示时序建模带来+1.61 UAR点收益（单侧p=0.031）。注意力集中在合理面部区域，单片段延迟47.4ms vs ViT-B/16的46.8ms。

Conclusion: 线性复杂度的GroupMamba时空主干配合几何感知可靠性融合，可在与ViT-B/16相当的开销下取得有竞争力的DFER性能，但其收益有限且不稳定：DFEW均值仅与近期大预训练模型持平、MAFW均值落后，时序建模优势为"温和"级别，说明方法在小幅提升效率与鲁棒性的同时尚未显著超越强基线。

Abstract: Recognizing facial expressions in unconstrained video suffers from two coupled problems: self-attention grows quadratically in cost with clip length, and crowdsourced labels are noisy. We present GM-GReFEL, a spatiotemporal state-space network with a geometry-aware decision stage. Its Modulated GroupMamba backbone replaces a spatiotemporal Vision Transformer. Six channel groups are scanned separately, four over the frame plane and two over time, so the cost grows linearly with sequence length ($\mathcal{O}(N)$); after each layer, a Channel Affinity Modulation gate mixes the groups again. On top of the backbone, a GReFEL reliability module turns the normalized entropy of each prediction into a mixing weight between the classifier output and a vote from trainable geometric anchors, so that facial geometry counts for more when the network is unsure. The model is initialized by static-to-dynamic weight transfer and trained with a Triple Loss. In five-fold cross-validation, the best fold scores 66.57\%/76.46\% UAR/WAR on DFEW, 0.3 points or less below the strongest compared method, and 45.66\%/62.27\% on MAFW, ahead of all compared methods; on FERV39k the mean over five splits, 42.30\%/52.66\%, is competitive. The five-fold means are 64.07\%/74.54\% on DFEW, on par with recent large pre-trained models, and 38.90\%/54.37\% on MAFW, below the best of them. A paired $t$-test on DFEW indicates a modest benefit of temporal modeling (+1.61 UAR points, one-sided $p=0.031$). Attention stays on plausible facial regions, and latency is close to a ViT-B/16 baseline (47.4 vs.\ 46.8 ms per clip). Code: https://github.com/ardiytama/GroupMamba-DFER.

</details>


### [70] [WildDepth-v2: A Synchronized RGB--LiDAR--Radar Dataset and Benchmark for Metric Depth, Motion, and Tracking of Wildlife](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31123)
*Muhammad Aamir,Sangyun Shin,Naoya Muramatsu,Matthew Wijers,Amir Patel,Andrew Loveridge,Andrew Markham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 WildDepth 2 数据集，在同步 RGB-LiDAR 的 WildDepth 基础上引入成像雷达（37 组序列、19,716 帧、含逐点多普勒速度与误差预算），构建冻结校验的隐藏测试集用于单目测距与深度补全基准，并提供基于 LiDAR/雷达/单目深度的度量 3D 轨迹。结果显示，物体级雷达标定可在推理时无需 LiDAR 的情况下显著改进单目深度（AbsRel 0.120 vs 0.198），雷达一次标定可将 3D 位置误差降低四倍（2.20 m→0.58 m），并使距离采样检测函数偏差由 -13% 降至 -2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物距离与运动的度量估计支撑丰度估计、形态测量与运动生态学，但单目视频缺乏度量尺度，野生动物数据集也缺乏度量真值，导致现有方法难以获得可靠的绝对距离与 3D 运动信息。

Method: 在同步 RGB-LiDAR 的 WildDepth 数据集上做三方面扩展：(i) 加入成像雷达，采集 37 组 RGB-LiDAR-4D 雷达序列共 19,716 帧，含逐点多普勒速度与实测误差预算；(ii) 构建冻结且带校验和的隐藏测试集，分别针对动物像素与背景像素评估 RGB-only 距离估计和 RGB+稀疏 LiDAR 深度补全；(iii) 利用 LiDAR、雷达或单目深度提升 2D 轨迹，得到度量 3D 轨迹，并将雷达多普勒迁移到 LiDAR 上生成运动掩码。

Result: 在 48,723 帧动物数据上，物体级雷达标定的单目深度优于场景级 LiDAR 标定（AbsRel 0.120 vs. 0.198），且推理时无需 LiDAR；动物级 LiDAR 仍最精确（0.052）。在 431 条轨迹上，每轨迹一次雷达尺度标定将 3D 位置误差降低约四倍（2.20→0.58 m），距离采样检测函数偏差由 -13% 降至 -2%，与每轨迹一次 LiDAR 标定相当。将雷达多普勒迁移至 LiDAR 所得运动掩码密度为纯雷达的 3.7 倍、精度 72%，使运动动物 LiDAR 累积误差降低 37%，径向速度估计相关系数 r = 0.60，但存在约 50% 的低估。

Conclusion: 低成本成像雷达可作为 LiDAR 的有效替代或补充：在推理阶段无需 LiDAR 即可获得接近 LiDAR 级的度量深度与轨迹精度，并显著降低距离采样偏差；雷达多普勒还能增强运动分割与 LiDAR 配准。局限在于径向速度估计存在约 50% 低估，且动物级 LiDAR 标定仍为精度上界。

Abstract: Measuring animal distance and motion in metric units underpins abundance estimation, morphometrics, and movement ecology, yet monocular video lacks metric scale and wildlife datasets lack metric ground truth. We present WildDepth 2, extending the synchronized RGB-LiDAR WildDepth dataset in three directions. (i) Imaging radar: 37 RGB-LiDAR-4D-radar sequences (19,716 frames) with per-point Doppler velocity and a measured error budget, to our knowledge the first imaging-radar wildlife recordings. (ii) Benchmarking: a frozen, checksummed hidden test set for RGB-only distance estimation and RGB+sparse-LiDAR depth completion, evaluated separately on animal and background pixels. (iii) Motion and tracking: metric 3D trajectories obtained by lifting 2D tracks using LiDAR, radar, or monocular depth. Across 48,723 animal frames, object-level radar calibration improves monocular depth over scene-level LiDAR calibration without requiring LiDAR at inference (AbsRel 0.120 vs. 0.198), although animal-level LiDAR remains more accurate (0.052). Across 431 tracks, once-per-track radar scaling reduces 3D position error four-fold (2.20 to 0.58 m) and distance-sampling detection-function bias from -13% to -2%, matching once-per-track LiDAR calibration. Transferring radar Doppler onto LiDAR produces motion masks 3.7 times denser than radar alone at 72% precision, reduces moving-animal LiDAR accumulation error by 37%, and estimates radial speed (r = 0.60), albeit with approximately 50% underestimation.

</details>


### [71] [ResGVC: Residual Coding for High-fidelity Generative Video Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31039)
*Ding Ding,Daowen Li,Ying Chen,Kai Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ResGVC：通过在去噪各步传输残差段为生成式视频压缩引入显式信源约束，并用扩散Transformer作熵模型，在像素域去噪，LPIPS/PSNR BD-rate分别降低63.7%/83.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式视频压缩（GVC）依赖解码端扩散先验去噪，缺乏原始信号约束，且多在潜空间去噪，受潜表示瓶颈影响造成不可逆信息损失。

Method: 先用学习型视频编解码器编码原图；将编解码重建与原图之间的失真分解为一系列残差段，每个去噪步取一个残差段；残差段由新型残差编码器压缩，其熵模型为扩散Transformer；去噪直接在像素空间进行，并在整个去噪过程中引入显式信源约束。

Result: 与之前最优方法相比，LPIPS与PSNR的BD-rate分别降低63.7%和83.8%，并具有更优的视觉质量。

Conclusion: 以残差传输提供显式信源约束、扩散熵模型增强分布建模、像素域去噪规避潜空间瓶颈，是提升生成式视频压缩性能的有效路径。

Abstract: Generative video compression (GVC) methods usually employ decoder-side diffusion models to denoise reconstructed signals based on generative priors. Besides, most of these methods perform denoising in latent space rather than directly in pixel domain. In this paper, we propose ResGVC, a residual coding framework for GVC that guides denoising toward original pictures by transmitting residual signals. ResGVC first encodes the original pictures within a learned video codec, and then decomposes the distortion between the codec reconstruction and the original pictures into a sequence of residual segments, where each residual segment is acquired at each denoising step. Residual segments can further be compressed by a novel residual codec with a diffusion transformer as an entropy model. Unlike prior methods, ResGVC introduces explicit source constraints throughout denoising rather than relying on decoder-side generation alone. The diffusion-based entropy model also enables more effective distribution modeling capability based on the powerful generative priors. Moreover, denoising directly in pixel space avoids the irreversible information loss caused by latent representation bottlenecks. Experimental results show that, compared with the previous state-of-the-art method, ResGVC achieves 63.7% and 83.8% BD-rate reductions in terms of LPIPS and PSNR, respectively, while also delivering superior visual quality.

</details>


### [72] [Diagnostic Loss: Asymmetric Supervision for Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30980)
*Ignacy Kolton,Wenxuan Li,Zongwei Zhou,Jarosław B. Ćwikła,Arkadiusz Sitek,Pedro R. A. S. Bassi,Szymon Płotka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Diagnostic Loss（DLoss），一种作用于解码器 token 概率的可微、架构无关的目标函数，通过对受支持与不受支持诊断词表进行非对称池化，缓解常规语言建模与放射报告非对称诊断需求之间的目标错位，提升 3D MedVLM 的诊断忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MedVLM 多采用 token 级最大似然目标，优先保证文本一致性而非诊断关键术语，导致模型遗漏关键诊断或引入错误诊断。作者将此界定为常规语言建模与放射报告"非对称诊断需求"（必须明确陈述受支持诊断、同时避免不受支持诊断）之间的目标错位。

Method: DLoss 直接在解码器 token 概率上操作，分别对与参考报告所支持诊断相关的词表和与不受支持替代诊断相关的词表聚合概率质量；在报告各 token 位置上采用非对称池化——鼓励受支持诊断至少在某一位置获得足够概率，而在所有位置上抑制不受支持诊断的虚假概率尖峰。该方法无需修改视觉骨干、语言解码器或推理流程，也不增加推理开销。

Result: 在 50,000 例腹部 CT 扫描上，跨多种 3D MedVLM 架构，于分布内与分布外报告生成基准进行训练与评估。DLoss 在各设定下提升诊断忠实度，同时在常规语言生成指标上保持有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 结果表明显式编码诊断需求的非对称序列级监督对放射报告生成具有价值；DLoss 作为一种轻量、可插拔的目标函数，可广泛用于现有 3D 医学视觉语言模型。代码已开源。

Abstract: Automated radiology report generation requires models to do more than produce fluent clinical narratives: they must explicitly state supported diagnoses while avoiding unsupported ones. However, existing medical vision-language models (MedVLMs) are typically trained with token-level maximum likelihood objectives, which prioritize text agreement over diagnostically critical terms. As a result, models can miss key diagnoses and introduce wrong ones. We identify this as an objective misalignment between conventional language modeling and the asymmetric diagnostic requirements of radiology reporting. To encode these requirements, we introduce Diagnostic Loss (DLoss), a differentiable, architecture-agnostic objective that operates directly on decoder token probabilities. DLoss separately aggregates probability mass over vocabularies associated with diagnoses supported by the reference report and with unsupported alternatives. Using asymmetric pooling across token positions in the report, it encourages supported diagnoses to receive sufficient probability in at least one position while suppressing spurious probability spikes for unsupported diagnoses across all positions during training. The loss can be applied to existing 3D MedVLMs without modifying their vision backbones, language decoders, or inference procedures, and introduces no additional inference-time computation. We train and evaluate DLoss on 50,000 abdominal CT scans across several 3D MedVLM architectures on both in-distribution and out-of-distribution report-generation benchmarks. Across these settings, DLoss improves diagnostic faithfulness while maintaining competitive performance on conventional language-generation metrics, showing the value of asymmetric sequence-level supervision for radiology report generation. The code for DLoss is available at https://github.com/gmum/DLoss.

</details>


### [73] [GSHeadRelight: Fast Relightability for 3D Gaussian Head Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30619)
*Henglei Lv,Bailin Deng,Jianzhu Guo,Xiaoqiang Liu,Pengfei Wan,Di Zhang,Lin Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对3D感知人像重打光任务，基于生成式3D高斯与统一的神经辐射传输表示，构建对入射光线性响应的光照模型，仅用in-the-wild图像训练，实现SOTA重打光效果与约12倍渲染加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人像重打光与新颖视角合成在摄影、VR/AR中至关重要，但3D感知人像重打光需同时满足照片级真实感、实时性与对未见主体的泛化。现有方法依赖昂贵且有限的光照棚（light stage）监督数据，或结果欠佳；且多基于生成式NeRF，存在3D一致性差、实时性低的问题。

Method: 采用生成式3D高斯（generative 3D Gaussians）作为表示，设计基于统一神经辐射传输（neural radiance transfer）表示的光照模型，使其对入射光照呈线性响应，从而支持重打光；训练仅使用in-the-wild图像，无需光stage数据。

Result: 在人像重打光任务上取得SOTA效果，与以往3D感知人像重打光方法相比渲染速度提升约12倍（×12）。

Conclusion: 将光照线性响应的神经辐射传输表示与生成式3D高斯结合，可在无光照棚监督、仅用in-the-wild数据的条件下实现高质量、实时的3D感知人像重打光与新颖视角合成。

Abstract: Relighting and novel view synthesis of human portraits are essential in applications such as portrait photography, virtual reality (VR), and augmented reality (AR). Despite recent progress, 3D-aware portrait relighting remains challenging due to the demands for photorealistic rendering, real-time performance, and generalization to unseen subjects. Existing works either rely on supervision from limited and expensive light stage captured data or produce suboptimal results. Moreover, many works are based on generative NeRFs, which suffer from poor 3D consistency and low real-time performance. We resort to recent progress on generative 3D Gaussians and design a lighting model based on a unified neural radiance transfer representation, which responds linearly to incident light. Using only in-the-wild images, our method achieves state-of-the-art relighting results and a significantly faster rendering speed ($\times12$) compared to previous 3D-aware portrait relighting research. Project page: http://www.geometrylearning.com/GSHeadRelight/.

</details>


### [74] [AVAtt: A Free-Viewing Eye-Tracking Dataset and Benchmark for Visual Attention on Paintings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30481)
*Mohamed Amine Kerkouri,Marouane Tliba,Aladine Chetouani,Alessandro Bruno*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AVAtt——首个覆盖16个艺术流派、五个世纪绘画的自由观看眼动数据集，含扫描路径与注视数据、注视/显著图、时间显著视频及艺术元数据；并系统评测经典与深度显著模型、排序44种扫描路径相似度度量（含基于CNN/ViT/自监督编码器的无语言图像编码器度量族），揭示艺术感知建模的巨大提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显著性与扫描路径模型几乎全部基于自然照片构建，而绘画在纹理、色彩与抽象程度上与照片不同，且由艺术家有意构图以引导视线，现有模型对此类艺术图像存在明显适配缺口，缺乏专门的大规模自由观看眼动数据集。

Method: 构建AVAtt自由观看眼动数据集：多幅绘画、每幅由多名观察者观看，记录个体扫描路径（含注视时序与眼屏距离）、注视图、按1°视角平滑的显著图、时间显著视频与艺术元数据。实验上以人类一致性为基准评测经典与深度显著模型；排名44种扫描路径相似度度量，并提出基于10个CNN/ViT/自监督编码器的无语言图像编码器度量族；同时设计去除观看偏置的对照实验，分析面部注视富集、局部结构吸引、构图规则与艺术风格对观察者一致性的影响。

Result: 基准测试显示艺术感知建模仍有显著提升空间；在去除观看偏置后，面部被注视的频率显著高于期望值，且该效应在半秒内出现；局部结构对视线具有吸引力；由图像内容拟合的构图规则可预测注视位置；艺术风格影响观察者间的一致性程度。

Conclusion: 绘画与照片在眼动行为上存在系统性差异，亟需艺术感知感知模型；AVAtt提供了数据集、代码与Python包，为艺术感知建模、显著性与扫描路径度量研究提供了统一基准与方法工具。

Abstract: Saliency and scanpath models are developed almost exclusively on natural photographs, yet paintings are composed by artists to guide the viewer's eye and differ from photographs in texture, colour and abstraction. We introduce \avatt, a free-viewing eye-tracking dataset on \nImages{} paintings spanning sixteen art movements and five centuries, with \nScanpaths{} scanpaths and \nFixations{} fixations from \nParticipants{} participants (\spPerImageMin--\spPerImageMax{} observers per painting). For every painting we release individual scanpaths with fixation timing and eye--screen distance, fixation maps, saliency maps smoothed at one degree of visual angle, temporal saliency videos and rich art metadata. We benchmark classic and deep saliency models against human agreement, revealing substantial headroom for art-aware models, and rank 44 scanpath-similarity measures, including a new language-free family of image-encoder metrics built on ten CNN, ViT and self-supervised encoders. With controls that remove viewing biases, we show that faces are fixated \faceEnrich$\times$ more than expected and within half a second, that local structure draws the eye, that composition rules fitted from image content predict where people look, and that art style shapes how much viewers agree. Dataset, code and a Python package are available at: https://huggingface.co/datasets/Amine-CV/AVAtt.

</details>


### [75] [SpectralCanary: Embarrassingly Simple Low-Frequency Traces for T2I Dataset-Use Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31659)
*Sy-Tuyen Ho,Anirudh Satheesh,Soham Chawathe,Huy Nghiem,Yongkyun Lee,Andrew Adams Schoen,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpectralCanary：用低频平滑掩膜将固定金丝雀图像融入数据集每张图，无需生成或逐图优化，轻量验证器在可疑模型输出中检测该信号，实现高TPR、高保真、可抗自适应攻击的数据集使用审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 判断T2I模型是否在特定图像集合上微调很困难：模型可吸收集合级视觉特征却不复现训练图。主动式数据集使用审计需在发布前埋入共享信号，但现有方法在可验证性、保真度、鲁棒性与可部署性之间存在权衡。

Method: 提出SpectralCanary：用平滑低通掩膜把固定金丝雀图像混合进每张集合图像，不改动caption，无需辅助生成或逐图优化；再用轻量验证器在可疑模型输出中检测转移到输出的信号。设计基于低频放置，并以匹配能量消融检验频率位置的影响。

Result: Pokemon数据集8项测试与3种真实变换、两类架构上，平均单生成TPR@1%FPR达91.1%，30次生成下固定阈值模型级准确率94.8%，均为对比方法最高。SD3.5下跨艺术、医学、卫星、时尚五数据集平均TPR@1%FPR 92.4%，SSIM 0.989、LPIPS 0.022、平均ΔFID 0.05。消融显示低频比中/高频迁移更可靠；最强自适应去除攻击需激进修改并带来>25 ΔFID。

Conclusion: 低频视觉信号可作为集合级T2I审计痕迹的有效载体，SpectralCanary在可验证性、保真度、鲁棒性和部署便利性间取得较好平衡。

Abstract: Auditing whether a text-to-image (T2I) model was fine-tuned on a particular image collection is difficult: a model can absorb collection-level visual characteristics without reproducing training images. Proactive dataset-use auditing plants a shared signal in a dataset before release, then later checks whether that signal appears in a suspect model's generated outputs. Existing approaches often trade off verifiability, fidelity, robustness, and deployability. We introduce $\textbf{SpectralCanary}$, which uses a smooth low-pass mask to blend a fixed canary image into every collection image while leaving captions unchanged and requiring neither auxiliary generation nor per-image optimization. A lightweight verifier detects the transferred signal in suspect-model outputs. Across eight tests on the Pokemon dataset and three realistic data transformations, spanning two architectures families, SpectralCanary achieves the highest average per-generation TPR@$1\%$ FPR ($91.1\%$) and fixed threshold model-level accuracy using 30 generations ($94.8\%$) among the evaluated methods. Across five datasets spanning artistic, medical, satellite, and fashion domains with SD3.5, SpectralCanary averages $92.4\%$ per-generation TPR@$1\%$ FPR while preserving the released images (SSIM $0.989$, LPIPS $0.022$) with a near-zero mean change FID (mean $Δ$FID $0.05$) across datasets. Matched-energy ablations on Pokemon and ROCOv2 support our design choice, low-frequency placement transfers more reliably compared to mid/high-frequency placement. Under the evaluated adaptive attacks, the most effective removal requires aggressive modification and incurs more than $25$ $Δ$FID points. These results identify low-frequency visual signals as effective carriers for collection-level T2I audit traces.

</details>


### [76] [Gate3DGen: A Pre-processing Model that Selectively Purifies Real-World Images for 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31649)
*Xiaolin Li,Zihao Huang,Fanpeng Meng,Chen Yang,Guanjun Wu,Kai Zhang,Jiemin Fang,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Gate3DGen，一个可插拔的选择性预处理模型：用退化感知路由器判断输入是否干净（干净则绕行、不改动），并提取特征引导"3D友好"的一步净化器修复退化图像，配合合成数据引擎提供成对监督；无需重训练现有3D生成器即可提升真实退化图像的下游3D几何质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图3D生成在精选基准上保真度高，但在光照不均、模糊、遮挡等真实图像上性能显著下降。直接用现有生成器处理此类输入不可靠；而净化输入需要解决两个问题：决定何时介入（不能污染干净输入），以及在净化时保留3D生成所需的视觉线索。

Method: 1) 退化感知路由器（degradation-aware router）对输入分类：判为干净则直接旁路至3D生成器，判为退化则提取特征用于引导后续净化；2) 3D导向一步净化器（3D-oriented one-step purifier）以路由特征为条件，通过空间损失（spatial losses）约束物体身份、轮廓与纹理线索的保留；3) 构建合成数据引擎，渲染3D资产的干净视图并合成复合退化，提供成对监督；整体为对现有3D生成器的前处理门控，无需重训练生成器。

Result: 在合成基准上，净化平均仅需2.3秒，同时提升图像保真度与下游3D几何质量，相对退化输入使 TRELLIS.2 的网格距离降低61.5%；在真实照片上的VLM评测与人类偏好评估进一步验证了身份保持与退化纠正效果。

Conclusion: 选择性门控式预处理是弥合"精选基准"与"真实图像"之间3D生成性能差距的有效且轻量途径：仅对退化输入介入、保持干净输入不变，即可在低时延下显著改善下游几何。项目已开源代码与演示。

Abstract: Single-image 3D generation has achieved high fidelity on curated benchmarks, yet degrades sharply on real-world images with uneven illumination, blur, and occlusions. Purifying such inputs requires deciding when to intervene and preserving the visual cues needed for 3D generation, while leaving clean inputs unchanged. We introduce Gate3DGen, a selective pre-processing model that gates image inputs to existing 3D generators without retraining them. A degradation-aware router bypasses inputs classified as clean and extracts features to guide purification of the remaining inputs. Conditioned on these features, a 3D-oriented one-step purifier purifies degraded inputs with spatial losses that encourage preservation of object identity, contours, and textural cues. To provide paired supervision, we construct a synthetic data engine that renders clean views of 3D assets and synthesises composite degradations. On the synthetic benchmark, the purifier improves image fidelity and downstream 3D geometry in only 2.3s on average, reducing TRELLIS.2 mesh distance by 61.5% relative to degraded inputs. VLM evaluation and human preferences on real photographs further support identity preservation and degradation correction. More demos and code are available at https://supra-3d.github.io/gate3dgen.

</details>


### [77] [OmniRay: Camera-Agnostic Monocular 3D Reconstruction with Composite Ray Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31645)
*Namitha Guruprasad,Abhay Yadav,Cheng Peng,Rama Chellappa,Anand Bhattad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniRay 提出层级式射线表示（单调径向基 + 球谐残差 + 自由形式残差），以单图前馈方式实现相机无关的单目三维重建，在多种相机配置下达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目三维重建模型多假设针孔相机，在鱼眼/全景等广角图像上产生畸变几何；相机感知方法虽以逐像素射线场摆脱该假设，却用单一固定基同时表达整体相机投影与细节变化，表达能力受限。作者认为相机几何的射线表示具有层级复杂性。

Method: Ray Module 由三部分级联构成：捕捉径向对称结构的单调径向基、刻画平滑偏离的球谐残差、以及建模剩余局部变化的自由形式残差；三者以球面上的相继旋转方式复合，从而适配多样相机几何。给定单张图像，为每像素预测射线方向与尺度未知的径向距离，直接恢复三维点云。

Result: 在不同相机配置上的大量实验表明，OmniRay 在相机无关的单目三维重建任务上取得新的 SOTA；推理阶段无需任何相机内参或相机信息。

Conclusion: 将相机几何视为层级结构并以可复合的球面旋转表示射线，是一种简洁且有效的相机无关建模范式，可替代单一固定基的射线场表示。

Abstract: Monocular models recover 3D geometry from a single image in one pass, but most assume a pinhole camera and produce distorted geometry on the wide-angle imagery produced by fisheye and panoramic lenses. Camera-aware models remove this assumption by predicting a per-pixel ray field. However, they represent different camera geometries using a single fixed basis to capture both the overall camera projection and its finer variations. We argue that camera geometry can be viewed as having a hierarchy of complexity for representing rays. We present OmniRay, a feed-forward model whose Ray Module is composed of three components: a monotone radial base that captures radially symmetric structure, a spherical-harmonic residual for smooth deviations from it, and a free-form residual for remaining local variation. These components are composed as successive rotations on the sphere, allowing the representation to accommodate diverse camera geometries. Given a single image, OmniRay predicts a ray direction and an up-to-scale radial distance for every pixel, from which a 3D point cloud is recovered without needing camera information at inference time. Extensive experiments across different camera configurations demonstrate that OmniRay establishes a new state-of-the-art in camera-agnostic monocular 3D reconstruction.

</details>


### [78] [UniVOE: A Removal-Driven Framework for Interaction-Aware Universal Video Object Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31641)
*Shuzhou Yang,Lanqing Guo,Lingen Li,Weiqi Li,Zhao Zhong,Liefeng Bo,Jian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 UniVOE，以"移除驱动注入"的统一框架同时训练视频物体移除与注入模型：先用视频修复先验训练效果感知移除器，将真实物体存在视频转成物体缺失视频以构成大规模配对监督，再反向训练注入模型，并结合奖励模型做偏好对齐后训练，在两类任务上均达 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移除与注入互为逆操作，但训练难度不对称：移除可从视频修复中有效自举并适配以擦除交互效应；注入则需大规模配对监督来建模物体外观、动态与场景相关效应，而真实配对难以获取、合成数据真实性多样性不足。因此需要一个能复用移除能力的统一训练范式。

Method: 1) 迁移视频修复先验，并在物体存在/缺失配对数据上微调，得到效果感知（effect-aware）移除器；2) 用该移除器把真实物体存在视频自动转为物体缺失视频，构造大规模配对监督；3) 在此配对上按反向目标训练注入模型，恢复物体及其动态与交互效应；4) 引入基于专有奖励模型的 reward-based 后训练，使注入结果与人类偏好对齐，弥补像素级重建对感知真实性的不足。

Result: 与强开源系统及商业系统对比，UniVOE 在物体移除与注入两项任务上均取得 SOTA，尤其在交互效应等困难场景中优势明显。

Conclusion: 移除与注入可通过"移除驱动"方式统一建模：移除器既是任务模型，也是注入任务的数据引擎；配合奖励后训练可进一步提升感知质量。该范式缓解了注入任务配对数据稀缺的问题。

Abstract: Video object removal and injection are reciprocal editing operations between object-present and object-absent video states, yet they differ substantially in training difficulty. Removal can be effectively bootstrapped from video inpainting and further adapted to erase object-related interaction effects. Injection, however, requires large-scale paired supervision to model object appearance, dynamics, and scene-dependent effects, while perfectly aligned real-world pairs are difficult to collect and synthetic data lacks sufficient realism and diversity. To address this challenge, we present UniVOE, a removal-driven framework for training both video object removal and injection models. We first train an effect-aware remover by transferring video inpainting priors and finetuning on object-present/object-absent pairs. The trained remover converts real object-present videos into object-absent counterparts, creating large-scale paired supervision for injection. An injection model is trained on these pairs in the reverse direction to recover objects together with their dynamics and interaction effects. Moreover, since pixel-level reconstruction alone cannot adequately capture perceptual realism, we introduce a reward-based post-training method that aligns injection results with human preferences using a proprietary reward model. Experiments against strong open-source and commercial systems demonstrate that UniVOE achieves state-of-the-art performance on both object removal and injection, particularly in challenging interaction-effect cases.

</details>


### [79] [Diffusion-Based Synthesis of Complete 3D Biparametric Prostate MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31523)
*Claudia Giardina,Montse Pardàs,Verónica Vilaplana*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出条件式3D扩散框架，由解剖掩膜合成完整前列腺bpMRI，支持顺序合成与联合合成两种策略，并在三个公开数据集上验证可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模且具代表性的前列腺MRI数据集稀缺，限制了深度学习应用；合成双参数MRI（bpMRI）有望缓解这一数据瓶颈。

Method: 构建条件式3D扩散框架，实现解剖掩膜条件化的完整bpMRI生成。支持两种路径：顺序合成（分别训练模型生成T2w与扩散模态）与联合合成（单模型同时生成全部bpMRI模态）。基于PI-CAI、FastMRI Prostate与PROSTATEx训练五种配置，考察不同条件方案、目标模态与合成策略；采用分布类指标、配对图像相似度及定性评估进行评价。

Result: 两种合成路径均具可行性；联合合成在生成完整bpMRI的同时取得有竞争力的分布保真度；直接合成ADC与计算高b值DWI优于由合成扩散加权图像间接推导。

Conclusion: 解剖条件化3D扩散合成可用于前列腺bpMRI数据增广，联合合成在完整性与保真度间较为均衡，且关键派生模态宜直接生成而非由合成源图推导。

Abstract: Synthetic biparametric MRI (bpMRI) may help alleviate the limited availability of large and representative prostate MRI datasets for deep learning applications. In this work, we present a conditional 3D diffusion framework for anatomy-conditioned synthesis of complete prostate bpMRI from anatomical masks. The framework supports sequential synthesis, in which T2w and diffusion modalities are generated by separately trained models, and joint synthesis, in which all bpMRI modalities are generated simultaneously within a single model. Five configurations were trained using PI-CAI, FastMRI Prostate, and PROSTATEx datasets to investigate different conditioning schemes, target modalities, and synthesis strategies. Evaluation using distribution-based metrics, paired-image similarity, and qualitative assessment demonstrated the feasibility of both synthesis pathways. Joint synthesis achieved competitive distributional fidelity while generating complete bpMRI, whereas direct synthesis of ADC and computed high-b-value DWI outperformed their derivation from synthetic diffusion-weighted images.

</details>


### [80] [AnyGameRenderer: Entity-Consistent Generative Rendering Across Games and an Entity-Level Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31466)
*Ruicong Liu,Liangyang Ouyang,Fengbo Lan,Zhixiang Wang,Kaipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AnyGameRenderer 是一个视频到视频的生成式渲染器，可将 AI 编程代理制作的"程序员美术"式游戏画面（灰模、色块、语义色块）转化为具备角色、动作细节与特效的高质量视觉，同时保持布局、相机以及每个实体的位置与时序不变，并在实体级基准上显著改善了实体对应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 编程代理已能快速生成可玩的小游戏，但画面普遍停留在灰模、彩色几何体、扁平语义色块等"程序员美术"水平，缺乏角色造型、动作细节与特效。作者希望在不改变游戏逻辑与画面结构的前提下，低成本地提升这类原型的视觉质量，使生成式渲染适用于代理所产出的多种游戏类型。

Method: 以纯视频作为游戏状态输入，无需引擎 G-buffer、逐物体类别标签或低模代理；灰模、彩色基元、语义图和线框均可作为输入。实现方式为对 33B 的参考视频条件视频生成模型 MiniMax-H3 ref2va 进行 LoRA 微调。训练数据约 1 万对配对片段，来自三类来源：(i) 由 AI 游戏创作工具 GEAR 导出的 38 款代理制作游戏的多流渲染数据；(ii) Unreal Engine 双流渲染数据，人群、数量变化与实体尺度均程序化控制；(iii) 真实游戏录像，通过 3D 框检测重建配对。此外提出包含 59 个留出案例、753 个实体的实体级基准，以真值框为评分依据。

Result: 相较基座模型：时序匹配率从 50.9% 提升至 62.9%；漏检数量变化从 34.7% 降至 21.3%；幻觉实体与未渲染实体数量均减少。方法在塔防、自走棋、俯视角生存、回合制战斗、卡丁车竞速、人群跑酷等多类游戏上均具泛化性，并能保持群体计数正确及实体在正确帧出现/消失。

Conclusion: AnyGameRenderer 表明仅以视频作为接口即可实现结构保真、实体一致的通用游戏画面生成式渲染，无需复杂引擎表征；其数据构造策略（GEAR 多流数据、UE 可控数据、真实录像 3D 框重建）与实体级基准为后续研究提供了可复用的范式，但实体对应性仍有较大提升空间。

Abstract: AI coding agents can now build small playable games in minutes, but the results usually look like programmer art: gray blockouts, colored geometric primitives, and flat semantic blobs. We present AnyGameRenderer, a video-to-video generative renderer that turns such footage into high-quality visuals with characters, motion detail, and effects. Throughout, it keeps the layout, camera, and every entity's position and timing intact.
  Our AnyGameRenderer generalizes across a wide range of genres that agents build, such as tower defense, auto-battlers, top-down survivors, turn-based battles, kart racing, and crowd runners.
  AnyGameRenderer takes plain video as its game-state input, with no complex representations such as engine G-buffers, per-object class labels, or low-poly proxies. Gray blockouts, colored primitives, semantic maps, and wireframes all work as input.
  It also preserves entity correspondence: group counts stay correct, and entities appear and disappear on the right frame.
  We implement AnyGameRenderer by LoRA fine-tuning MiniMax-H3 ref2va, a 33B reference-video-conditioned video generation model. Training uses $\sim$10k paired clips from three sources: (i) multi-stream rendering data from 38 agent-made games exported from GEAR, an AI game-creation tool; (ii) two-stream rendering data from Unreal Engine, with programmatically controlled crowds, count changes, and entity scales; and (iii) real gameplay recordings, with pairs reconstructed via 3D-box detection.
  To evaluate the entity correspondence, we propose an entity-level benchmark of 59 held-out cases with 753 entities, scored against ground-truth boxes. Compared with the base model, AnyGameRenderer better preserves entity correspondence: temporal match rises from 50.9\% to 62.9\%, missed count changes fall from 34.7\% to 21.3\%, and fewer entities are hallucinated or left unrendered.

</details>


### [81] [VisCurate: Evaluating Behavioral Equivalence of Visual Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30405)
*Md Farhan Ishraq,Md Farhan Ishmam,Reaz Hassan Joarder,Md Hasanul Kabir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 VisCurate：基于执行行为而非文本描述来整理视觉技能库的框架与基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉智能体的技能库（裁剪、二值化、边缘检测等图像到图像操作）不断膨胀，积累了大量重复、错标与缺陷技能；而常规用于治理的信号（名称、描述、源码）几乎无法反映技能对图像的实际视觉改变，故需转向基于观察到的执行结果进行治理。

Method: VisCurate 在共享探针电池上运行每个候选技能对，判定六种行为关系之一，每种关系对应一种可安全执行的库编辑；基准通过向干净库中按受控速率注入七类缺陷，生成 300 个可复现实例，含真值关系与修复方案。

Result: 文本基线在困难负例上最多接受 6 种错误合并中的 5 种，而本文验证器零误接受，并恢复 51.2% 真正可合并的技能对；在两个真实未治理库上，发现某包与其下游副本共享技能中 92% 为冗余，且 17 条已公布的等价声明中执行仅确认 10 条；九个自动治理器中最佳者仅恢复约一半修复。

Conclusion: 视觉技能库应依据行为而非描述来治理；同时该治理问题仍为开放问题，现有自动化方法远未充分解决。

Abstract: Modern vision-based agents have shifted toward skill-based problem-solving, where a skill is an executable image-to-image operation, e.g., cropping, thresholding, or edge detection. As these skill libraries grow, they accumulate duplicated, mislabeled, and defective skills. The signals normally used to curate them (a skill's name, description, or source code) say little about how the skill visually changes the image. We present VisCurate, a framework and benchmark that curates visual skill libraries from observed execution rather than from text. VisCurate runs each candidate skill pair on a shared probe battery and assigns one of six behavioral relations, each tied to a library edit that is safe to make. The benchmark injects seven defect types into a clean library at controlled rates, producing 300 reproducible instances with ground-truth relations and repairs. While text baselines accept up to five of six false merges on hard negatives, our verifier accepts none and recovers 51.2% of the pairs that are truly mergeable. On two real, uncurated libraries, VisCurate finds that 92% of the skills a package shares with its downstream copy are redundant, while execution confirms only 10 of 17 published equivalence claims. The curation problem also remains open, as the strongest of the nine automated curators recovers only about half of the repairs. We emphasize that visual skill libraries should be curated by behavior, not by description.

</details>


### [82] [FiLM-CAM: Keyed Feature Modulation for Conditional-Access Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30347)
*Yash Kulthe,Vishal Asnani,Shruti Agarwal,Andrew Gilbert,John Collomosse*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FiLM-CAM：用密钥条件化的特征级线性调制（FiLM）在特征层将水印载荷与密钥耦合，实现授权编码、解码与移除的条件访问图像水印框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像水印缺乏对“谁可使用”的控制：需要一种条件访问机制，使正确密钥下可靠嵌入/提取，错误密钥下失效，并支持受密钥约束的水印移除。

Method: 以秘密密钥条件化FiLM机制，在嵌入前变换水印信号的内部表示，使载荷与密钥在特征层而非像素层耦合；同一密钥用于条件化解码器恢复载荷；训练目标为最大化授权与未授权使用间的性能差距；编码器估计对已有水印不变的残差，配合迭代精化的重编码-相减实现条件移除。

Result: 在多种水印编解码骨干网络上评估，键控特征调制均能作为有效的、架构无关的条件访问模块（CAM），正确密钥下可靠提取、错误密钥下诱导失败。

Conclusion: 特征级键控调制是实现图像水印条件访问的通用方案，兼顾授权提取与受控移除，且与骨干架构解耦。

Abstract: We propose FiLM-CAM; a conditional-access image watermarking approach that enforces authorized encoding, decoding, and removal through keyed feature modulation. A secret key conditions a Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM) mechanism that transforms the internal representation of the watermark signal before embedding, thereby coupling the payload to the key at the feature, rather than pixel level. The same key must be presented to condition the decoder to correctly recover the payload embedded in an image. To enforce conditional access, we train the system to maximize the performance gap between authorized and unauthorized use, ensuring reliable extraction under the correct key while inducing failure under incorrect keys. In addition, we design our encoder to estimate the watermark residual invariant to the presence of pre-existing watermarks. This design enables conditional watermark removal via simple re-encoding and subtraction of the residual using iterative refinement, contingent on knowledge of the secret key. We evaluate FiLM-CAM across multiple watermarking encoder-decoder backbones, demonstrating that keyed feature modulation serves as an effective, architecture-agnostic conditional access module (CAM) for image watermarking.

</details>


### [83] [EgoHand: Monocular 3D Hand Reconstruction from Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30293)
*Chao-Yeh Chen,Tiffany Chen,Lin Duan,Tianyi Hu,Yu-Hsiang Huang,Umar Iqbal,Jiefeng Li,Edy Lim,Yanchen Liu,Yihan Pang,Hang Qiu,Jun Saito,Shao Su,Qingsi Wang,Xiufeng Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出单目第一视角3D手部重建模型EgoHand，构建1400万图像训练语料，在HOT3D/ARCTIC/H2O零样本评测中取得SOTA，推理延迟约9.69 ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一视角（egocentric）视频下的单目3D手部重建缺乏兼顾场景多样性与监督质量的训练数据，现有方法在域外泛化、几何一致性与图像对齐上表现不足。

Method: 整理1400万张图像的训练语料（合成数据+野外视频+多相机动作捕捉）；采用SOMA-Hand参数化表示；对2D手部裁剪块用Transformer编码器处理，并引入相机线索与辅助2D分支以提升几何一致性与图像对齐。

Result: 在HOT3D、ARCTIC、H2O上零样本评测中关节位置、根节点平移与加速度误差均低于对比基线，推理延迟约9.69 ms；盲测用户偏好研究显示重建姿态优于Apple EgoDex与Ropedia Xperience-10M。

Conclusion: EgoHand在第一视角单目3D手部重建上实现SOTA与强域外泛化，并以OpenMDW许可开源模型与代码，允许免费商用。

Abstract: We introduce EgoHand, a monocular 3D hand reconstruction model that achieves state-of-the-art performance on egocentric video. Central to our approach is the curation of a high-quality training corpus of 14 million images, combining synthetic data, in-the-wild video, and multi-camera motion capture to balance scene diversity and supervision quality. The model adopts SOMA-Hand as its parametric representation and processes 2D hand crops with a transformer encoder, incorporating camera cues and an auxiliary 2D branch to improve geometric consistency and image alignment. Zero-shot evaluations on HOT3D, ARCTIC, and H2O demonstrate strong out-of-domain generalization, with lower joint-position, root-translation, and acceleration errors than competing baselines at an inference latency of approximately 9.69 ms. A blinded user preference study further shows that participants favor EgoHand's reconstructed hand poses over those from Apple EgoDex and Ropedia Xperience-10M. We release the EgoHand model and source code under the OpenMDW license, allowing free commercial use of the model.

</details>


### [84] [Stream4D: Streaming 4D Reconstruction with Training-free Motion Decoupling via Causal KV-Cache Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31320)
*Wei Zhang,Ying Feng,Mengtong Wu,Shiqiang Gong,Huaqi Zhang,Qi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Stream4D 是一种免训练、动静解耦的流式 4D 重建框架，利用因果注意力 KV cache 中的时序运动线索生成动态掩码，并通过软键掩码抑制动态区域干扰，提升相机位姿估计与静态场景重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式重建方法普遍假设场景静态，在长序列动态场景中会产生位姿漂移与重影伪影，需要一种能处理动态物体、且无需额外训练、可直接嵌入现有流式架构的方案。

Method: 核心观察是因果注意力在 KV cache 中隐式编码了丰富的时序运动线索。方法自适应地从多个注意力层与不同时间窗口聚合这些线索，并与深度先验融合以应对遮挡、弱纹理等困难情形，从而生成精确动态掩码实现动静解耦；随后基于该掩码施加软键掩码（soft key masking），抑制因果注意力中动态区域的干扰以提升位姿估计精度。框架免训练，保持因果注意力的效率并兼容 StreamVGGT、Stream3R 等骨干。

Result: 在多个动态场景基准上，Stream4D 显著提升相机位姿估计与静态场景重建质量，产出可靠的动态掩码，并有效缓解长序列重影问题。

Conclusion: 因果注意力的时序运动线索可被直接挖掘用于动静解耦，无需训练即可嵌入现有流式 Transformer，为动态场景流式 4D 重建提供高效且通用的增强方案。

Abstract: 4D scene reconstruction requires robust handling of dynamic objects, yet existing streaming methods commonly assume static scenes, leading to pose drift and ghosting artifacts over long sequences. We introduce Stream4D, a training-free, motion-decoupled streaming reconstruction framework that can be directly integrated into causal Transformer backbones (e.g., StreamVGGT, Stream3R). Our key observation is that causal attention implicitly encodes rich temporal motion cues within the KV cache. We adaptively aggregate these cues from multiple attention layers and across temporal windows, and fuse them with depth priors to handle challenging cases such as occlusions and textureless regions, producing precise dynamic masks that decouple static and dynamic regions. We then apply a soft key masking scheme that leverages these dynamic masks to suppress interference from dynamic regions in causal attention, thereby improving camera pose estimation accuracy. Stream4D inherits the efficiency of causal attention and remains compatible with existing streaming architectures. On diverse dynamic-scene benchmarks, Stream4D significantly improves camera pose estimation and static-scene reconstruction quality, yields reliable dynamic masks, and effectively mitigates long-sequence ghosting.

</details>


### [85] [Beyond Best-of-$k$: Prior-Consistent DAPS for Ambiguous Nonlinear Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31592)
*Merve Kocabas,Benjamin A. Burns,Andreas Geiger,Kent Gauen,Sara Fridovich-Keil*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PC-DAPS：在单次采样中维护多个候选解，随退火噪声下降按先验一致性剪枝替换，抑制"测量一致但先验不支持"的错误模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型已成为求解逆问题的强先验。但非线性逆问题（如相位检索）中，存在多个测量一致的解，仅能靠先验区分（称为模糊测量）。现有扩散采样器依赖重建过程中的局部近似，此时测量引导可能偏向某个"测量一致但先验不支持"的模式（虽靠近先验支持模式）。同时，评估常用best-of-k并按真值挑选，掩盖了此类失败。

Method: PC-DAPS：DAPS（Decoupled Annealing Posterior Sampling）的变体，在单次采样运行内维持多个候选解；随着退火噪声降低，依据先验一致性对较弱候选进行剪枝与替换，全程不使用ground truth。

Result: 降低先验不一致样本的出现概率，提升非线性逆问题的样本质量；在线性逆问题上保持样本质量与多样性。

Conclusion: 通过多候选与无监督的先验一致性筛选，可在不访问真值的前提下缓解模糊非线性逆问题中测量引导的先验不一致失败；主要局限是模糊性定义与先验一致性的度量方式未在摘要中细化。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become strong priors for solving inverse problems. However, nonlinear inverse problems such as phase retrieval remain challenging when multiple measurement-consistent solutions are distinguishable only by the prior; we call such measurements ambiguous. Existing diffusion-based samplers rely on local approximations during reconstruction. For an ambiguous measurement, these approximations can allow measurement guidance to favor a measurement-consistent mode that is close to a prior-supported mode but is itself not supported by the prior. Evaluation often masks this type of failure by reporting the best-of-$k$ runs, where the best is selected by comparison to the ground truth. We introduce Prior-Consistent DAPS (PC-DAPS), a variant of Decoupled Annealing Posterior Sampling (DAPS) designed to address this failure case by maintaining multiple candidate solutions within a single sampling run. As annealing noise decreases, PC-DAPS prunes and replaces weaker candidates according to their prior consistency, without any ground-truth access. PC-DAPS reduces the incidence of prior-inconsistent samples and improves sample quality on nonlinear inverse problems while preserving sample quality and diversity on linear inverse problems.

</details>


### [86] [RankAlign: Vision-Language Representation Alignment via Rank Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31145)
*Enzhe Zhao,Marco Fiorucci,Lamberto Ballan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 RankAlign：在无跨模态监督的类级别视觉-语言匹配中，先用基于秩的归一化把各模态内部相似度核转为序数关系表示，再做置换匹配，从而消除相似度值的单调重参数化差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Platonic Representation Hypothesis 认为视觉与语言模型趋向兼容的潜空间几何，但在无配对、无跨模态监督条件下恢复类级别对应仍困难。现有关系匹配方法直接比较模态内相似度核，而同一语义结构在不同模态中可能以不同的数值参数化呈现，导致直接比较失效。

Method: 将每个模态内的相似度核在置换匹配前转换为序数（秩）关系表示；不改动对应空间与匹配求解器。该表示对相似度值的严格单调递增重参数化保持不变，同时保留用于匹配的序数结构。

Result: 给出精确恢复与稳定性保证，分别在序数完全一致和部分一致情形下刻画对应关系可恢复性；在多种冻结视觉与语言编码器及多个数据集上的实验表明类级对应恢复更优且更稳定，包括标签损坏与域偏移情形。

Conclusion: 秩归一化是一种即插即用、理论可证的策略，可提升无监督跨模态类级匹配对数值参数化差异的鲁棒性。

Abstract: While the Platonic Representation Hypothesis posits that vision and language models converge toward compatible latent geometries, recovering class-level correspondences between unpaired modalities without cross-modal supervision remains challenging. Existing relational matching methods compare within-modality similarity kernels directly, although the same semantic structure may be represented through different numerical parameterizations across modalities. We propose RankAlign, a rank-based normalization strategy for class-level unpaired vision-language matching. RankAlign transforms each within-modality similarity kernel into an ordinal relational representation before permutation matching, while leaving the correspondence space and matching solver unchanged. The resulting representation is invariant to strictly increasing reparameterizations of similarity values while preserving the ordinal structure used for matching. We provide exact-recovery and stability guarantees that characterize correspondence recovery under both exact and partial ordinal agreement. Experiments across diverse frozen vision and language encoders and multiple datasets demonstrate improved and more stable class-level correspondence recovery, including under label corruption and domain shift.

</details>


### [87] [Pos4CHD: A Positive-Free Mask-Guided Counterfactual Synthesis Framework for Fetal Congenital Heart Defect Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31139)
*Wenjie Xuan,Yapeng Li,Hang Su,Chen Cheng,Juhua Liu,Sheng Zhao,Ziming Huang,Bo Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Pos4CHD 是一个无需正样本的掩码引导反事实生成框架，仅用正常胎儿心脏超声样本即可合成高保真、解剖一致的伪阳性样本，用于缓解胎儿先天性心脏病（CHD）诊断中的正样本稀缺问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胎儿CHD的自动化诊断受限于正样本稀缺：罕见亚型发病率低、隐私敏感导致正例难以采集，造成严重类别不平衡甚至缺失正类训练样本。现有条件生成式合成正样本的方法任务特定性强且依赖正样本训练，难以适用于胎儿CHD。

Method: 提出 Pos4CHD：(1) 首先构建 FE-Gen——面向胎儿心脏超声的掩码引导生成模型，缓解直方图漂移并增强图像-掩码空间对齐；(2) 在此基础上采用 mask-to-image 生成范式：先对语义掩码进行编辑以模拟胎儿心脏结构异常，再渲染出解剖一致的图像，从而实现仅依赖正常样本的伪阳性合成。

Result: 内部数据集与专家评估显示合成图像视觉保真度提升（FID -12.97）与解剖一致性提升（DSC +3.96%）。在下游真实CHD任务上取得稳定增益：零样本分类（EbA 93.25% ACC，TAPVC 72.92% ACC）、病灶检测（VSD mAP50 +6.95%）、解剖分割（胎儿心脏 DSC +10.49%）。

Conclusion: 掩码到图像的反事实生成可在无正样本条件下有效合成胎儿CHD伪阳性数据，兼具视觉与解剖合理性，并能显著提升多种下游诊断任务性能；代码与模型已开源。

Abstract: The scarcity of positive cases remains a major bottleneck in developing automated diagnostic systems for fetal Congenital Heart Defects~(CHDs), particularly for rare subtypes. Although normal cases are accessible with effort, positive cases are hard to collect due to low incidence rates and privacy concerns, causing severe class imbalance or absence of positive training samples. Existing works resort to synthesizing positives with conditional generative models, but they are task-specific and require positive training samples, hindering application for fetal CHDs. To this end, we propose a positive-free mask-guided counterfactual generation framework for fetal echocardiography, namely Pos4CHD, which enables realistic, high-quality synthesis of pseudo-positive samples from semantic masks, with only normal samples at hand. Technically, we first develop FE-Gen, a mask-guided generative model tailored for fetal heart ultrasound synthesis that mitigates histogram drift and enhances image-mask spatial alignment. Building on FE-Gen, Pos4CHD employs a mask-to-image generation paradigm for CHD synthesis. It first manipulates semantic masks to simulate fetal-heart structural anomalies and then renders anatomically consistent images. Extensive experiments on in-house datasets, together with expert evaluations, demonstrate the visual fidelity~($-12.97$ FID) and anatomical consistency~($+3.96\%$ DSC) of synthesized images. Further validations across diverse real-world CHD diagnosis tasks exhibit promising results and consistent increments, including zero-shot classification~(achieving $93.25\%$ ACC on EbA and $72.92\%$ ACC on TAPVC), lesion detection~($+6.95\%$ mAP50 on VSD), and anatomy segmentation~($+10.49\%$ DSC on fetal heart) compared with baselines. Codes and models are publicly available on \href{https://github.com/DREAMXFAR/Pos4CHD}{GitHub}.

</details>


### [88] [Through a PRISM, Clearly: Step-Sequence Reasoning for Mistake Recognition in Procedural Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31117)
*Olga Loginova,Oleksandr Bezrukov,Frank Keller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PRISM——通过可解释语义建模识别视频中程序性错误的框架：用语言grounding视频片段并映射为程序状态，以轻量HMM建模正确执行，实现插入/删除/替换/换位四类错误的显式识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序性错误识别需检测多步活动相对有效流程的偏差；现有方法缺乏可解释的显式推理接口，且跨异构基准的错误标注不统一，难以评估。

Method: 基于基准提供的步骤边界的"完成后识别"设定；PRISM将视频片段grounding到语言描述，映射到由仅用正确执行训练的轻量隐马尔可夫模型表示的程序状态；通过参考对齐与语义检查识别步骤内容与顺序错误。统一将各数据集标注映射为基于编辑的四类错误（insertion、deletion、substitution、transposition）。在三基准上比较gold与VLM生成描述在原始、语义角色、动作-对象三种形式下的表现，并设解码器对照（最近候选匹配 vs. 训练得到的局部解码器 vs. 序列解码）。

Result: 训练得到的局部解码器相较最近候选匹配在各基准上均带来一致增益，序列解码另带来依赖数据集的额外收益；语义角色标注在全部六组匹配的grounded比较中提升Any-F1，但增益随数据集而异，在独立配置选择下未转化为统一改进。

Conclusion: 语言grounding可作为显式程序性错误推理的有效接口；论文刻画了描述表示形式与解码策略各自的贡献，并为异构基准下的错误类型统一评估提供了方案。

Abstract: Procedural mistake recognition requires identifying deviations from valid multi-step activities. We study post-completion recognition from videos with benchmark-provided step boundaries. We introduce PRISM (Procedural mistake Recognition via Interpretable Semantic Modeling), which grounds video segments in language and maps their descriptions to procedural states using a lightweight hidden Markov model trained on correct executions. Reference alignment and semantic checks identify errors in step content and order. To support evaluation across heterogeneous benchmarks, we map dataset-specific annotations to four edit-based error types: insertion, deletion, substitution, and transposition. Across three benchmarks, we compare gold and VLM-generated descriptions in raw, semantic-role, and action-object forms. Decoder controls show consistent gains from the trained local decoder over nearest-candidate matching, with additional, dataset-dependent benefits from sequence decoding. Semantic role labeling improves Any-F1 in all six matched grounded comparisons, although the gains vary across datasets and do not translate into uniform improvements under separate configuration selection. These results establish language grounding as an effective interface for explicit procedural mistake reasoning and characterize the contributions of representation and decoding.

</details>


### [89] [Foundation4D: Towards a unified perception and reconstruction model for autonomous vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30340)
*NVIDIA,:,Sérgio Agostinho,Maximilian Apodaca,Emmanuel Attia,Guillem Braso,George Dong,Ilya Fedorov,Michele Fenzi,Gang Huang,Jiahui Huang,Shengyu Huang,Bingxin Ke,Laura Leal-Taixé,Kuan-Ting Lee,Mingxuan Liu,Tian Liu,Tim Meinhardt,Aljoša Ošep,Naveen Kumar Rai,Kefei Ren,Cristiano Saltori,Keqiang Wu,Ning Xu,Fei Xue,Jin-Bey Yu,Lei Zhang,Qunjie Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Foundation4D是一个统一的前馈模型，从多相机驾驶视频中同时完成几何估计、3D检测、语义占用与高斯重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶场景感知任务通常各自独立建模，缺乏能同时输出几何、检测、占用与重建的统一前馈框架，也缺少带稠密深度与语义占用标注的公开基准。

Method: 共享骨干网络采用因子化时空注意力跨相机与时间融合观测；视频推理阶段用位姿图优化精化相机轨迹并跨输入窗口组装目标轨迹；同时构建数据标注流程并发布PAI-Foundation4D基准（1,253个驾驶片段，含稠密深度与语义占用标签）。

Result: 经基准特定微调后，在Waymo与nuScenes上取得最低深度AbsRel，nuScenes上最高检测mAP，OpenOccupancy上最高占用mIoU；任务组合研究显示检测与几何/占用联合训练使top-class mAP提升1.2点，几何与检测联合训练使相机到车体平移误差相对仅几何训练降低13.4%。

Conclusion: 多任务协同训练对检测与几何均带来增益，且预测的几何与轨迹可作为无激光雷达NuRec的先验，支持驾驶场景重建与仿真。

Abstract: We introduce Foundation4D, a unified feed-forward model for geometry estimation, 3D detection, semantic occupancy, and Gaussian reconstruction from multi-camera driving video. A shared backbone uses factorized spatiotemporal attention to efficiently combine observations across cameras and time. Our video inference pipeline uses pose graph optimization to refine camera trajectories and assembles object tracks across input windows. We also develop a data annotation pipeline and release PAI-Foundation4D, a benchmark with dense depth and semantic occupancy labels for 1,253 driving clips. With benchmark-specific fine-tuning, Foundation4D achieves the lowest depth AbsRel on Waymo and nuScenes, the highest detection mAP on nuScenes, and the highest occupancy mIoU on OpenOccupancy among the compared methods. Our task-combination study shows benefits for both detection and geometry: co-training detection with geometry and occupancy improves top-class mAP by 1.2 points over detection-only training, while co-training geometry with detection reduces camera-to-rig translation error by $13.4\%$ relative to geometry-only training. We further use the predicted geometry and trajectories as priors for lidarless NuRec, supporting reconstruction of driving scenes for simulation.

</details>


### [90] [Unified Trajectory and 3D Pose Forecasting for Multi-Agent Sports Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30519)
*Marc Peral,Guillem Capellera,Luis Ferraz,Antonio Rubio,Stella Graßhof,Dan Witzner Hansen,Antonio Agudo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出社会-时间编码器在输入层面融合轨迹与姿态，通过共享因子化注意力联合建模，并辅以逐关节损失重加权与频域轨迹平滑，配合无重叠评测协议，在WorldPose与3DPW上超越先前联合预测方法，姿态精度提升最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测人体未来运动需同时推理全局轨迹与3D人体姿态，二者紧密耦合；团队运动中多智能体交互带来复杂社会动态，单独建模难以充分利用耦合关系。

Method: 1) 社会-时间编码器：输入层融合轨迹与姿态，经共享的因子化注意力联合建模；2) 逐关节损失重加权：强调动态关节、避免退化解；3) 频域轨迹平滑项：提升轨迹预测并间接改善姿态预测；4) 识别并纠正重叠序列构造导致的评测偏差，采用无重叠协议与确定性、无oracle的多未来预测策略。

Result: 在WorldPose与3DPW上均优于先前的联合预测方法，其中姿态精度提升最为显著，表明联合建模可泛化到非密集交互场景。

Conclusion: 轨迹与姿态的联合建模式预测有效且具泛化性；同时强调公平评测协议对联合预测方法比较的必要性。

Abstract: Forecasting future human motion requires jointly reasoning about an agent's global trajectory and 3D body pose, which are tightly coupled, particularly in team sports where multiple interacting players introduce complex social dynamics. We exploit a socio-temporal encoder that fuses trajectory and pose at the input level and models them jointly through shared factorized attention. The model is further enhanced by a per-joint loss reweighting scheme that emphasizes dynamic joints and prevents degenerate solutions, and a frequency-domain trajectory smoothing term that improves trajectory prediction while indirectly benefiting pose forecasting. To ensure fair comparison with prior joint forecasting methods, we identify and correct evaluation biases arising from overlapping sequence construction, adopting instead a non-overlapping protocol and a deterministic, oracle-free strategy for multi-future prediction. Our approach improves over prior joint forecasting methods on both WorldPose and 3DPW, with the largest gains on pose accuracy, demonstrating that the proposed joint formulation generalizes beyond the densely interactive scenarios that motivate it.

</details>


### [91] [Attention-guided classification and tumor localization of combined hepatocellular-cholangiocarcinoma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30944)
*Xiaowen Liang,Hélène Cazier,Aurélie Beaufrère,Miguel Albuquerque,Valérie Paradis,Cédric Wemmert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 利用HES染色的全切片图像训练分类模型，区分HCC、CCA与cHCC-CCA三种肝脏肿瘤亚型，并为混合型肿瘤图像中不同肿瘤成分的定位提供潜在方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: cHCC-CCA同时具有HCC与CCA成分，肿瘤内异质性强，其行为可能类似HCC或CCA，准确诊断困难；而不同肝癌类型预后不同，因此亟需改进形态学表征与识别以准确判定cHCC-CCA。

Method: 采集HCC、CCA与cHCC-CCA三类HES染色全切片图像（WSI），基于WSI训练亚型分类模型，并探索在混合型肿瘤图像中定位不同肿瘤类型。

Result: 摘要未给出具体量化结果，仅指出所训练模型可区分三种肝脏肿瘤亚型，并具备定位混合型肿瘤中不同肿瘤成分的潜力。

Conclusion: 基于HES-WSI的计算分类方法有望辅助cHCC-CCA的形态学表征与精确识别，为应对其肿瘤内异质性提供可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Combined hepatocellular-cholangiocarcinomas (cHCC-CCA) are a type of cancer that share components of two other primary liver cancers: hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) and cholangiocarcinoma (CCA). Accurate diagnosis of cHCC-CCA, of which the behavior may resemble that of either HCC or CCA, remains a challenge due to strong intratumoral heterogeneity. Since the prognoses differ between liver cancer types, it is necessary to improve their morphological characterization and recognition in order to accurately identify cHCC-CCA. In this work, we use whole slide images (WSIs) of the two main types of liver cancer (HCC and CCA) and cHCC-CCA, stained with Hematoxylin-Eosin-Safran (HES), to train a classification model that can distinguish between these subtypes and provides a potential solution for localizing different types of tumors in the combined liver cancer images.

</details>


### [92] [RECAP: Looking Once Is Not Enough for Vision-Language Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30834)
*Zhaolu Kang,Tailong Luo,Chenxin Li,Yingjie He,Zhenyu Yu,Fengyu Zhou,Jiachen Qian,Lei Wei,Shuang Chen,Jiachen Li,Eric Hanchen Jiang,Rongchao Zhang,Zhengtao Yao,Hoi Leong Lee,Guansu Wang,Kaiyue Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文揭示VLM长链推理中的"时间性视觉锚定失败"现象，提出以revisit（推理过程中对图像证据的时序局部重激活）作为正确性预测与因果信号，并设计GRPO兼容的RECAP训练方法，将revisit轨迹转化为信用分配信号，在三个骨干模型上稳定超越GRPO与VPPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长链思维链提升视觉语言模型推理能力，但也带来时序性视觉锚定崩塌：生成过程中模型可能逐渐偏离图像证据，而强化学习只提供最终答案奖励，无法把奖励精确归因到真正依赖视觉证据的推理步骤。作者希望填补这一"最终奖励—中间视觉依赖"之间的信用分配鸿沟。

Method: 以"visual revisit"（推理过程中对图像证据的时序局部重激活）为研究对象。先做诊断与因果验证：三个VLM骨干上revisit峰值可预测答案正确性；遮蔽峰值比遮蔽非峰值更显著降低准确率；向失败 rollout 注入类revisit峰值可部分恢复正确答案；并用对照分析排除原始注意力强度、不确定性、logit margin、隐状态范数等解释。在此基础上提出RECAP：一种GRPO兼容训练法，把detached的revisit轨迹变为信用分配信号，包含revisit条件优势估计、动态折扣、门控价值预测，使最终奖励沿视觉关键步骤回传，且不引入额外奖励、额外 rollout 或推理期改动。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL-7B、InternVL3-8B、LLaVA-OV-7B上，RECAP一致优于GRPO与VPPO，相对SFT基线在HallusionBench上提升$+6.1$--$+6.5$，同时保持推理与纯文本性能。在更大LoRA微调模型、中文零样本基准、视觉扰动实验及人工评估上均显示图像锚定推理的稳健提升。

Conclusion: 推理过程中的视觉revisit是可解释、可因果验证的中间信号，可作为无需额外奖励的细粒度信用分配依据；RECAP为VLM长链推理提供了一种轻量且通用的强化学习改进范式，缓解时序视觉漂移。

Abstract: Long chain-of-thought reasoning improves vision-language models (VLMs), but it also exposes a temporal grounding failure: as generation unfolds, models may drift from image evidence while reinforcement learning still provides only a final-answer reward. We study this gap through visual revisit, a temporally localized reactivation of image evidence during reasoning. Across three VLM backbones, revisit peaks are predictive of correctness and causally linked to performance: masking them degrades accuracy more than masking non-peaks, while injecting revisit-like peaks into failed rollouts partially restores correct answers. Control analyses show that this signal is not explained by raw attention strength, uncertainty, logit margin, or hidden-state norm. We propose RECAP, a GRPO-compatible training method that turns detached revisit traces into a credit-assignment signal. RECAP uses revisit-conditioned advantage estimation, dynamic discounting, and gated value prediction to propagate final rewards through visually important reasoning steps, without extra rewards, additional rollouts, or inference-time changes. Across Qwen2.5-VL-7B, InternVL3-8B, and LLaVA-OV-7B, RECAP consistently improves over GRPO and VPPO, yielding $+6.1$--$+6.5$ HallusionBench gains over the SFT base while preserving reasoning and text-only performance. Results on larger LoRA-tuned models, Chinese zero-shot benchmarks, visual perturbations, and human evaluation further demonstrate robust improvements in image-grounded reasoning.

</details>


### [93] [Evaluation of Front-End Feature Matching for Lunar Visual SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30774)
*Guillem Casadesus Vila,Grace Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文在9个合成、月面试验台与地球类比数据集上基准评估了11种经典与学习型特征检测器-匹配器组合（另含一个特征跟踪器），以成对位姿精度和立体VO轨迹误差为指标，发现SuperPoint类组合在月面试验台上优于SIFT+FLANN，但合成数据结果不足以推广到真实月面影像；且只有SuperPoint类组合能在全部8个序列数据集上保持跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月面巡视器在导航卫星服务部署前主要依赖相机定位，之后仍需相机用于相对导航、障碍检测与表面作业。VO/SLAM多通过帧间特征匹配估计运动，但现有特征匹配器多在城市、室内、驾驶场景开发；行星研究多在轨道配准或完整SLAM系统中比较，且带参考位姿的真实月面影像稀缺。因此，低纹理、强光照对比地形下的巡视器帧间匹配及其对VO的影响尚未被系统比较。

Method: 构建9个数据集（合成、月面试验台、地球类比场地），基准测试11种经典与学习型检测器-匹配器组合，并加入一个特征跟踪器。评估两类指标：成对位姿精度（分别对基线与轨迹相对于两种度量——仅旋转度量、旋转与平移方向联合度量）以及由此产生的立体VO轨迹误差；同时记录跟踪连续性与每帧耗时。

Result: 在仅旋转度量下，SuperPoint组合在两个月面试验台和一个类比场地上优于采用比值检验FLANN匹配的SIFT，合成数据上差异较小或未定。在旋转与平移方向联合评分时，该SIFT组合在四个合成数据集上超过两种SuperPoint组合，说明仅凭合成结果无法确定真实巡视器影像上的相对性能。立体VO中SuperPoint组合并非始终更准，但唯有它们在全部8个序列数据集上保持跟踪。经典检测器组合依赖光照，而SuperPoint变化很小：同一试验台视角下低聚光灯照射比漫射光更准确，与更高对比度和更多检测关键点一致。SuperPoint+LightGlue是速度最快的学习型组合（每帧16 ms）；DINOv2检索提议仍需几何验证。

Conclusion: 合成数据不能替代真实月面巡视器影像的评测；实际部署中SuperPoint类组合在月面类地形上精度与跟踪鲁棒性更佳且对光照不敏感，是较稳妥选择，但其优势并非在所有度量与场景下一致，需结合具体任务度量进行选择。

Abstract: Lunar rovers will primarily rely on cameras for localization until navigation satellite services are deployed, and will continue to need them afterwards for relative navigation, hazard detection, and surface operations. Visual odometry (VO) and Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) mostly estimate motion by matching features between frames. However, feature matchers are developed on urban, indoor and driving scenes, planetary studies compare them on orbital registration or within complete SLAM systems, and real lunar imagery with reference poses is scarce, so matching between rover frames on low-texture, harshly lit terrain, and its effect on VO, has not been compared. We benchmark eleven classical and learned detector-matcher combinations on nine synthetic, lunar-testbed, and terrestrial-analogue datasets, and a feature tracker, scoring pairwise pose accuracy against baseline and the trajectory error of the resulting VO. Under a common rotation-only metric, the SuperPoint pairings outperform SIFT with ratio-tested FLANN matching on both lunar testbeds and one analogue site, with smaller or unresolved differences on synthetic data. When rotation and translation direction are scored jointly, this SIFT pairing exceeds both SuperPoint pairings on four synthetic datasets, so synthetic results alone do not establish relative performance on real rover imagery. In stereo VO, the SuperPoint pairings are not consistently more accurate than SIFT, but only they keep tracking on all eight sequential datasets. Pairings with classical detectors depend on lighting while SuperPoint changes little: a low spotlight makes them more accurate than diffuse light on the same testbed views, consistent with more contrast and detected keypoints. SuperPoint with LightGlue is the fastest learned combination (16 ms per frame), and DINOv2 retrieval proposals still need geometric verification.

</details>


### [94] [ParaLin++: Accelerating Parallel Diffusion Integrator via Intrinsic Partially Linear Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30704)
*Jianrong Lu,Zhiyu Zhu,Hui Liu,Junhui Hou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 传统 FID/CLIP 类指标下"近无损"的无训练少步采样器在语义组合、密集指令与长文本渲染基准上会显著掉点；并行采样因保留全步离散化而近无损，作者据此提出 ParaLin++，实现 1.21×–4.8× 加速且保持全步质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无训练少步采样器常被宣称在 FID/CLIP 类评估下几乎无损，但该结论是否适用于更真实复杂的生成质量基准尚不清楚，需要系统化审视评估指标与真实质量之间的错位。

Method: 首先系统梳理用于评估扩散/流匹配模型无训练采样加速的质量指标，并在 GenEval、DPG-Bench、LongTextBench 等语义组合、密集指令、长文本渲染基准上重新评测代表性方法；据"并行采样保留全步离散化、仅去除求解器时间依赖"的观察提出 ParaLin++——步数对齐的并行采样器，保持全步离散化同时并行加速求解器迭代。

Result: 代表性少步方法在 FID/CLIP 指标下近无损，但在语义组合、密集指令与长文本渲染基准上出现显著质量退化；并行采样为明显例外。ParaLin++ 在 Stable Diffusion-v2、LSUN Church 上实现 2.6×–4.8× 加速，FLUX.1-dev 上 1.68×，Ming-Flash-Omni 2.0 上 1.21×，并保持与全步 Euler 一致的质量画像。

Conclusion: FID/CLIP 类指标会掩盖少步采样的真实质量损失，评估应引入语义组合与文本渲染类基准；保持全步离散化的并行采样路径可在近无损前提下获得可观加速，是更可靠的加速范式。

Abstract: Training-free few-step samplers are often described as nearly quality-lossless under conventional FID/CLIP-style evaluation. It remains unclear whether that conclusion transfers to more realistic and complex generation-quality benchmarks. We first systematize the quality metrics used to evaluate training-free sampling acceleration for diffusion and flow-matching models.
  We find that representative training-free step-shortening methods often remain nearly lossless under FID/CLIP-class metrics, yet can suffer substantial quality degradation when evaluated with semantic-composition, dense-instruction, and long-text-rendering benchmarks such as GenEval, DPG-Bench, and LongTextBench. Parallel sampling is a notable exception: by retaining the full-step discretization and removing only the solver's temporal dependency, it can preserve near-lossless quality while accelerating sampling. Building on this observation, we propose \textit{ParaLin++}, a step-parity parallel sampler that preserves the full-step discretization while accelerating the solver iterations. Experiments across Stable Diffusion-v2, LSUN Church, FLUX.1-dev, and Ming-Flash-Omni 2.0 show that ParaLin++ preserves the full-step Euler quality profile at $2.6\times$--$4.8\times$, $1.68\times$ on FLUX, and $1.21\times$ on Ming. Project page: https://paralinplus.github.io/.

</details>


### [95] [HiP3R: Hierarchical Pose Graphs for Long-Horizon 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31635)
*Zunnan Xu,Shangchen Zhou,Yihang Luo,Chen Change Loy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiP3R 用分层位姿图解决长视频分块重建的全局一致性问题：先优化块级 Sim(3) 图确定全局布局与尺度，再固定该布局逐帧估计共享 SE(3) 相机，融合重叠窗口预测为单一轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈模型可准确估计短视频窗口的相机运动与三维几何，但算力与显存限制使其无法直接处理数千帧长序列，需将长视频切分为重叠块分别推理。分块导致各局部重建坐标系不一致、共享相机被重复估计，需借助相邻重叠与远距离回环传播约束。位姿图优化可满足该需求，但图层级存在权衡：块级图能高效消除尺度漂移与闭合大回环，却因刚性变换无法修正块内误差与帧级冲突预测；帧级优化局部灵活，但全局扩展代价高昂且易破坏度量尺度。

Method: 提出分层位姿图 HiP3R：第一阶段在块级构建鲁棒的 Sim(3) 位姿图优化，建立全局布局与尺度；第二阶段固定该全局布局，为每帧估计一个共享的 SE(3) 相机位姿，将各重叠窗口的预测融合为统一轨迹。方法可即插即用于基于分块与流式的后端，无需额外训练或微调。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，HiP3R 有效改善相机轨迹精度与三维重建质量。

Conclusion: 分层位姿图以"块级 Sim(3) 定布局与尺度、帧级 SE(3) 定共享相机"的方式化解图层级选择的权衡，实现长视频重建的全局一致性，且具备良好的后端通用性与零训练成本。

Abstract: Feed-forward models accurately estimate camera motion and 3D geometry from short video windows. However, computational and memory costs prevent direct inference on sequences containing thousands of frames. Long videos must therefore be processed as overlapping chunks. This division produces independent local reconstructions with inconsistent coordinate frames and duplicate estimates of shared cameras. Achieving global consistency requires propagating constraints from adjacent overlaps and distant revisits across the sequence. This requirement naturally motivates pose graph optimization. However, the choice of graph level creates a trade-off. Chunk-level graphs efficiently resolve scale drift and close large loops. Yet, their rigid transformations cannot correct internal errors or conflicting frame predictions. Conversely, frame-level optimization offers local flexibility, but scaling it globally is computationally expensive and can break metric scale. HiP3R resolves this trade-off using a hierarchical pose graph. HiP3R first optimizes a robust chunk-level $\mathrm{Sim}(3)$ graph to establish the global layout and scale. It then holds this layout fixed and estimates one shared $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ camera per frame, turning overlapping window predictions into a single trajectory. Experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of HiP3R in improving camera trajectories and 3D reconstruction. Our method applies to both chunk-based and streaming backends without additional training or fine-tuning. The codes are available at https://kkakkkka.github.io/HiP3R.

</details>


### [96] [AgentVQA: A Unified Benchmark for Agentic Visual Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31494)
*Advait Gupta,Sachin Konan,Tom Yin,Gabriel Sarch,Zhuang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 AgentVQA 离线基准，从 23 个源数据集人工筛选 1000 个高难度真实 agent 样例，覆盖 Web Agents、机器人、第一视角视频、游戏、空间规划五个领域，用于评估 VLM 的 agent 能力。最优模型仅 58.5% 准确率，且模型排名与在线评测高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 在 agentic 场景中的表现仍缺乏系统理解。在线模拟器/真实环境评测成本高、难以规模化；现有离线 VQA 基准零散且有限，无法可靠、可扩展地比较不同 agent 领域的模型能力。

Method: 构建离线基准 AgentVQA：从 23 个源数据集人工挑选 1000 个具有挑战性的真实 agent 样例，并改造为可稳定离线评测的格式，覆盖 Web Agents、Robotics、Egocentric Videos、Games、Spatial Planning 五个领域；评测 22 个模型并做跨域错误分析。

Result: 基准保留交互环境的真实场景特征，其模型排序与对应在线评测排序一致；22 个模型中最佳准确率仅 58.5%；跨域分析显示错误主要源于对当前及动态视觉状态的基础定位与表征维护失败（UI 元素定位、运动理解、时序状态跟踪、3D 空间关系推断），而非任务误解或下游逻辑推理。

Conclusion: AgentVQA 提供了可扩展、可复现的离线 agent 能力评测方案，可作为在线交互评测的互补代理；结果表明当前 VLM 的瓶颈在于视觉状态感知与跟踪等底层能力，而非高层推理。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can perform a broad range of tasks across diverse settings, yet their performance in agentic contexts remains poorly understood. Although online evaluations in simulators and real-world environments are essential for measuring interactive agent performance, they are costly and challenging to scale. Complementary offline VQA benchmarks can enable faster and more reliable comparisons across agentic domains, yet existing benchmarks remain limited and fragmented. To address this gap, we introduce AgentVQA, an offline benchmark for evaluating VLMs on realistic agentic scenarios across five agent domains: Web Agents, Robotics, Egocentric Videos, Games, and Spatial Planning. We build AgentVQA by manually selecting 1000 challenging, real-world agent examples from 23 source datasets and adapting them for reliable offline evaluation. The resulting benchmark preserves realistic scenarios from interactive environments across diverse domains, and its model rankings track those from corresponding online evaluations. Across the 22 evaluated models, the best achieves only 58.5% accuracy. Our cross-domain analysis shows that errors are driven primarily by failures to ground and maintain accurate representations of the current and evolving visual state, including localizing relevant UI elements, interpreting motion, tracking state changes over time, and inferring 3D spatial relationships, rather than by task misinterpretation or downstream logical reasoning.

</details>


### [97] [MixPT: Point Transformer with Interleaved Window and Global Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31455)
*Minkyun Seo,Hyungtae Lim,Jaesik Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MixPT是一种交替混合窗口注意力与全局注意力模块的点云Transformer，通过对空间池化后的token施加全局注意力并回传残差，在室内外基准上以更低时延取得有竞争力的精度，并可深度堆叠扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 窗口注意力计算高效但跨窗口信息交换依赖窗口归属变化与层次池化，全局注意力能直接建立场景级交互但token数量大时开销高。作者希望在大规模token场景下平衡计算成本与场景级交互能力。

Method: 提出MixPT：以交错调度方式组合窗口注意力块与全局注意力块，窗口块采用单一固定序列化模式；对token数较多的场景，先对token做空间池化再计算全局注意力，并将残差更新回传至原始分辨率流；并通过重复交错模式在深度上扩展注意力阶段。

Result: 在室内与室外点云基准上，MixPT取得与代表性窗口注意力及全局注意力基线相当（有竞争力）的精度，同时推理时延更低；验证了深度扩展注意力阶段的可行性。

Conclusion: 交错混合窗口与全局注意力、并在池化token上执行全局注意力，是兼顾场景级交互与计算效率的有效设计，且该结构具备良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Point cloud transformers aim to capture detailed local geometry and relationships across a scene at a manageable computational cost. Two common approaches are window attention and global attention. They differ in how they enable scene-wide interaction, which refers to information exchange among tokens across a scene. Window attention reduces computational cost by restricting self-attention to groups of tokens called windows; in serialized designs, each window contains a fixed number of consecutive tokens from a spatially ordered sequence. Because each block only directly connects tokens within the same window, information exchange across windows relies on changes in window membership across blocks and hierarchical pooling. Global attention, in contrast, directly connects all tokens within a scene, but computing attention over all token pairs can be costly when the number of tokens is large. To balance computational cost and scene-wide interaction, we introduce MixPT, a point transformer that uses a mix of window and global attention blocks in an interleaved schedule, while retaining a single fixed serialization pattern for its window blocks. For scenes with a large number of tokens, MixPT applies global attention to spatially pooled tokens and returns residual updates to the original-resolution stream. Across indoor and outdoor benchmarks, MixPT achieves competitive accuracy with lower latency than representative window and global attention baselines. We further demonstrate the feasibility of scaling the attention stage in depth by repeating the interleaved pattern of window and global attention blocks.

</details>


### [98] [A Unified Framework for Image-to-3D Part Generation via Variable Granularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31671)
*Qiwen Gu,Xin Zhou,Xiwu Chen,Zhongyang Zhu,Zhuo Zhang,Junqiao Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlexPart 是一个统一的图像到 3D 部件生成框架，通过门控自适应调制融合稀疏点、稠密包围盒与掩码等异质条件，在单一模型中支持可变粒度的几何控制，并在 PartVerseXL 掩码条件下取得更优的部件几何精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可控 3D 部件生成对数字资产创建至关重要，但现有方法缺乏灵活性，无法适配用户工作流中不同层次的控制需求（从粗粒度到细粒度）。作者希望用单一模型覆盖多种控制粒度，避免为每种条件类型单独建模，并利用不同粒度间的互补信息实现跨模态协同。

Method: 提出统一图像到 3D 部件生成框架 FlexPart：1）通过门控自适应调制（gated adaptive modulation）无缝集成稀疏点、稠密包围盒与掩码等异质输入；2）提出非对称几何引导机制（asymmetric geometric guidance），利用强空间先验在不同粒度间对齐表示，提升几何一致性并促进任务间协同。

Result: 在 PartVerseXL 的掩码条件下，FlexPart 的部件 Chamfer Distance 相较 TripoSG + SAM3D 降低 16.6%，相较 OmniPart 降低 10.5%，同时保持有竞争力的整体网格质量。

Conclusion: 可变粒度条件可在单一模型中有效统一，异质几何先验的跨粒度对齐能同时提升部件生成精度与整体一致性；FlexPart 为多样化用户工作流提供了灵活的可控 3D 部件生成方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Controllable 3D part generation is pivotal for digital asset creation, yet existing methods lack the flexibility to accommodate diverse user workflows requiring varying levels of control. In this paper, we introduce FlexPart, a unified image-to-3D part generation framework that supports variable-granularity geometric conditions within a single model. By seamlessly integrating heterogeneous inputs, ranging from sparse points to dense bounding boxes and masks, via gated adaptive modulation, FlexPart unlocks a powerful cross-modal synergy. We also propose an asymmetric geometric guidance mechanism that leverages strong spatial priors to align representations across granularities, improving geometric consistency and facilitating inter-task synergy. Under mask conditioning on PartVerseXL, FlexPart reduces Part CD by 16.6% relative to TripoSG + SAM3D and by 10.5% relative to OmniPart, while maintaining competitive whole-mesh quality. The code can be found at https://github.com/Gucci233/FlexPart

</details>


### [99] [Sensorimotor Alignment: A Platonic Proxy for Embodied Policy Success](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31670)
*Yunfei Xie,Howard H. Qian,Shiyi Lan,Kaiyu Hang,Chen Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Sensorimotor Alignment（S²），一种无需训练的离线指标，用感官表征与物理运动几何结构的一致性来低成本预测具身模型的下游任务表现，并将 Platonic Representation Hypothesis 拓展至具身模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础模型通常依赖昂贵的闭环 rollout 评估，亟需廉价的离线代理指标；同时 Platonic Representation Hypothesis（PRH）提示表征提升时不同模态会收敛到共享的统计结构，作者由此追问运动轨迹是否构成另一模态，使感官表征随能力提升向物理运动结构收敛。

Method: 提出 S² 指标：无需训练、无需真机部署，度量模型感官表征与物理运动几何结构之间的对齐程度；跨具身模型家族考察该指标与下游任务成功率的关联，并用于在昂贵微调前筛选 VLM 骨干。

Result: S² 在不同具身模型家族中与下游机器人任务成功率正相关，可作为闭环评估前的低成本筛选代理；还能在 VLM 微调为 VLA 之前识别有潜力的骨干；并能为 VLA 与 world action model（WAM）中的若干现象提供表征层面的解释。

Conclusion: S² 将 PRH 拓展到具身模型，使 sensorimotor alignment 可被实证测量，不仅低成本预测闭环成功率，还揭示具身模型成功背后的表征机制。

Abstract: Robotic foundation models are typically evaluated through costly closed-loop rollouts, which motivates cheap offline proxies for model capability. The Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) suggests that as representations improve, different modalities such as vision and language converge toward a shared statistical model of reality. We ask whether motion trajectories form another such modality: as embodied models become more capable, should their sensory representations converge toward the structure of physical motion? To examine this, we propose Sensorimotor Alignment ($S^2$), a training-free offline metric that measures how well a model's sensory representation aligns with the geometry of physical motion. We find that $S^2$ correlates positively with downstream robot task success across embodied model families, making it a low-cost offline proxy for prioritizing models before closed-loop evaluation. $S^2$ can also identify promising vision-language model (VLM) backbones even before costly fine-tuning into vision-language-action models (VLAs). Beyond this correlation, $S^2$ offers representation-level explanations for phenomena observed in VLAs and world action models (WAMs). With $S^2$, we extend PRH to embodied models and make sensorimotor alignment empirically measurable, offering insight into why an embodied model succeeds beyond its closed-loop success rate.

</details>


### [100] [WeVisOne-VL: Unified Visual Perception from Objects to Pixels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31329)
*Tianming Liang,Junyi Pan,Yuxi Li,Canmiao Fu,Zhipeng Huang,Chong Sun,Chen Li,Jing Lyu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WeVisOne-VL通过结构化下一词元预测统一目标级与像素级视觉感知，在30+基准上取得领先表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在细粒度视觉感知上仍不可靠，难以支持从点到掩码等多粒度定位。

Method: 在VLM中通过特殊<OBJ>词元接入轻量像素解码器以输出实例级像素结果；采用三阶段课程式SFT后接混合任务RL训练；支持检测、指代定位、计数、OCR、分割与抠图。

Result: 在30余个基准上于目标级与像素级任务均表现领先，并对目标缺失查询保持稳健拒答能力。

Conclusion: 该工作报告开源模型WeVisOne-VL，推动VLM从宽泛视觉理解走向可靠感知系统，服务真实部署。

Abstract: Existing vision-language models (VLMs) remain unreliable for fine-grained visual perception. In this report, we present WeVisOne-VL (WeChat Vision All-in-One), a powerful VLM that unifies object-level and pixel-level perception through structured next-token prediction. It supports multiple visual tasks including object detection, referring localization, counting, OCR, segmentation, and matting, enabling localization at any user-specific granularity, from points and boxes to masks and alpha mattes. Architecturally, we integrate a lightweight pixel decoder into the VLM via a special <OBJ> token to generate instance-level pixel outputs. We train the model with a three-stage curriculum SFT followed by mixed-task RL. Across over 30 benchmarks, WeVisOne-VL demonstrates leading performance on both object-level and pixel-level tasks, while maintaining robust rejection for object-absent queries. By releasing WeVisOne-VL to the open-source community, we hope to advance VLMs from broad visual understanding toward truly reliable perception systems for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [101] [Moving Regions, Not Moving Objects: Segmenting What Actually Moves under Camera Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31209)
*Wenhua Jin,Tiancheng Chen,Sheng Tang,Jiahao Li,Yupeng Liu,Weiqi Zhang,Juntong Fang,Zesong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出MoRSe，将运动区域分割建模为双帧联合推理问题，用单一RGB编码器直接预测"运动区域"掩码，区分真实场景运动与相机引起的表观运动，无需光流、点轨迹或独立掩码生成器，并发布MoRSe-5K基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动目标分割在序列/实例层面建模，只要实例在视频中任一处运动就整块标为运动；近期的瞬时（instantaneous）方法虽区分运动与静止区间，但仍依赖冻结的分割基础模型在实例级生成掩码，导致运动被归因于整个实例而非真正运动的局部区域；更根本的问题是，实例级预测在实例完全不动时仍会输出掩码。作者认为运动并非实例的固有属性，必须通过至少两帧比较才能判定。

Method: MoRSe联合解读两帧图像，预测运动区域掩码，从而将真实场景运动与相机运动引起的表观运动区分开。模型用单一RGB编码器替代多阶段基础模型堆叠，推理阶段不需要光流、点轨迹或单独的掩码生成器。同时构建MoRSe-5K基准，包含42个序列，设Static、Dynamic-Static、Dynamic三种设置，用于评估模型在不同相机与场景运动条件下区分并定位运动/静止区域的能力。

Result: MoRSe优于专门的运动目标分割方法，以及经过运动阈值化预测的稠密4D重建方法；在无任何运动的序列上假阳性最少。

Conclusion: 运动判定应基于帧间比较并在区域级定位，而非依赖实例级先验；单编码器双帧范式可在不引入额外几何/跟踪模块的前提下实现更精确的运动区域分割，并显著抑制静止场景下的误检。

Abstract: Moving object segmentation is conventionally formulated at the sequence and object level, marking an entire instance as moving once it moves anywhere in a video. Recent instantaneous formulations distinguish moving from stationary intervals, but still generate masks at the object level through frozen segmentation foundation models. Because the mask is derived from an instance-level proposal, motion is typically attributed to the entire instance rather than localized to the regions that actually move. Moreover, such instance-level predictions can produce masks even when no part of the instance is actually moving, revealing a more fundamental issue: motion is not an intrinsic property of an instance; it can only be determined by comparing at least two frames. Building on this premise, we introduce MoRSe, which jointly interprets two frames to predict a moving-region mask, distinguishing actual scene motion from apparent motion induced by the camera. A single RGB encoder replaces multi-stage foundation stacks, so no optical flow, point tracks, or separate mask generator is needed at inference. We release MoRSe-5K, a benchmark of 42 sequences with Static, Dynamic-Static, and Dynamic settings designed to evaluate the model's ability to distinguish and localize moving and stationary regions under different camera and scene motion conditions. MoRSe outperforms both specialized moving object segmentation methods and dense 4D reconstruction methods with motion-thresholded predictions, while producing the fewest false positives on sequences where nothing moves.

</details>


### [102] [CellTrack-Bench: Microscopic Cell Tracking Benchmark for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31566)
*Ilan Theodoro,Jordão Bragantini,Loïc A. Royer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 CellTrack-Bench，用于评估视觉语言模型（VLM）在细胞追踪任务上的表现，并与专用追踪算法对比；基准覆盖 4 种成像模态、15 个标准数据集，共 1,537 个问题、9,041 次细胞链接决策，并评测了 9 个闭源与 13 个开放权重前沿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型在代码、数学、写作等文本任务上表现优异，训练也逐步扩展到蛋白质结构、生物图像等高度专业化的非文本科学领域；但相较专用算法，其通用准确性仍存疑。细胞追踪需要时空动态感知，是检验这一问题的合适场景。

Method: 构建基准 CellTrack-Bench：整合 4 种成像模态下 15 个标准细胞追踪数据集，生成 1,537 个问题、对应 9,041 次细胞链接决策；通过识别多种不同算法追踪器共同出现的失败案例，筛选出需更强推理能力的困难子集；在此基础上（i）比较不同 VLM 环境脚手架（harness），（ii）对 9 个闭源与 13 个开放权重前沿模型进行基准测试，（iii）分析其失败模式。

Result: 给出了不同环境脚手架下 VLM 的追踪表现对比、22 个前沿模型的基准成绩，以及对失败模式的细粒度分析；同时开源代码、模型输出与可从源数据重建基准的流水线。

Conclusion: VLM 在专业细胞追踪任务上与专用算法仍存在差距，CellTrack-Bench 为系统评估与诊断其时空推理能力提供了标准化平台。

Abstract: Large language models have shown outstanding performance on text-first tasks such as coding, math, and writing. Recent efforts are shifting models' training to include scientific problems that often live in highly specialized domains beyond text, such as protein structure and biological images. However, in these scenarios, their general-purpose accuracy compared with specialized algorithms remains in question. One such domain is cell tracking, which requires spatiotemporal awareness of cell dynamics. To evaluate this, we created a new benchmark, CellTrack-Bench, which assesses Vision-Language Models' (VLMs) cell-tracking-related performance against specialized trackers. The full benchmark draws from 15 standard cell-tracking datasets across four imaging modalities and comprises 1,537 problems corresponding to 9,041 cell-linking decisions. We selected a hard subset requiring greater reasoning effort by identifying failures shared across distinct algorithmic trackers. With this proposed dataset, we: (i) evaluated different environment harnesses for VLMs; (ii) benchmarked 9 frontier closed-source models and 13 open-weight models; and (iii) provided a detailed analysis of their failure modes.
