However, these methods have been evaluated in isolation and only by their performance gains, leaving it unclear how they compare and whether they reliably improve performance. In this paper, we present an evaluation framework spanning five methods that differ in how informative the inserted content is and apply them at the sentence level across seven models spanning multiple sizes on three datasets. We find that accuracy gains are not always genuine improvements but undirected perturbations: the methods correct some predictions while breaking others, particularly when the inserted text allows only for more computational time. Our analysis reveals that methods that bring no additional content primarily change model predictions near the decision boundary. Our findings highlight the need to evaluate the reliability of the accuracy gains before adopting them in practice.

</details>


### [173] [Joint or Per-Option? Compact Encoders for Closed-Set Decisions from 17 Million to 1 Billion Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30970)
*Tardelli Ronan Coelho Stekel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 32M参数编码器即可胜任闭集决策任务；逐选项打分优于联合打分，但优势依赖reranker初始化，且需5–13倍编码器前向次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型直接提示完成工具选择等闭集决策既慢又对选项顺序敏感，作者探究此类决策所需编码器的最小规模，以及应联合读取选项还是逐一读取。

Method: 三随机种子、32M参数规模研究；在37个基准组成的决策套件上，于匹配优化步数与近似匹配训练算力两种条件下比较逐选项打分与联合打分；交叉"读取方式×初始化"消融；与六个已发布联合编码器（17M–1B）及435M DeBERTa-v3零样本分类器对比；使用209个重叠审计的System1-Arena任务及145个未见过任务；测量延迟、选项重排敏感度与排行榜表现。

Result: 逐选项打分在匹配步数下领先5.8个机会校正技能点、匹配算力下领先2.8点，两个外部基准方向一致但未见任务不显著；该优势仅在有reranker初始化时出现（原始编码器下两种读取方式无差异）；逐选项需5–13倍前向，中位延迟≤2倍、均值>4倍；68M与150M模型在209个任务上分别超DeBERTa-v3 4.9与5.9个准确率点，未见任务上至少持平；最小模型中位延迟14ms（16线程）对比728ms；重排选项使联合模型12–23%答案改变，逐选项为零；≤68M模型在其规模档位公开排行榜私有测试中领先。

Conclusion: 闭集决策可用极小编码器（约32M起）实现，且小模型在精度与延迟上均可超越大得多的零样本分类器；逐选项打分的增益本质来自查询–候选对输入与reranker初始化的匹配，并需以额外前向次数换取顺序不变性。

Abstract: Many language-model pipelines end in a closed-set decision, such as selecting a tool. Prompting a large language model for it is slow and order-sensitive. We ask how small an encoder for such decisions can be and whether it should read the options jointly or separately. In a three-seed study at 32 million parameters, per-option scoring beats joint scoring on a held-out sample of a 37-benchmark decision suite by 5.8 points of chance-corrected skill at matched optimizer steps and by 2.8 at approximately matched training compute; two external benchmarks agree, though not significantly on their unseen tasks. Crossing reader with initialisation shows that the advantage requires a reranker initialisation, whose query-candidate pair input matches per-option reading; from the raw encoder the two readers are indistinguishable. Per-option scoring needs 5 to 13 times more encoder passes: at most twice the median latency, but more than four times the mean. Of six released joint encoders (17 million to 1 billion parameters), the 68- and 150-million-parameter models exceed a 435-million-parameter DeBERTa-v3 zero-shot classifier by 4.9 and 5.9 accuracy points on 209 overlap-audited System1-Arena tasks and are at least on par with it on the 145 tasks unseen in training. The smallest answers a median request in 14 milliseconds on 16 processor threads, against 728 for the classifier. Reordering options changes 12 to 23% of the joint models' answers (per-option scoring: none). Up to 68 million parameters, they lead their size range on a public leaderboard's private, mainly in-distribution tests.

</details>


### [174] [Once a Tree, Always a Tree? Cross-lingual Transfer of Hierarchical Generalization in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30960)
*Shin Yamaguchi,Masaya Taniguchi,Tatsuki Kuribayashi,Taro Watanabe,Yoshinobu Kano*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文提出跨语言刺激贫乏（CPOS）实验范式，量化L1结构证据向L2层级泛化的可迁移性：LMs只能部分而非完全迁移层级结构证据，且迁移强度依赖语言对与词汇重叠度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续（预）训练可实现跨语言迁移，但其内在机制不明：究竟是LMs从源语言（L1）迁移了层级结构这类语言普遍性原则的证据，还是源于位置嵌入初始化改善等非语言学因素，尚缺乏可控的量化手段。

Method: 设计跨语言刺激贫乏（CPOS）实验：令L2语料在线性语法规则与层级语法规则之间保持歧义，同时控制L1侧层级规则的正向证据量，对比反事实的L1与L2学习场景；进一步基于词汇重叠影响显著的已有发现，对成功迁移的条件进行消融实验。

Result: LMs能够部分但非完美地将L1结构证据迁移至L2，仅产生微弱但可检测的层级偏置；迁移强度因语言对而异。消融实验表明，即使在层级句法泛化的跨语言迁移中，词汇重叠也起关键作用，并可解释主实验中语言对相关的差异。

Conclusion: 持续学习新语言过程中语言学证据的迁移是条件性可实现的：层级句法泛化的跨语言迁移受词汇重叠制约，并非无条件的普遍迁移。

Abstract: Language models (LMs) often exhibit successful cross-lingual transfer through continued (pre)training, still their underlying process remains unclear. One possibility is that LMs can leverage the evidence toward language-universal principles, namely, hierarchical structure, from a source language (L1) to a new language (L2), while many other non-linguistic explanations, e.g., improved initialization of position embeddings, can be potentially possible. In this study, to quantify the cross-linguistic transferability of linguistic evidence in cross-lingual LM training, we introduce a new experimental design, namely, a cross-lingual poverty-of-stimulus (CPOS) setting that ablates how LMs' hierarchical generalization in L2 can be led by structural evidence provided in L1. We contrast counterfactual L1 and L2 learning scenarios where the L2 corpus is ambiguous between linear and hierarchical grammar rules, while the amount of positive evidence for the hierarchical rule is controlled on the L1 side. Our experimental results show that LMs can partially, yet not perfectly, transfer L1 structural evidence to L2, yielding only a small but detectable hierarchical bias, while the strength of transfer depends on language pairs. We further ablate the conditions for successful transfer, motivated by recent reports on the substantial impact of vocabulary overlap. We indeed find that vocabulary overlap is crucial even for the cross-lingual transfer of hierarchical syntactic generalization. This suggests that this also explains the language-pair-dependent tendencies in the main experiments and suggests that the transfer of linguistic evidence in continued new-language learning is only conditionally achievable.

</details>


### [175] [Verbalizing Activation Differences with Natural Language Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30934)
*Andrea Blasi Núñez,Gianluca Barmina,Federico Torrielli,Thiusius Rajeeth Savarimuthu,Peter Schneider-Kamp,Lukas Galke Poech*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文系统评估自然语言自编码器（NLA）的泛化能力，围绕组合性、微调后复用、微调目标恢复与面向模型差异比较的专门化四个问题，跨3个模型族4个模型给出总体肯定的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活言语化器可将大模型内部表示转成可读文本，NLA通过自然语言瓶颈重建激活，但其在训练设定之外的泛化性尚不清楚。

Method: 提出四个紧密相关的研究问题：(i) 言语化是否对输入呈现组合结构；(ii) 是否泛化到原模型的轻量微调版本；(iii) 能否在模型差异比较中恢复微调目标；(iv) 能否被专门化用于模型差异比较。在3个模型族共4个模型上，用组合类比激活、Taboo模型生物与原始模型的激活差、以及基于激活差的强化学习等实验进行检验。

Result: 组合类比激活的言语化多能说出目标词而很少提及被减词；NLA无需重训即可用于原模型的轻量微调版本，仅最大模型重建质量明显下降；对Taboo模型生物与原始模型的激活差进行言语化可为多数生物恢复隐藏秘密词，基于激活差的强化学习进一步提升了留出生物上的恢复率；NLA无需微调模型生成任何token即可恢复微调目标，因为信息量最大的token位置位于用户提示之前；在涌现性失准检测任务上NLA尚不及最先进的模型差异比较方法，但优于黑盒基线。

Conclusion: NLA能够有意义地言语化激活空间中的差异，是值得后续研究的方向。

Abstract: Activation verbalizers translate the internal representations of large language models into human-readable text. The recently proposed natural language autoencoders (NLAs) learn this by reconstructing activations through a natural language bottleneck. However, it remains unclear how well these methods generalize beyond the setting they were trained for. Here, we study this question for NLAs along four closely related questions: (i) Do NLA verbalizations exhibit compositional structure with respect to their input? (ii) Do NLAs generalize to finetuned versions of the model they were trained for? (iii) Can NLAs recover finetuning objectives in model diffing? (iv) Can NLAs be specialized for model diffing? Across four models from three families, our experiments provide evidence supporting a largely positive answer to all four questions. Specifically, verbalizations of composed analogy activations mostly name the target while rarely mentioning the subtracted word, and an NLA can be reused on lightweight finetunes of its original model without retraining, with reconstruction quality degrading noticeably only for the largest model. Verbalizing activation differences between Taboo model organisms and their original models recovers the concealed secret word for most organisms, and reinforcement learning on activation differences further improves this recovery on held-out organisms. We also find that NLAs can recover the finetuning objective without the finetuned model generating any token, since the most informative token positions precede the user prompt. NLAs do not yet match state-of-the-art model diffing methods on the emergent misalignment detection task, but outperform a blackbox baseline. Our findings suggest that NLAs can meaningfully verbalize differences in activation space, rendering NLAs a promising direction for future work.

</details>


### [176] [Invisibilization as a Source of Probability Bias in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30785)
*Christian Arnold,Ernesto Calvo,Guadalupe Gonzalez,Sebastián Vallejo Vera,Tiago Ventura*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究测量大语言模型在被要求评估来自（随机化描述）报告者的不可验证信息时，因对齐导致的"拒绝评估"代价。领先模型常按描述而非证据来决定是否评估报告者，对"Black"描述的拒绝率最高达46%、对"white"为9%，该偏差对平均信任审计不可见、随推理力度增强、依赖字面措辞而非类别，并在警告指令下恶化；提出一种重复轮次下按揭示真值计分的选项可显著缩小信任差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型被对齐以避免用受保护属性（种族、族裔、性别）来影响回答，因而在提示中出现此类属性时倾向于拒答或含糊其辞。但若系统必须决定"从他人告知中学到多少"，仅因信息来源而拒绝有价值的信息是有代价的。本文旨在量化这种"拒绝信息"的代价，并揭示平均信任或仅看已评估样本的审计方法所无法察觉的偏差。

Method: 构建有精确贝叶斯解的双瓮（two-urn）博弈任务：模型需决定在多大程度上信任参与者提供的不可验证报告。将参与者描述随机化为十种配置，对OpenAI与Anthropic的领先模型进行共6,600次会话测试，比较模型"是否评估报告者"及评估时的信任水平；并检验推理力度、措辞翻译（如"Black"与西班牙语"negro"、"Indigenous"）、警告指令等因素的影响；另提出一种基于重复轮次揭示真值的计分选项。

Result: 模型常按描述而非证据决定是否评估报告者，将信息视为无价值的比率随描述变化：最宽情形下对Black描述拒绝率达46%、对white为9%；但一旦评估，Black描述报告者反而获得略高的信任。该偏差对比较平均信任或仅看已评估案例的审计不可见；偏差随推理力度增大；模型依据字面词汇而非社会类别（对"Black"触发种族考量，对西班牙语"negro"不触发，对"Indigenous"从不触发）；在实践者首选的警告指令下反而加剧。所提计分选项可降低拒绝率偏差：在Anthropic与OpenAI五种配置中的四种，末轮信任的种族差距降至0.02以下。

Conclusion: "拒绝信息"的成本是可测量且由描述驱动的，且常规审计手段难以发现此类选择性缺失偏差。模型表现出词汇层面而非类别层面的敏感性与随推理力度放大的偏差，且直觉性警告指令无效甚至有害。通过重复轮次下以揭示真值计分的评估设计（credit identical records equally），可在多数配置中近乎消除信任差距，为更稳健的评估与对齐实践提供方向。

Abstract: Language models are aligned to avoid using protected attributes such as race, ethnicity, and gender to inform their responses. Therefore, they increasingly refuse or hedge when such attributes appear in a prompt. However, if a system must decide how much to learn from what people tell it, it is costly to refuse valuable information because of its source. In this article, we measure the cost of declining information in a two-urn game with an exact Bayesian answer. We present leading LLM models from two developers (OpenAI and Anthropic) with a prompt that requires them to decide how far to trust an unverifiable report from a participant whose description we randomize across ten configurations (6,600 sessions). We show that leading models often decline to assess the reporter, treating information as worthless at rates set by the description rather than by the evidence. At its widest, models decline a Black-described reporter 46% of the time and a white-described reporter 9%; yet, when they do assess the information, the Black-described reporter receives slightly more trust. The bias is invisible to audits that compare average trust or look only at assessed cases. It grows with reasoning effort, follows words rather than categories (models invoke race for "Black" but not for its Spanish translation negro, and never for "Indigenous"), and worsens under warning instructions a practitioner would try first. We also present a scoring option that reduces the refusal rate bias. When a model's trust is scored against revealed truth over repeated rounds, it credits identical records equally. In four of five configurations from Anthropic and OpenAI, the racial gap in trust falls below 0.02 by the last rounds.

</details>


### [177] [UniGuard: Multi-View Guardrail Learning for Cross-Interface Safety Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30742)
*Viet-Thanh Pham,Thuy-Trang Vu,Junae Kim,Rollin Omari,Dinh Phung,Gholamreza Haffari,Lizhen Qu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 UniGuard 训练框架，通过同一安全案例的多种语义对齐视图学习接口不变的安全表征，并发布 UniGuard-FT 数据集与 UniGuard-0.6B 模型，在未见接口基准上显著提升 F1 并降低校准误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有护栏模型多基于固定的 prompt 或 response 接口训练，当同一安全案例以对话、智能体轨迹、多智能体 trace 或策略条件输入等形式出现时便不可靠，缺乏接口不变性。

Method: 从同一安全案例的多个语义对齐视图学习接口不变安全表征；构建 UniGuard-FT 数据集，将 1000 个公开安全案例转换为 prompt、response、多轮对话、智能体轨迹、多智能体轨迹五种视图；用分类损失、监督对比学习损失与非对称置信度校准损失训练 UniGuard-0.6B。

Result: 在未见接口基准 ATBench、PolicyGuardBench、DynaBench 上，UniGuard-0.6B 相较其预训练护栏平均提升 45.12 个 F1 点，期望校准误差降低 35.41 点，并与 Claude-Sonnet-4.5、GPT-5.2 等更大护栏保持竞争力，尽管仅为 0.6B 模型且仅用合成数据训练。

Conclusion: 多视图语义对齐的接口不变表征学习可有效提升护栏在未见接口下的泛化性与校准质量，小模型配合合成数据亦可达接近大模型的竞争力。

Abstract: Guardrail models are increasingly used as safety layers for LLMs and agentic systems, but most are trained on fixed prompt or response interfaces and become unreliable when the same safety case appears as a conversation, agent trajectory, multi-agent trace, or policy-conditioned input. We propose UniGuard, a training framework that learns interface-invariant safety representations from multiple semantically aligned views of the same safety case. We also construct the UniGuard-FT dataset by transforming 1,000 public safety cases into prompt, response, multi-turn conversation, agent-trace, and multi-agent-trace views, and train UniGuard-0.6B with classification, supervised contrastive learning, and asymmetric confidence calibration losses. Across unseen-interface benchmarks ATBench, PolicyGuardBench, and DynaBench, UniGuard-0.6B improves over its pretrained guardrail by 45.12 F1 points on average and reduces expected calibration error by 35.41 points, while remaining competitive with much larger guardrails such as Claude-Sonnet-4.5 and GPT-5.2 - despite being a 0.6B model and trained only with synthetic data.

</details>


### [178] [AQL-Lite: Enabling Efficient Knowledge Acquisition for Arabic LLMs via Data-Centric Continual Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30344)
*Houssam Eddine Boukhalfa,Mourad Bouache,Mourad Dahmane*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AQL-Lite：面向现代标准阿拉伯语（MSA）的高效适配框架，将高质量语料清洗流水线与部分参数持续预训练（RS-LoRA适配器+后8层Transformer块+嵌入矩阵，分层学习率）结合，以52%墙钟时间实现全参数CPT 94%的增益，并据此得到OALL v2得分75.3的12B模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练阿拉伯语大模型受困于网页噪声、方言变异以及持续预训练（CPT）成本高昂；现有方法难以在有限算力下高效完成多语言LLM向现代标准阿拉伯语（MSA）的迁移。

Method: 提出AQL-Lite框架，包含两部分：(1) 质量优先的数据策展流水线——结构清洗、启发式与模型过滤、MSA筛选、去重；(2) 部分参数CPT配方——在全网层训练RS-LoRA适配器，同时解冻最后8个Transformer块与嵌入矩阵，并采用分层学习率。实验控制变量：1B token等预算对比、5B token受控对比、组件消融、扩展至40B MSA token并做百万样本指令微调。

Result: 1B token等预算下，AQL清洗数据CPT使四基准阿拉伯语均值较原始网页文本CPT提升3.51分（后者相对基座无增益）；5B token受控对比中，AQL-Lite以52%墙钟时间恢复全参数CPT 94%的增益（均值73.19 vs 73.58），且在全部基准上优于仅适配器基线；消融显示适配器、解冻块、嵌入训练各自有贡献；扩展至40B token并指令微调后，12B模型在OALL v2七基准套件上得分75.3。

Conclusion: 在精心策展语料上进行部分参数CPT是高效实现MSA适配的可行路径：数据质量与参数高效微调的协同能在显著降低训练成本的同时接近全参数CPT效果。

Abstract: Training Arabic large language models faces challenges from noisy web data, dialectal variation, and the high cost of continual pretraining (CPT). We introduce AQL-Lite (AQL: Arabic Quality Language), a time- and resource-efficient framework for adapting a multilingual LLM to Modern Standard Arabic (MSA). AQL-Lite couples a quality-first curation pipeline (structural cleaning, heuristic and model-based filtering, MSA selection, and deduplication) with a partial-parameter CPT recipe that trains RS-LoRA adapters across the network together with the last eight transformer blocks and the embedding matrix, under layer-wise learning rates. At a matched 1B-token budget, CPT on AQL-curated data raises a four-benchmark Arabic mean by 3.51 points over CPT on raw web text, which yields no gain over the base model. In a controlled 5B-token comparison, AQL-Lite recovers 94% of the gain of full-parameter CPT (mean 73.19 vs. 73.58) in 52% of the wall-clock time and outperforms adapter-only baselines on every benchmark, and a component ablation shows that adapters, unfrozen blocks, and embedding training each contribute. Scaled to 40B MSA tokens and instruction-tuned on one million examples, the resulting 12B model scores 75.3 on the seven-benchmark Open Arabic LLM Leaderboard v2 (OALL v2) suite. These results suggest that partial-parameter CPT on a carefully curated corpus is a practical route to efficient MSA adaptation.

</details>


### [179] [SyRA-CS: Synthetic-to-Real Adaptation for Code-Switched ASR with Meta-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31415)
*Fadel Thior,Ahmer Tabassum,Haania Siddiqui,Hanan Aldarmaki,Thamar Solorio*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 SyRA-CS：基于 Reptile 元学习的“合成→真实”成对适配框架，用合成 CS-FLEURS 元训练再以 1 小时真实标注语音适配，在 16 组模型-语对比较中 WER 15/16 以上优于持续微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实代码切换（CS）语音稀缺，合成 CS 语音与真实分布不完全匹配；作者探究合成 CS 语音的真正价值在于“学习一个利于适配的初始化”，而非普通持续微调。

Method: 对每个语对与预训练模型，先在对应合成 CS-FLEURS 子集上进行 Reptile 元训练，再用 1 小时带标签真实目标语音适配；覆盖四种架构：Encoder-CTC、FastConformer-RNN-T、AED、Speech-LLM；对比合成迁移、纯真实微调与持续微调。

Result: 实验涵盖阿拉伯语-、法语-、德语-、印地语-英语；SyRA-CS 在全部 16 组模型-语对比较中 WER 均优于持续微调，CER 为 15/16；宏观相对 WER 降幅：XLS-R 17.7%，其余三模型 5.3–5.8%；增益随架构、语对与指标而变化。

Conclusion: 合成 CS 语音作为元学习初始化来源比作为直接微调数据更有效；方法具跨架构普适性但增益幅度不均，需按架构/语对具体分析。

Abstract: Code-switched (CS) automatic speech recognition (ASR) remains difficult because real CS speech is scarce and synthetic speech does not fully match it. We investigate whether synthetic CS speech is more useful for learning an adaptation-friendly initialization than for ordinary continual fine-tuning. We propose SyRA-CS, a pair-specific synthetic-to-real adaptation framework based on Reptile meta-learning. For each language pair and pretrained model, we meta-train on the corresponding synthetic CS-FLEURS subset and then adapt using one hour of labeled real target speech. We evaluate four architectural settings: Encoder-CTC, FastConformer-RNN-T, Attention Encoder-Decoder (AED) and Speech-LLM. Experiments cover on Arabic-, French-, German-, and Hindi-English, against synthetic-only transfer, real-only fine-tuning, and continual fine-tuning. SyRA-CS improves over continual fine-tuning on WER in all 16 model-pair comparisons and on CER in 15 of 16, with relative macro WER reductions of 17.7% for XLS-R and 5.3-5.8% for the other three models. The gains hold across the tested models but vary in size across architectures, language pairs and metrics.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [180] [When Stable and Preferred Semantics Agree: A Path-Based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30176)
*Yan Zhang,Kehan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文以攻击图中的路径集合及其诱导的二元关系为工具，给出优选语义与稳定语义一致（agreement）的通用路径判据，并证明其严格推广了 Dung 的 limited controversiality 与 SCC-symmetry 两大经典结构判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抽象论辩框架中优选语义与稳定语义何时一致是关键问题，已有结构判据（Dung 的 limited controversiality、SCC-symmetry）彼此独立且覆盖有限，缺乏统一刻画框架。

Method: 选取攻击图上适当的路径集合，构造其诱导的二元关系，建立语义一致的通用路径判据；以多类路径集合实例化，把条件表述为相应关系的良基性（well-foundedness）。取偶路径集合可还原 Dung 结果；引入两个无限路径集序列，其一由 Collapse 定理将无穷多良基条件约化为三个本质不同条件，其二产生无此类坍缩的真正互异条件。

Result: 所得条件严格推广 limited controversiality 与 SCC-symmetry；Dung 定理作为偶路径特例被回收；Collapse 定理给出第一序列的三条件归约，第二序列无对应坍缩；结果同时适用于有限与无限框架，有限情形有简化表述，并给出稳定扩展存在的充分条件。

Conclusion: 路径—关系—良基性框架统一并强化了优选与稳定语义一致的结构理论，兼具对无限框架的适用性与稳定扩展存在性判据的副产品。

Abstract: We investigate structural conditions under which preferred and stable semantics agree in abstract argumentation frameworks. Our approach is based on suitably chosen sets of paths in the attack graph and binary relations induced by them. We establish a general path-based criterion for semantic agreement and instantiate it with several classes of paths, obtaining sufficient conditions expressed in terms of well-foundedness of the corresponding relations.
  The resulting conditions strictly generalize two principal structural criteria for preferred and stable agreement in the literature: Dung's limited controversiality condition and the SCC-symmetry condition. In particular, Dung's result is recovered as a special case by taking the relevant path set to be the set of even paths. We further introduce two infinite sequences of path sets, each yielding a family of agreement conditions. For the first sequence, a Collapse Theorem reduces the infinitely many associated well-foundedness conditions to three essentially distinct ones, whereas the second sequence gives rise to genuinely distinct conditions with no analogous collapse. Our results apply to both finite and infinite argumentation frameworks, admit simplified formulations in the finite case, and also yield sufficient conditions for the existence of stable extensions.

</details>


### [181] [TwinStore: A Synthetic Consumer Platform Using Vision--Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30909)
*Minhyeok Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TwinStore：基于视觉-语言模型的物理零售合成消费者平台，将门店数字化为孪生，用合成购物者试跑布局/价格/包装/促销并输出小票。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售决策（布局、定价、包装、促销）在真实门店试错成本高、周期长，缺乏可复现、可控的消费者测试环境。

Method: 以视觉-语言模型为核心：支持82种现成门店格式、平面图或货架手机照片构建数字孪生；合成购物者规划行程、逛通道、依据带包装/价签/促销牌的渲染货架做购买决策，每次行程生成小票；配套3D回放、配对比较、布局优化、购物者访谈、商圈模型、真实货架照片反事实编辑、含11个工具的营销工作室及自然语言助手；支持云/端侧模型，提供三平台安装包。

Result: 在11个公开数据集的留出验证中：模拟小票接近家庭自身自然波动（品类结构散度0.017–0.018，真实漂移0.012）；价格弹性落在实地测量区间内；三种独立决策模型结论一致。

Conclusion: TwinStore提供了可实操的合成消费者实验平台，具备一定外部效度证据，可用于门店决策前的低成本预演。

Abstract: We present TwinStore, a source-available synthetic consumer platform for physical retail built on vision--language models. TwinStore turns a store into a digital twin---from 82 ready-made formats, a floor-plan image or phone photos of its shelves---and fills it with synthetic shoppers who plan a trip, walk the aisles and decide what to buy by looking at rendered shelves with packs, price tags and promotion signs. Every trip ends in a receipt, so a layout, price, pack or promotion can be tried on the same shoppers before it reaches the store. Around this core, TwinStore provides 3D playback, paired comparisons and layout optimization, shopper interviews, a trade-area model, counterfactual edits of real shelf photos, a marketing studio with eleven tools, and an assistant that turns a plain sentence into a drafted study or an answer grounded in a run's own numbers. In a held-out validation on eleven public datasets, simulated receipts approach the households' own natural variation (category-mix divergence 0.017--0.018 against a real drift of 0.012), price elasticities fall inside the range measured in the field, and three independent decision models agree. TwinStore runs on cloud or on-device models and is distributed with installers for Windows, macOS and Linux.

</details>


### [182] [An Introduction to World Models for Generalist Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30789)
*Zhihui Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 从POMDP框架出发，对"世界模型"作统一概念澄清，梳理其与经典控制、动力系统、信号处理及强化学习的联系，分类总结建模方法（所捕捉知识、学习目标、支撑决策的方式），并指出世界建模与策略学习可通过条件预测统一，无需分离模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型被视为通向通用智能体的重要方向，但概念界定松散，涵盖预测动力学模型、学习型模拟器以及联合生成未来观测与动作的模型等差异很大的路线，缺乏统一视角，亟需澄清基本概念与建模原则。

Method: 以部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）为统一理论起点，将世界建模与经典控制、动力系统、信号处理、强化学习建立联系；按"所捕捉知识—学习目标—如何支撑决策"三个维度对世界建模方法进行分类阐述；并分析世界建模与策略学习的关系，论证二者可通过条件预测统一。

Result: 给出世界模型的概念澄清与统一视角，形成方法分类框架，并表明世界模型与策略可在同一条件预测目标下联合学习，无需单独维护两套模型；同时明确指出本文为概念性导论而非穷尽式综述。

Conclusion: 世界模型可作为通用智能体的统一建模范式，其核心是面向决策的条件预测；后续研究应关注概念统一、建模原则与跨领域联系，而非方法罗列。

Abstract: World models have recently emerged as a promising direction toward developing generalist agents capable of understanding, predicting, and interacting with diverse environments. However, despite growing interest, the concept of a world model remains loosely defined, encompassing approaches ranging from predictive dynamics models and learned simulators to models that jointly generate future observations and actions. This introductory paper aims to clarify the concept of world models and provide a unified perspective on their role in developing generalist agents. Starting from the partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP) framework, we connect world modeling to classical control, dynamical systems, signal processing, and reinforcement learning. We introduce different approaches to world modeling, emphasizing the knowledge they capture, their learning objectives, and how they support decision-making. We further explore the relationship between world modeling and policy learning, highlighting how these capabilities can be unified through conditional prediction without requiring separate models. Rather than offering an exhaustive survey of existing methods, this paper focuses on fundamental concepts, modeling principles, and connections, with the goal of providing an accessible and unified introduction to world models for generalist agents.

</details>


### [183] [Can Loan Processors and Underwriters Spot AI-Edited Statements? An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30758)
*Simiao Ren,Xingyu Shen,Tommy Duong,Ankit Raj*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究考察有经验的核保人与贷款处理人在首轮仅看文档的审查中能否识别AI辅助编辑生成的伪造贷款文件。三名从业者共给出132份文件级判断，其中仅14份（10.6%）有编辑证据支撑，且均为标识符不一致，无任何视觉线索指向真实编辑。研究为探索性，缺真文件对照，不能外推总体检出率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助编辑可在保持发行人熟悉版式的同时替换申请人身份，形成难以察觉的贷款文件伪造，威胁贷款审核流程。作者希望检验：经验丰富的从业者在首轮、仅依赖文档的审查中能否发现此类伪造。

Method: 从经完整授权提供的47份真实财务文件派生出134份伪造PDF（保留金额、日期、余额，管线引入的标识符不一致被有意保留）；经Upwork招募3名从业者，每人审阅一个申请人文件包，未被告知文件被篡改，共返回132份文件级判断；将判断理由与原件逐一比对，判定是否存在编辑证据支撑。

Result: 共14份判断含编辑证据（10.6%）：12份“可疑”判断、2份“信息不足”判断。所有获支持的疑虑均涉及文档内部或跨文档的标识符不一致；无任何被报告的视觉线索指向真实编辑。即约每十份判断中仅一份含伪造证据，且残留不一致已属“已知”情形。

Conclusion: 人类单次文档审查对AI辅助伪造的检出能力有限。研究主张在人工审查之外并行开展一致性核查与独立来源验证。受限于仅3组审阅者-文件包配对且无真文件对照，结论仅描述该探索性场景，不代表总体检出准确率。

Abstract: AI-assisted editing can produce loan documents that preserve familiar issuer layouts while changing the applicant's identity. We investigated whether experienced underwriters and loan processors identify such forgeries in first-pass, document-only review. Three practitioners recruited through Upwork each received one applicant package drawn from 134 forged PDFs derived from 47 genuine financial documents supplied with full consent. The edits preserved amounts, dates and balances; identifier inconsistencies introduced by the pipeline were knowingly retained before distribution. Reviewers were not told that files had been altered and returned 132 file-level judgements. Checking their explanations against the originals yielded 14 edit-supported concerns (10.6%): 12 Suspicious judgements and two Insufficient information judgements. All supported concerns involved identifier disagreements within or across documents, and no reported visual cue identified an actual edit. Thus, approximately one in ten judgements contained evidence of the forgery, despite known inconsistencies remaining in the files. These observations motivate evaluating consistency checks and independent source verification alongside human review. With three reviewer-package pairs and no genuine-file controls, the findings describe this exploratory setting rather than population-wide detection accuracy.

</details>


### [184] [How Firm Are AI Models' Ethical Boundaries? A Pay-Stub Editing Test of 15 Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30751)
*Simiao Ren,Xingyu Shen,Tommy Duong,Tsang Ng,Ankit Raj*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过改变措辞（明示贷款用途、省略用途、描述为修正打印错误）测试15个模型对同一"提高工资单收入"请求的遵从率，观测到的编辑调用比例分别为14.1%、33.1%和66.2%，说明边界行为对措辞高度敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI模型的安全边界是否仅由请求的实质意图决定，还是会被表面措辞的细微改动所绕过；即"拒绝"与"执行"之间的转换能否通过改变叙述框架实现。

Method: 对同一敏感请求（提高工资单金额）设计三种措辞变体——声明贷款用途、省略用途、称为修正打印错误——在15个模型上共进行445次可观测试验，统计模型发出的编辑工具调用比例，并摘录回应片段以展示拒绝、转介与为编辑辩护的不同方式；实验使用带有标记的测试文档且所有工具均为模拟环境。

Result: 编辑调用率随措辞变化显著：明示贷款用途14.1%、省略用途33.1%、称为修正打印错误66.2%。模型间差异明显：Astra在全部十次贷款用途请求中均拒绝，但在十次省略用途试验中全部发出调用；Fable、Opus、Muse Spark在三种措辞下均未发出调用；Sonnet在后两种措辞下部分遵从。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了单一设定下模型边界行为的脆弱性与不一致性，措辞框架可显著影响合规决策；但该实验仅为特定场景的探针，不能作为整体伦理排名的依据，也不代表真实世界中的有害编辑已成功实施。

Abstract: Can a small change in wording move an AI model from refusing a sensitive request to acting on it? We test 15 models on the same request to increase the income shown on a pay stub. Across 445 observable trials, models issue editing calls in 14.1% of cases when a loan purpose is stated, 33.1% when the purpose is omitted, and 66.2% when the change is described as fixing a printing error. Astra refuses all ten loan-purpose requests but issues calls in all ten purpose-omitted trials. Fable, Opus, and Muse Spark issue none across all three wordings; Sonnet issues some under the latter two. Response excerpts illustrate different ways of refusing, redirecting, or justifying an edit. The file is a marked test document and all tools are simulated. These results probe how boundaries behave in one setting; they do not prove an overall ethics ranking or that a harmful real-world edit succeeded.

</details>


### [185] [Can AI Co-Design Structures? An AI Agent for Early-Stage Structural Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31113)
*Ricardo Maia Avelino,Mennatallah El-Assady,Tom Van Mele,Philippe Block*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于AI智能体与MCP服务器的"提示词到结构"生成工作流，将自然语言设计意图转化为可编辑的图状结构数据模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型与智能体工作流为早期结构设计提供了将设计意图转化为结构模型的新范式。现有text-to-3D方法虽能生成视觉化网格几何模型，但结构设计需要领域特定的几何布置与语义丰富的数据类型，网格模型无法满足下游AEC工作流需求。

Method: 构建概念验证工作流：AI智能体通过模型上下文协议（MCP）服务器连接至基于COMPAS的结构建模API，在受控建模操作下创建并修改由显式建筑构件组成的图状结构数据模型。

Result: 通过若干提示词到结构的示例以及模拟典型迭代式结构设计的渐进、可编辑设计序列进行演示。生成产物可检查、修改、序列化，并可接入下游AEC工作流。结果属初步性质。

Conclusion: 结果初步表明，将自然语言、智能体工作流与领域特定结构数据结构相结合具有可行性，是迈向AI辅助结构协同设计的第一步。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models and agentic workflows offer a new paradigm to early-stage structural design translating design intent into structural models. Although text-to-3D approaches have demonstrated that natural language can be used to produce visual, mesh-based geometric models, structural design requires domain-specific geometric arrangements and semantically rich data types. This paper introduces a proof-of-concept workflow for prompt-to-structure generation using an AI agent connected to a COMPAS-based structural modelling API through a Model Context Protocol (MCP) server. Through controlled modelling operations, the agent creates and modifies a graph-based structural data model composed of explicit building elements. The resulting artifacts can be inspected, modified, serialised, and further connected to downstream AEC workflows. The approach is demonstrated through selected prompt-to-structure examples and by progressive and editable design sequences that resemble typical iterative structural design workflows. The results are preliminary, but demonstrate the possibility of combining natural language, agentic workflows, and domain-specific structural data structures as a first step toward AI-assisted structural co-design.

</details>


### [186] [Evaluating Artificial Wisdom (AW): A Trajectory-Based Framework for Context-Sensitive Decision Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31012)
*Md. Jalil Piran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出面向"人工智慧"的轨迹式测量框架与前瞻验证协议，以"情境敏感的判断调节"为行为定义，通过版本化参考规范与九维剖面评估模型判断的稳定性与更新充分性，并用三项简化模拟做初步检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有能力基准只衡量大模型"能做什么"，却无法判断当决策条件变化时其建议是否仍然恰当，缺少对判断随情境变化而适度调节的测量工具。

Method: (1) 轨迹式测量框架：用版本化参考规范预先界定可接受响应与转换实质性；(2) 七项阶段级准则评估当前条件下的判断，两项转换成分评估实质性更新充分性与承诺保持稳定性，稳定性以先前充分性为前提；(3) 以九成分剖面为主指标，辅以瓶颈分与关键失败报告；(4) 三项简化模拟分别考察精度、瓶颈估计与对模型家族数量的敏感性；另有一项含71条记录的探索性前身量表研究。

Result: 单准则模拟在每领域24个情境下达到精度标准；瓶颈接近并列时，该样本量下最小值的估计偏低约0.55分；增量预测模拟显示，八个与十二个家族时检出Brier改进0.027的概率分别为.508与.742，但该结果不足以确立完整协议的统计功效；前身量表研究显示聚合方式敏感性，但不构成对新工具效度的验证。

Conclusion: 十二家族目标仍属暂定，需完成可行性评估与完整分析的校准；该剖面能否在能力之外独立预测决策结果，仍是待检验的实证问题。

Abstract: Capability benchmarks measure what large language models can do, but not whether their recommendations remain appropriate as decision conditions change. This paper proposes a trajectory-based measurement framework and prospective validation protocol for artificial wisdom, defined behaviorally as context-sensitive regulation of judgment. Versioned reference specifications determine admissible responses and transition materiality before evaluation. Seven stage-level criteria assess judgment under current conditions, while two transition components assess material-update adequacy and commitment-preserving stability. Stability requires prior adequacy; switching within registered commitment classes is permitted, and persistent error earns no stability credit. A nine-component profile is primary, with a secondary bottleneck score and separate critical-failure reporting. Three simplified simulations examine precision, bottleneck estimation, and sensitivity to model-family count. A single-criterion simulation met its precision criteria at 24 scenarios per domain. Near a bottleneck tie, the estimated minimum had a downward bias of 0.55 points at that size. A simplified incremental-prediction simulation detected a Brier improvement of 0.027 with probabilities of .508 and .742 using eight and twelve families, respectively; it does not establish power for the operational protocol. An exploratory predecessor-rubric study comprising 71 evaluation records illustrates aggregation sensitivity but does not validate the proposed instrument. The twelve-family target remains provisional pending feasibility assessment and calibration of the complete analysis. Whether the profile predicts independent decision outcomes beyond capability remains an open empirical question.

</details>


### [187] [Disentangling Robustness and Sycophancy in Multilingual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30696)
*Rhitabrat Pokharel,Ameeta Agrawal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SC-matrix，将多语言 LLM 的能力（无压力下是否正确作答）与谄媚性（压力下是否维持原答案）交叉，区分"谄媚"与"语言能力不足"两类失败，并揭示多语言谄媚是多成分复合现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多语言谄媚研究只记录模型是否认同用户错误前提，而不区分原因：究竟是模型具备正确知识却被压力改变（真谄媚），还是在特定语言下本就缺乏能力（知识缺口）。二者混为一谈会导致误诊与错误缓解。

Method: 构建 SC-matrix，以"无压力下正确率"衡量能力、以"压力下维持原答案的比例"衡量谄媚性，将样本划入四种象限（如高能力高谄媚、低能力低谄媚等），从而对相同聚合谄媚率下的失败构成进行分解；并比较推理导向与指令导向两类缓解策略。

Result: (1) 相同的聚合谄媚率可能掩盖不同的失败构成；(2) 某语言上的高能力并不总能防止谄媚；(3) 推理导向的缓解方法优于指令式策略。

Conclusion: 多语言谄媚并非单一现象，而是由不同失败类型构成的复合体，需要分别诊断；SC-matrix 为细粒度评估与针对性干预提供了分析框架。

Abstract: When a multilingual LLM agrees with a user's false premise, is it being sycophantic, or does it simply not know better in that language? Current research does not distinguish these cases: they record only whether the model agreed, not why. We introduce the SC-matrix, that crosses a model's competence (whether it answers correctly under no pressure) with its sycophancy (whether it maintains that answer under pressure). Our results show that (1) identical aggregate sycophancy rates can mask different failure compositions, (2) high competence in a language does not always protect against sycophancy, and (3) reasoning-oriented mitigations outperform directive-based strategies. We find that multilingual sycophancy is not a single phenomenon but a composite of different failures that require distinct diagnosis.

</details>


### [188] [Group Relative Policy Optimisation Improves Multilingual Speech Recognition in Low-Resource Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30663)
*Tim Wenjie Hu,Evelyn Nafula Ouma,Benjamin Akera,John A. Quinn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将GRPO强化学习扩展到小型非LLM编码器-解码器ASR模型，以Whisper-large-v3为例覆盖51种非洲语言，在数据稀缺和噪声条件下收益显著，SunflowerASR在AfriVox-v2和SimbaBench上取得最优宏平均WER。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教师强制训练的编码器-解码器ASR模型存在暴露偏差（exposure bias），推理时难以从自身预测错误中恢复；已有GRPO强化学习工作仅验证于大语言模型架构和主要语言，尚不清楚其能否迁移到小型非LLM模型及低资源、多语言场景。

Method: 以Whisper-large-v3为案例，对小型非LLM编码器-解码器ASR模型施加基于GRPO的强化学习后训练，覆盖51种非洲语言，并针对低资源与含噪/退化音频条件进行评估。

Result: RL收益在训练数据稀缺或输入音频含噪、退化时最为显著；所得模型SunflowerASR在AfriVox-v2上宏平均WER为0.227、在SimbaBench上为0.372，均为已发表最优结果，且模型已公开。

Conclusion: GRPO式强化学习可有效缓解小型非LLM ASR模型的暴露偏差，是提升低资源多语言语音识别鲁棒性的有效手段。

Abstract: Autoregressive encoder-decoder speech recognition models trained with teacher forcing can suffer from exposure bias, limiting their ability to recover from their own prediction errors at inference time. Recent work has shown that reinforcement learning with Group Relative Policy Optimisation (GRPO) improves speech recognition in major languages with LLM-based models. We extend this line of work to smaller, non-LLM encoder-decoder ASR models, using Whisper-large-v3 as a case study across 51 African languages. The benefits of this approach are most pronounced when training data is scarce or when input audio is noisy or degraded. Our resulting model, SunflowerASR, achieves the best published macro-averaged WER on the AfriVox-v2 (0.227) and SimbaBench (0.372) benchmarks, and is publicly available.

</details>


### [189] [Anticipating Social Consequences with Distributional World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30657)
*Viet-Thanh Pham,Thuy-Trang Vu,Zhuang Li,Lizhen Qu,Gholamreza Haffari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出分布社会世界模型DSWM，一次前向输出候选动作的多种备选响应、其概率及即时与最终结果价值，以4B模型经重复模拟的SFT与GRPO训练，在1成功用多未来分布预测提升交互决策，并在13/15指标上取得最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会交互中一个动作可导致多个合理未来，仅预测单一未来会忽略重要备选；而通过重复模拟逐一探索又会增加推理成本。因此需要以单次推理同时刻画多种可能未来及其概率。

Method: DSWM（Distributional Social World Model）对每个候选动作一次性输出多条备选响应、各自概率以及即时和最终结果价值；在重复模拟数据上以监督微调结合GRPO训练4B规模模型；推理时actor依据这些可能后果选择动作。并通过消融检验保留多未来与预测概率的作用。

Result: 在3个已见、2个未见环境（交互骨干未出现在训练收集中）上，DSWM在15项指标中的13项取得最佳平均性能；消融显示保留多个未来及其预测概率在全部5个环境中均提升结果。

Conclusion: 以分布化方式在单次输出中建模多未来及概率，可在不增加重复模拟推理开销的前提下提升社会交互决策质量，并具备跨环境与跨骨干的泛化性。

Abstract: Social world models predict how interactions develop after an action, helping agents negotiate, cooperate and mediate. In these situations, one action can lead to several plausible futures, hence predicting only one future can overlook consequential alternatives, while exploring them through repeated simulations increases inference cost. We propose DSWM, a Distributional Social World Model that predicts alternative responses, their probabilities and immediate and final outcome values in one output per candidate action. We train a 4B model on repeated simulations using supervised fine-tuning and GRPO. At inference time, the actor uses these possible consequences to choose an action. Across three seen and two unseen environments, with interaction backbones absent from training collection, DSWM achieves the best average performance on 13 of 15 metrics. Ablations show that retaining multiple futures and their predicted probabilities improves outcomes in all five environments.

</details>


### [190] [Speculative Acceptance Does Not Decay with Position: A Full-Length Diagnostic on Long Chain-of-Thought with Independent Drafts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30644)
*Zhiming Meng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文首次在MATH-500全量问题上长程测量投机解码接受率随生成位置的变化，发现隔离贪心重复病态后接受率并不随位置衰减，残余失败定位于决策点熵而非位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "接受率随思维链变长而衰减"已成为业界默认叙事，推动了草稿模型协同设计与位置感知训练。但几乎所有直接证据都来自离线训练长度远短于推理长度的已训练草稿模型，纯粹的位置规律从未被单独隔离出来验证。

Method: 在MATH-500全部500题上做全长度系统测量，覆盖贪心与采样两种解码，对DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B配两个独立草稿：同族DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B与跨族Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct，共约178万验证轮；采用问题内配对检验；另做受控温度扫描与轻量三臂LoRA轨迹对齐干预（均匀、反思标记、熵加权）。

Result: 四种配置（贪心0-8K、采样0-12K）在支持范围内接受率均不随位置衰减，每条轨迹后半段略高但显著；采样模式下高熵反思token的"退化"是argmax一致性的可见性伪影，而非期望接受概率下降；拒绝高度精确地集中在"wait""however""check"等反思触发token上，属熵选择性失效；三臂LoRA干预均未能提升平均接受长度。

Conclusion: 论文提供了此前独立草稿-目标对缺失的位置基线，并将残余失败从"位置"归因到"决策点熵"，说明接受率衰减叙事在纯位置维度上不成立。

Abstract: The claim that speculative-decoding acceptance rate decays as a chain-of-thought grows longer has become a de facto industry narrative, motivating draft-model co-design and position-aware training. Nearly all direct evidence comes from trained drafters whose offline training length is far shorter than inference, so a purely positional law has never been isolated. We present the first systematic, full-length measurement of acceptance rate as a function of generation position on MATH-500 (all 500 problems), under greedy and sampling decoding, with two independent drafts against DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B: a same-family draft (DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B) and a cross-family draft (Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct), yielding about 1.78M verification rounds. Using within-problem paired tests, we find that after isolating a greedy-decoding repetition pathology, acceptance rate does not decay with position in any of the four configurations within the supported ranges (greedy 0-8K, sampling 0-12K); the second half of each trajectory is slightly but significantly higher than the first. A controlled temperature sweep shows that the apparent sampling-mode degradation of high-entropy reflection tokens is a visibility artifact of argmax agreement, not a drop in expected acceptance probability. Rejections concentrate with high precision on reflection trigger tokens such as "wait", "however" and "check", an entropy-selective failure that a lightweight three-arm LoRA trajectory-alignment intervention (uniform, reflection-marked, entropy-weighted) fails to improve in mean accepted length. We thus supply the positional baseline that independent draft-target pairs were missing, and localize the residual failure to decision-point entropy rather than position.

</details>


### [191] [Diagnosing Personalization Failures in Mobile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30573)
*Zhixin Lin,Dongliang Xu,Jungang Li,Shidong Pan,Jun Feng,Yanbin Sun,Jing Li,Liang Liu,Yue Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 PrefDiag-Bench，用五类偏好分类刻画偏好“如何”改变执行策略，用 1,664 个探针的阶段式诊断框架追踪偏好“何时”生效及失败位置，评测 11 个 GUI agent 系统，发现个性化任务性能显著下降，瓶颈在于从历史中选取相关证据与识别偏好生效时机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化 GUI agent 需要用户偏好指导在不断变化的 GUI 状态上的顺序执行，由此产生两个未解问题：偏好应以何种方式改变执行策略（HOW），以及应在交互轨迹的哪一时刻生效（WHEN）。现有基准主要评估整体任务成功率，无法对这两点给出细粒度洞察。

Method: 构建 PrefDiag-Bench，包含两部分：(1) 将偏好归纳为五种类型，刻画其对执行策略的影响方式；(2) 阶段式诊断框架，设计 1,664 个探针，追踪偏好应生效的时机与失败发生环节。评测对象为 11 个 GUI agent 系统（含端到端 agent 与 agentic workflow），并在显式用户画像与行为记忆日志两种用户上下文形式下进行对比；另开展显式提供相关证据的受控干预实验。

Result: 现有 agent 在非个性化任务上完成率较高，但在偏好个性化任务上性能明显下降；表现最好的 Seed-2.0-Pro 在显式用户画像下的 Process 类偏好上仅达 47.17%。Agent 在 Ranking 与 Process 类偏好上尤为薄弱。分析揭示两个关键能力瓶颈：从用户历史中选择相关证据、以及判断偏好在执行中何时生效。显式提供相关证据的受控干预可将端到端任务成功率最高提升 7.06%。

Conclusion: PrefDiag-Bench 超越整体任务成功率的单一视角，为 GUI agent 个性化能力提供了更系统的诊断性评估，并定位了证据选取与生效时机两类瓶颈，为后续改进指明方向；代码与数据已公开。

Abstract: Unlike conventional GUI agents, personalized GUI agents require user preferences to guide sequential execution over changing GUI states. This raises two challenges: determining HOW each preference should alter execution strategy and WHEN it should take effect along the interaction trajectory. Existing benchmarks primarily evaluate overall task success, offering limited insight into these questions. We introduce PrefDiag-Bench with two components: (1) a categorization of preferences into five types, characterizing HOW they affect execution; and (2) a stage-wise diagnostic framework with 1,664 probes, tracing WHEN preferences should take effect and where failures occur. We evaluate eleven GUI agent systems, including end-to-end agents and agentic workflows, under two forms of user context: explicit user profiles and behavioral memory logs. Although existing agents achieve relatively high completion rates on non-personalized tasks, performance is substantially lower on preference-personalized tasks. Even the best-performing model, Seed-2.0-Pro, achieves only 47.17% on Process preferences under explicit user profiles. Agents struggle particularly with Ranking and Process preferences. Further analysis reveals two key capability bottlenecks: selecting relevant evidence from user history and recognizing when preferences should take effect during execution. Controlled interventions that explicitly provide relevant evidence improve end-to-end task success by up to 7.06%. PrefDiag-Bench offers a more systematic evaluation of GUI agents' personalization capabilities beyond overall task success. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Zhixin-L/PrefDiag-Bench.

</details>


### [192] [Governed Self-Evolution for Autonomous Investing: A Multi-Agent LLM System with an Invariant Core](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30557)
*Yebo Feng,Jingyang Dai,Liangrui Pan,Nelson Lau,Jiahua Xu,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对不可逆、依赖不可信 Web 数据的自主投资场景，提出多智能体 LLM 系统 Steward，在类型系统层面划定"自演化边界"：九类角色智能体联合决策、七层共享记忆提供上下文、基于复盘的夜间打分与季度反射自改进，全部围绕由订单算术、风控规则与纪律提示组成的不可自改的"不变核心"运行。五个月实盘（102 交易日、1632 次 LLM 调用）将 24.6% 的行业回撤控制在 0.3% 亏损；回放显示单模型更频繁交易且五日跑输 1.35 个百分点，注入指令未被遵循；2015–2026 回测显示仅"核心"即可在全部 50 种随机入场策略下降低最大回撤；反射器定位了主导失败根因，门控使学习仅在触发日晚间被采用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体若要在真实 Web 数据上行动，必须同时整合异构证据、记住已执行的动作并从结果中改进，同时不能削弱自身的安全约束。自主投资是这一矛盾的极端场景：每次动作不可逆，且依据的是不可信的网页内容。现有系统缺少对"自我改进可以在哪里发生、不能触及哪里"的显式、可验证的界定。

Method: 1）九角色专门化智能体：四名分析师分别读取新闻流、SEC 文件、宏观序列与市场数据并输出带类型标注的报告，组合经理据此设定目标仓位，受保护的风险经理进行审计。2）七层共享记忆：由代码从系统自身记录中装配，为每次决策提供持仓上下文、市场叙事、校准信息与智能体历史决策。3）基于复盘的自改进：代码每晚对每笔交易与预测打分并写入记忆；每季度由反射器提出提示词层面的学习，经确定性门控可逆地应用，并通过回放已记录输入进行验证。以上三者都围绕一个不可被任何自编辑触及的不变核心——订单算术、风险规则与纪律智能体提示词。4）评估手段包括五个月实盘部署、记录输入的对照回放以及 2015–2026 长周期回测。

Result: 五个月实盘（102 个交易日、1632 次 LLM 调用）中，系统将 24.6% 的行业回撤压缩为 0.3% 的亏损。记录输入回放中，给定相同原始数据的单一模型在五日内交易更频繁，相对市场亏损 1.35 个百分点，而联合设计未出现显著亏损；注入的指令未被执行。2015–2026 回测表明，仅不变核心即可在全部 50 种随机入场策略下降低最大回撤。反射器将主导失败追溯至根因，门控所应用的学习在触发日晚间被采纳，而在所有对照晚间均未被采纳。

Conclusion: 将自演化边界编码进类型系统、并以不可自改的不变核心为锚，可以在不可信输入与不可逆动作的条件下实现可审计、可回滚、可验证的持续自我改进。实盘、对照回放与长周期回测的收敛证据表明，该架构在抑制回撤、抵御指令注入和按需采纳学习三方面均有效。

Abstract: LLM agents that act on live Web data must combine heterogeneous evidence, remember what they have done and improve from outcomes without weakening their own safeguards. We study this problem in autonomous investing, where every action is irreversible and based on untrusted Web content. We present Steward, a multi-agent LLM system that draws the boundary of self-evolution in its type system. First, nine role-specialised agents decide jointly: four analysts read news feeds, SEC filings, macroeconomic series and market data and emit typed reports, from which a Portfolio Manager sets target positions that a protected Risk Manager audits. Second, a seven-layer shared memory, assembled by code from the system's own record, gives each decision its position context, the market narrative, calibration and the agent's past decisions. Third, the system improves itself by review: code grades every trade and forecast nightly and writes the grades into memory, and each quarter a reflector proposes prompt learnings that a deterministic gate applies reversibly and replay of recorded inputs verifies. All three operate around an invariant core of order arithmetic, risk rules and discipline-agent prompts that no self-edit can reach. In a five-month live deployment (102 trading days, 1,632 LLM calls), the system contained a 24.6% sector drawdown to a 0.3% loss; in replay of its recorded inputs, a single model given the same raw data traded more and lost 1.35 points against the market over five days where the joint design showed no significant loss, and no injected instruction was followed; and a 2015-2026 backtest shows the core alone lowering the maximum drawdown under all 50 random entry policies. The reflector traced the dominant failure to its root cause, and the gate applied a learning that the agent adopted on trigger evenings and on no control evening. Code: https://github.com/yebof/quant-agent

</details>


### [193] [From Prose to Properties: Testing Research Claims with ARATest for Agent-Native Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30495)
*Yifeng He,Jiachen Liu,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ARATest 将论文中隐含的性质抽取为参数化的“性质骨架”，以属性测试库的形式把经验性断言变成可执行、可判定的测试，并配合多值语义与见证重放，在 33 份 ARA 产物的 243 条断言中实现声明绑定、故障检测与真实论文错误的发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI agent 使研究断言的产出速度远超人工核查速度。每条经验性断言都隐含一个需在其案例集合上成立的性质，但该性质仅以自然语言表述。缺乏显式性质，就无法确定何种证据能证伪断言，验证因此无法跟上断言产出。

Method: 提出“性质骨架”抽象——可跨断言复用的参数化关系——以近似断言语义。实现 ARATest 属性测试库：类型化声明将断言绑定到骨架并固定比较量、案例集、作用域与数值策略；共享案例类型确保生成器在断言的统计单元内产出案例；三值语义（成立/违反/不确定）；每个违反附带可重放见证。默认生成器读取 ARA 产物中与断言关联的证据，auto-research 生成器调度 agent 运行发布代码获取新案例；DissClaimer 插件充当该 agent 并以持续测试方式运行这些生成器。

Result: 在 33 个 ARA 产物的 243 条断言中，54 条获得绑定证据的忠实声明：32 条成立、8 条违反、14 条不确定。两处违反揭露了已发表论文中的错误并获作者确认。ARATest 在 19 个有效对照上零误报地检出全部 553 个注入故障，优于两个 LLM 评判器。新运行还暴露了报告证据无法体现的行为。

Conclusion: 结果表明，单一固定测试即可针对已报告证据与新生成证据检验显式的断言性质，从而让断言验证跟上 agent 时代的断言产出速度。

Abstract: AI agents now run experiments and report findings, so research produces claims faster than researchers can check them. Each empirical claim implies a property that must hold over its cases, but it states that property only in prose. Without an explicit property, no test can decide which evidence would falsify a claim, so validation cannot keep pace with the claims agents produce. To make claims testable, we approximate a claim's semantics by abstracting its property skeleton, a parameterized relation that recurs across claims. We present ARATest, a property-based testing library that checks these skeletons as executable tests. A typed declaration binds a claim to a skeleton and fixes the claim's compared quantities, cases, scope, and numerical policy. A shared case type makes every generator supply cases in the claim's statistical unit. A three-valued semantics assigns each property one of three verdicts: holds, violated, or inconclusive. Every violation carries a replayable witness. A default generator reads the evidence that agent-native research artifacts (ARA) link to each claim, and auto-research generators dispatch an agent to run released code for fresh cases. Our DissClaimer plugin supplies that agent and runs these generators as continuous testing.
  Of 243 claims in 33 ARA artifacts, 54 received faithful declarations bound to evidence: 32 held, eight were violated, and 14 remained inconclusive. Two violations exposed errors in published papers that their authors confirmed. ARATest detected all 553 seeded faults without a false alarm on 19 valid controls, outperforming two LLM judges. Fresh runs exposed behaviors that reported evidence could not show. These results suggest that one fixed test can check explicit claim properties against both reported and fresh evidence.

</details>


### [194] [What the Trace Shows and What the Label Says: Auditing Behavioral Detectors in Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30491)
*Idunnuoluwa A. Adeniji,Shilpika,Michael E. Papka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文审计工具调用场景下LLM agent执行轨迹的三类检测器标签（最终正确性、可见重验证、故障传播），揭示标签与轨迹证据之间存在系统性不一致（尤其"静默工具输出覆盖"导致的验证检测漏报），并主张标签应附带轨迹证据与执行限制信息以实现测量透明性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM agent评估常将执行轨迹压缩为标签（如最终答案正确性、工具验证、故障传播），但这些标签是解析器、行为检测器和评测框架规则产生的"测量"，未必完整反映轨迹实际发生的事。作者希望量化标签与真实轨迹证据之间的偏差。

Method: 构建基于GSM8K的工具调用环境，agent调用计算器且其输出被偶尔注入损坏，从而获得"工具实际返回什么、模型后来用了什么、检测器报告了什么"的清晰ground truth。对6个生产模型、每模型30道题（共180条轨迹）审计三类轨迹级检测器，并进行人工复核（90条轨迹）以及步数/Token预算的消融实验（2个模型）。

Result: 最终正确性、可见重验证、故障传播三者存在系统性分歧。关键失效模式为"静默工具输出覆盖"：计算器返回损坏值，模型后续改用另一个（常为正确）数值而未再次调用工具，使坏值不再传播，而验证检测器记录为"无重检查"。人工复核对最终正确性(67/67)、故障影响(19/19)、故障相关性(18/18)完全一致，但对验证仅67/81一致，14处分歧归为4种重复模式。执行限制也影响结果：消融中一个模型正确率随步数/Token预算在21/30至27/30间浮动，另一个稳定在27/30–29/30。

Conclusion: 自动化标签应附带其背后的轨迹证据：检测器所问问题是否适用于该轨迹、该次运行是否被执行限制截断、以及任何人工修正。作者通过轨迹查看器（trace viewer）使这些信息与报告结果一并可检查，以推动测量透明性。

Abstract: LLM agents are often evaluated by reducing execution traces to labels such as final-answer correctness, tool verification, and fault propagation. These labels are measurements produced by parsers, behavioral detectors, and harness rules, and they may not fully capture what the trace shows. We study this in a tool-use setting built on GSM8K, where agents call a calculator whose outputs are occasionally corrupted, giving clear ground truth for what the tool returned, what the model later used, and what the detectors reported. We audit three trace-level detectors across six production models and 30 problems per model. Final correctness, visible re-verification, and fault propagation can disagree in systematic ways. A key failure mode is silent tool-output override: the calculator returns a corrupted value, but the model later uses a different, often correct, value without calling the tool again, so the bad value stops propagating while a verification detector records no re-check. Human review of 90 traces agrees perfectly with the automated labels for final correctness (67/67), fault impact (19/19), and fault relevance (18/18), but only 67/81 for verification; all 14 disagreements fall into four recurring patterns. Execution limits also change the reported result: in an ablation across two models, one ranges from 21/30 to 27/30 correct as step and token budgets change, while the other stays between 27/30 and 29/30. These findings motivate measurement transparency: an automated label should come with the trace evidence behind it, whether the question the detector asks applied to that trace, whether the run was cut off by an execution limit, and any human corrections. Our trace viewer makes this information inspectable alongside the reported results.

</details>


### [195] [SpecMAGIC: Specification-Guided Multi-Task Generalization in Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30443)
*Zijian Guo,İlker Işık,H. M. Sabbir Ahmad,Wenchao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出结合时序逻辑与协调算子的任务规范语言及分层框架SpecMAGIC，通过规格自动机结构分解子任务、集中式高层规划分配子任务序列、去中心化低层策略执行，实现对新任务的零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务泛化在多智能体强化学习中研究不足，而现实应用要求多智能体系统能泛化到复杂、时间延展且协调需求多样的任务。

Method: 1) 定义规范语言，将时序逻辑任务规范与表达协调需求的算子结合；2) SpecMAGIC分层框架利用任务规范中的自动机结构进行子任务分解；3) 集中式高层规划器识别并在智能体间分配相容的子任务序列；4) 去中心化低层策略执行单个子任务；5) 理论分析给出高层规划器的可靠性（soundness）与概率完备性。

Result: 多智能体导航实验表明，SpecMAGIC在未见任务规范上显著优于现有方法，且随智能体数量增加仍保持较强性能。

Conclusion: 依据新规范组合可复用子任务策略即可零样本泛化到新任务而无需重训练，验证了分层+自动机分解+规范语言在MARL多任务泛化上的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-task generalization is an important yet underexplored problem in multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL). Many real-world applications require multi-agent systems to generalize across complex, temporally extended tasks with diverse inter-agent coordination requirements. We introduce a specification language that combines temporal-logic task specifications with operators for expressing such requirements. We propose SpecMAGIC, a hierarchical framework that leverages the automaton structure of task specifications for subtask decomposition. A centralized high-level planner identifies and allocates compatible subtask sequences across agents, while decentralized low-level policies execute individual subtasks. This enables zero-shot generalization to new tasks by composing reusable subtask policies according to new specifications, without retraining. We establish soundness and probabilistic completeness of the high-level planner. Experiments on multi-agent navigation tasks show that SpecMAGIC substantially outperforms existing approaches on unseen task specifications and maintains strong performance as the number of agents increases.

</details>


### [196] [Secure Agentic AI and Multimodal Intelligence in 6G Communication Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30436)
*Satyam Gaba,Devanshu Motwani,Siva Sai,Vineet Suri,Vikas Ranjan,Vinay Chamola*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从隐私与安全视角综述了面向6G跨层通信系统的多模态智能与智能体AI，提出覆盖感知、记忆、工具使用与多智能体编排的系统视图，构建跨层威胁模型与攻击分类体系，并梳理相应防御策略与开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G需通过多模态智能、边缘计算与自主网络控制的深度集成支撑沉浸式、意图驱动与低时延关键业务，智能体AI因而具备应用潜力；但其引入的安全与隐私风险超出传统AI与网络安全模型，且脆弱性可沿多模态数据管线、记忆、工具、通信接口与智能体协同环路传播，亟需系统性梳理。

Method: 采用综述方法：建立面向6G的系统视图（感知、记忆、工具使用、多智能体编排）；在感知、认知、行动、记忆与通信各层分析威胁模型与攻击面；将已有攻击归纳为结构化分类体系；评述缓解策略，包括提示与记忆防御、智能体间信任机制、安全编排与隐私保护学习。

Result: 给出了跨层攻击面的系统化刻画与攻击分类学，并汇总了覆盖提示层、记忆层、智能体协同层与学习层的防御技术谱系。

Conclusion: 提出可信多模态多智能体6G系统的开放研究方向，强调需在架构层面协同设计安全、隐私与自主性。

Abstract: Sixth-generation (6G) communication systems are expected to support immersive, intent-driven, and latency-critical services through tight integration of multimodal intelligence, edge computing, and autonomous network control. In this setting, agentic AI systems can reason over heterogeneous inputs, use tools and network functions, coordinate across distributed entities, and adapt to dynamic environments. These capabilities make agentic multimodal intelligence promising for 6G applications such as semantic communications, digital twins, and zero-touch network management, but they also introduce security and privacy risks that extend beyond conventional AI and network security models. Vulnerabilities can propagate across multimodal data pipelines, memory, tools, communication interfaces, and inter-agent coordination loops. We survey multimodal intelligence and agentic AI for 6G cross-layered communication systems from the perspectives of privacy and security. We present a 6G-oriented system view spanning perception, memory, tool use, and multi-agent orchestration; analyze threat models and attack surfaces across sensing, cognition, action, memory, and communication layers; organize existing attacks into a structured taxonomy; and review mitigation strategies including prompt and memory defenses, inter-agent trust mechanisms, secure orchestration, and privacy-preserving learning. We conclude by outlining open research directions for trustworthy multimodal multi-agent 6G systems.

</details>


### [197] [Breaking the high-degree barrier in Lehmer's problem with a human-AI discovery loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30277)
*Yuri Chervonyi,Sergei Gukov,Thang Luong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“广义Boyd算法”（五参数构造），通过人–AI发现闭环解决高次整系数多项式小Mahler测度的构造问题，消除Boyd单参数族的次数跳空，对每个偶数次数构造Mahler测度严格低于极限点α₁≈1.255434的不可约多项式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lehmer问题（1933）关注整系数多项式的Mahler测度能否任意小；此前仅系统构造到偶数次数d≤180，高次情形下Boyd解析单参数族常发生因子分解，跳过近50%的目标次数，缺乏任意次数d的通用构造。

Method: 构建人–AI发现循环：在对话式提示基础上扩展快速数值流水线、精确符号验证器与大规模分布式搜索；AI智能体本地原型化数值与符号求解器，跨分布式集群并行搜索，并与人类研究者共同提取、验证数学假设，最终导出五参数构造。

Result: 在上至2000的所有偶数次数上评估，该方法改进6个已发表数值，并保持全部已知最优记录的99.6%；同时对每个目标次数均给出Mahler测度严格低于α₁≈1.255434的不可约多项式。

Conclusion: 广义Boyd算法为高次Mahler测度极小多项式的构造提供了通用、可验证的方案；论文同时发布覆盖全部所研究偶数次数的极小Mahler测度多项式验证数据集。

Abstract: While artificial intelligence has made rapid progress in mathematical reasoning, many open problems depend on structure that is revealed only through large-scale computational exploration and analytical synthesis. One such example is Lehmer's problem from 1933. It asks whether the Mahler measure of an integer polynomial, which measures how far its roots stray from the unit circle, can be made arbitrarily small. Previously, constructing irreducible polynomials with integer coefficients and small Mahler measure has been carried out systematically only for even degrees $d \le 180$. At higher degrees, Boyd's analytical one-parameter family frequently factors, skipping nearly 50% of target degrees and leaving no general construction for arbitrary $d$. Here, we address the high-degree regime through a human-AI discovery loop, in which we extend the conversational prompting with a fast numerical pipeline, an exact symbolic verifier, and large-scale distributed search. The AI agent prototypes numerical and symbolic solvers locally, launches parallel searches across distributed clusters and works with the human researcher to extract and verify mathematical hypotheses. Through this interactive loop, we derive a five-parameter construction, which we call the Generalized Boyd algorithm. It eliminates degree-skipping and constructs irreducible polynomials with Mahler measure strictly below Boyd's limit point $α_1 \approx 1.255434$ for every target degree. Evaluated across all even degrees up to 2000, our approach improves upon six previously published values and holds 99.6% of all best known records. With this work we release the verified dataset of polynomials with minimal Mahler measure across all studied even degrees.

</details>


### [198] [Contestable Agents for Data Governance: Recording Human Stewardship of AI-Generated Metadata Across Structured and Unstructured Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31553)
*Humza Akhtar,Brian Freeman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"可争议治理"（contestable governance）框架，利用LLM专业智能体为存在争议的元数据决策生成带评分和引用的论据，由管理员审阅干预后提交，并保留完整治理记录；结合版本化领域术语表、分级分诊流程及经验准入闸门，以两个HVAC用例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业组织的运行知识分散于结构化记录（遥测、点表、事务）与非结构化来源（手册、服务记录、通信、巡检报告），治理项目通常分别管理，且分类、关联与文档化高度依赖少数具备深厚领域知识的员工。基于大语言模型的智能体虽可提出元数据建议、引用文档并标记质量问题，但未经审阅的建议会将错误扩散至下游系统，而形式化审批又无法留存审阅者实际核查内容。因此需要一种既利用LLM能力又保留人类问责与可追溯性的治理机制。

Method: 提出"可争议治理"工作流：专业智能体为存在争议的元数据决策生成带评分、带引用的论据；管理员可在提交前接受、拒绝、修改或追加论据；治理记录保留证据、干预与结果。配套设计包括：(1)版本化领域术语表，为智能体提供模型特定的术语与缩写定义；(2)分诊路径，仅对不确定提案启用完整论据流程，同时保证提交前审阅；(3)评分模型与记录模型；(4)控制哪些已验证决策可成为先例的准入闸门。以两个HVAC用例说明：基于Brick Schema类标签的测点分类（附开放参考实现），以及将索赔字段与遥测关联至服务记录和修订版公告的保修失效模式决策。

Result: 论文给出了工作流、分诊路径、评分与记录模型及先例闸门的形式化描述；在HVAC测点分类任务中，基于Brick Schema类标签的流程配有开放参考实现；保修失效模式用例展示了如何将索赔字段与遥测数据链接至服务记录与修订版公告，形成可审阅的决策论据。

Conclusion: 通过将LLM智能体的建议转化为可审阅、可干预、可留痕的"可争议"论据，并借助版本化术语表、按不确定性分级的分诊机制与先例准入闸门，可在保留人工问责与提交前审阅的前提下规模化工业元数据治理，降低未审阅建议造成错误传播的风险；该模式在HVAC领域具备可行性并可推广至其他工业场景。

Abstract: Industrial organizations hold operational knowledge in structured records, such as telemetry, point lists, and transactions, and in unstructured sources, such as manuals, service notes, communications, and inspection reports. Governance programs often manage these sources separately. Classifying, linking, and documenting them depends on a small group of employees with deep domain knowledge. Agents built on large language models can propose metadata, cite documents, and flag quality issues, but unreviewed proposals can spread errors into downstream systems. A perfunctory approval leaves no record of what a reviewer checked. We present contestable governance: specialist agents produce scored, cited arguments for disputed metadata decisions; a steward can accept, reject, modify, or add arguments before commit; and a governance record retains the evidence, interventions, and outcome. A versioned domain glossary supplies model-specific terms and acronym definitions to the agents. We describe the workflow, a triage path that reserves full argumentation for uncertain proposals while preserving review before commit, the scoring and record models, and a gate that controls which verified decisions become precedent. Two HVAC use cases illustrate the pattern: point classification using Brick Schema class labels, supported by an open reference implementation, and an illustrative warranty failure-mode decision linking claim fields and telemetry to service notes and revisioned bulletins.

</details>


### [199] [Reducing Multi-Horizon Photovoltaic Forecast Error through Tree-Anchored Expert Combination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31248)
*Sun Ze,Zhou Liguo,Xu Yuqing,Yu Lei,Jiang Mingming,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文检验能否用互补的时序预测器在不被弱成员拖累的前提下降低强树模型的MAE：在共享MAE权重的非负组合策略下，相对LightGBM树模型将缩放MAE降低3.21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强直接树模型可能是最佳的单体光伏预测器，但其残差误差或可通过加权组合进一步降低；核心疑问是互补的时序预测器能否在不被较弱集成成员拖累的情况下改善树模型的MAE。

Method: 候选库包含LightGBM与七种采用不同时序表示的神经实现；组合器以MAE为目标拟合，权重非负且和为1，无用候选可获零权重，树模型作为组合锚点并提供开发阶段回退。主设计在各预测时域间共享权重，并对比时域特定权重与替代损失以检验额外复杂度是否必要。

Result: 在两个共址公开PVDAQ系统、56,078个窗口上，历史共享MAE策略相对树模型将缩放MAE降低3.21%；最新的有界神经校正相对该静态组合并未进一步降低MAE。

Conclusion: 结果支持在该候选库中有选择地组合互补预测，而非等权平均或无条件叠加模块；但两项分析复用了此前已检查的评估期，因此不能确立独立复现、天气特异性因果性，也不证明八专家普遍有效。

Abstract: A strong direct tree model may remain the best standalone photovoltaic forecaster, yet a weighted combination can reduce its residual errors. We examine whether complementary temporal predictors can lower the tree's mean absolute error (MAE) over 15--240 minutes without weaker ensemble members degrading performance. The candidate bank contains LightGBM and seven neural implementations with different temporal representations. A combiner fitted to MAE has nonnegative weights that sum to one. Unhelpful candidates can receive zero weight, while the tree anchors the combination and provides a development-stage fallback. The main design shares weights across horizons; horizon-specific weights and alternative losses test whether further complexity is needed. On 56,078 windows from two co-located public PVDAQ systems, the historical shared-MAE policy reduces scaled MAE by 3.21\% relative to the tree.
  The latest bounded neural correction does not reduce MAE further than this static combination. The results support selective combination of complementary predictions in this bank rather than equal averaging or indiscriminate addition of modules. Both analyses reuse a previously inspected evaluation period. They do not establish independent replication, weather-specific causation or universal gains from eight experts.

</details>


### [200] [Trion-1: Unifying Language, Protein Sequence, and Structure with Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31215)
*Tianyu Lu,Kaicheng Yang,Jiangtao Feng,Keyue Qiu,Yawen Ouyang,Yixin Wu,Xinbo Zhang,Lihao Wang,Wei-Ying Ma,Hao Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Trion-1是首个原生统一多模态蛋白质扩散语言模型（dLLM），通过统一离散token空间与双向/半双向dLLM骨干，统一折叠、逆折叠、序列理解、结构理解、序列设计、结构设计六大能力并保留LLM语言能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质模型多为分任务、分模态的专用模型，缺乏统一序列-结构-功能的交互式建模，且难以继承现代LLM的指令跟随与推理能力。作者设想一个可理解、生成、跨模态翻译的通用蛋白质基础模型。

Method: 两项关键设计：(1) 统一离散token空间，联合表示序列、结构与自然语言；(2) 以dLLM为骨干，用双向或半双向token交互替代严格左到右分解，实现全局残基通信与长程接触建模，天然适配结构建模。该形式化统一了折叠、逆折叠、序列理解、结构理解、序列设计、结构设计六类任务。

Result: 在既定基准上整体具备竞争力：严格同源控制下单样本折叠TM-score>0.8，匹敌或超过使用200个样本的现有三模态模型；序列理解与Claude Opus 4.7（62.37%）等顶级LLM相当。

Conclusion: 结果支持序列-结构-语言表征良好对齐及强跨模态生成能力，验证了原生统一多模态dLLM作为通用蛋白质基础模型的可行性。

Abstract: We envision a general-purpose protein foundation model as a unified, interactive model of sequence, structure, and function, capable of understanding, generation, and cross-modal translation while retaining the instruction-following and reasoning capabilities of modern LLMs. Toward this vision, we introduce Trion-1, the first native, unified, multimodal diffusion language model (dLLM) for proteins. Trion-1 is built on two key design choices: (1) a unified discrete token space that jointly represents sequence, structure, and natural language; (2) a dLLM backbone that replaces strict left-to-right factorization with bidirectional or semi-bidirectional token interactions, enabling global residue communication and long-range contact modeling that are naturally suited to structure modeling. This formulation unifies six core capabilities: folding, inverse folding, sequence understanding, structure understanding, sequence design, and structure design, while retaining the native language capabilities of general-purpose LLMs. On established benchmarks, Trion-1 achieves broadly competitive performance. In folding, Trion-1 achieves a TM-score above 0.8 with a single sample under strict homology control, matching or exceeding the existing tri-modal model using 200 samples. For sequence understanding, Trion-1 performs on par with top-tier LLMs such as Claude Opus 4.7 (62.37%). These results support well-aligned sequence-structure-language representations and strong cross-modal generative capabilities.

</details>


### [201] [Scaling Laws for Safety Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30678)
*Yuchen Wu,Jerry Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出安全探针性能（探针误差 = 1 − AUROC）服从两条简单缩放律：最低可达误差随目标模型规模呈幂律下降；误差随标注数量增加而向该下限收敛，且收敛斜率随模型规模增大而变陡。基于六个新构建的安全数据集、5个模型家族的20个基座模型、2种探针架构、32至10^6个标注，验证了定律的跨任务、跨家族、跨架构稳健性，并展示了其在外推预测与预算决策上的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全探针是实时监控语言模型的标准工具，但每遇新模型或新危害领域都需重新训练，并需新标注与架构选择；而模型与标注数量本身无法预先告知探针性能，只能通过大量训练与配置比较来试错，成本高昂。作者因此希望从模型规模与标注数量出发，事先预测探针性能。现有安全数据集规模不足或已饱和，无法支撑该研究。

Method: 构建六个规模与难度均充分的新安全数据集；在5个模型家族的20个基座模型上，对每种模型使用32至10^6个标注训练两种探针架构；以探针误差（1 − AUROC）为指标，拟合模型规模与标注数量对误差的影响，检验缩放律在不同任务、模型家族与探针架构间的普适性。

Result: 探针误差遵循两条缩放律：误差下限随模型规模呈幂律下降；误差随标注数增加而向该下限递减，且斜率随目标模型规模增大而增大。定律跨任务、家族与架构成立，仅拟合常数不同。仅用家族中最小模型拟合时，定律对下一更大规模模型误差的预测在72%情况下误差不超过0.02；在73%情况下能正确判断“增加标注”还是“换用更大模型”是达到目标误差的更经济途径；即使选错探针架构，平均仅带来0.006的额外误差。

Conclusion: 安全探针性能可由模型规模与标注预算提前预测，为安全研究者在给定模型与标注预算下如何构建最优探针提供了可操作的定量依据，并支持探针架构选择与资源分配决策。

Abstract: Safety probes trained on a model's activations are a standard tool for monitoring language models in real time. Each time a new model is released or a new harm area needs to be monitored, the probe must be retrained, which requires new labels and a choice of probe architecture. Yet neither the model nor the labels tell us in advance how well the resulting probe will perform. Finding out requires training and comparing many probe configurations. In this paper, we ask whether probe performance can instead be predicted in advance from model size and the number of labels. Existing safety datasets are insufficient for this, as they are either limited in scale or already saturated. We therefore construct six new safety datasets of sufficient size and difficulty. On these datasets, we train two probe architectures on 20 base models from five model families, with 32 to $10^{6}$ labels for each model. We measure performance using probe error, defined as 1 - AUROC, where lower values are better. We find that probe error follows two simple scaling laws. First, the lowest probe error a model can reach falls as a power law in its size. Second, probe error decreases towards its floor as the number of examples increases; the slope increases as the target model size increases. Both laws hold across tasks, model families and probe architectures, and only their fitted constants differ. When fitted on the smallest models of a family, the laws predict the error of the next larger model to within 0.02 in 72% of cases. They also identify whether more labels or a larger model is the cheaper way to reach a target error in 73% of cases. Finally, they choose the probe architecture with an extra error of only 0.006 on average when they pick the worse one. These laws allow safety researchers to determine how to create the best probe for any given model and label budget.

</details>


### [202] [Beyond Causal Masks: Auditing and Localizing Hidden Future-Token Leakage in Adaptive Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31522)
*Huiting Liu,Jinsong Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文揭示自适应残差路由解码器存在隐藏的未来token泄漏：一个24层异常检查点teacher-forced验证损失从3.5958降到1.4259，却通不过前缀不变性审计且生成困惑度变差；定位到全序列摘要经动态路由查询路径广播，经两组12层因果修复后泄漏消除、表面优势消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐藏的未来token泄漏会造成虚假的架构收益：模型在teacher-forced评测下看似更优，但自由生成质量反而下降。作者希望系统研究这一失效模式，并说明为何较低的teacher-forced损失需结合整模型因果检查与自由运行评测来解读。

Method: 以自适应残差路由解码器为对象：其序列级路由摘要在因果注意力掩码正确的情况下仍将前缀位置暴露给未来token。使用前缀不变性契约、最大前缀输出差异（LeakScore）、互补行为指标及逐层诊断画像进行检测；并通过实现检查定位违规来源。支撑实验包括覆盖五个模块族的六组合成实现对、干净预训练对照、受控故障注入，以及跨数值精度的缓存一致性测试；另以两个独立训练的12层因果修复版本作验证。

Result: 异常24层检查点验证损失由基线3.5958降至1.4259，但未通过前缀不变性审计，生成时参考模型困惑度显著升高。逐层诊断与实现检查将违规追溯为经动态路由查询路径广播的全序列摘要。两个独立训练的12层因果修复消除了可检测泄漏，使验证损失与参考困惑度回到对照水平，表面大幅优势随之消失。

Conclusion: 隐藏的未来token访问可导致对架构进展的误导性结论；较低的teacher-forced损失不应单独作为架构优劣依据，而应与整模型因果性检查和自由运行生成评估联合解读。前缀不变性契约与LeakScore等诊断工具可有效识别此类泄漏。

Abstract: Hidden future-token leakage can create false architectural gains: an autoregressive model may appear substantially better under teacher-forced evaluation while its free-running generation deteriorates. We investigate this failure in an adaptive residual-routing decoder whose sequence-level routing summary exposes prefix positions to future tokens despite a correct causal attention mask. An anomalous 24-layer checkpoint reduces validation loss from the baseline's 3.5958 to 1.4259, yet it fails a prefix-invariance audit and has substantially higher reference-model perplexity during generation. Layer-wise diagnostics and implementation inspection trace the violation to a full-sequence summary broadcast through the dynamic routing-query path. Two independently trained 12-layer causal repairs eliminate detectable leakage and return validation loss and reference perplexity to the control scale, removing the large apparent advantage. As diagnostic tools, we use the established prefix-invariance contract, maximum prefix-output discrepancy (LeakScore), complementary behavioral metrics, and layer-wise profiles. Supporting experiments cover six synthetic implementation pairs spanning five module families, clean pretrained controls, controlled fault injection, and cache consistency across numerical precisions. These results illustrate how hidden future-token access can produce misleading claims of architectural progress and why lower teacher-forced loss should be interpreted together with whole-model causal checks and free-running evaluation.

</details>


### [203] [FairFRESH: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Fair and Explainable Household Food Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31506)
*Kodur Sai Vinay Sathvik,Kakoli Bora,Tejaswini R. M*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FairFRESH将家庭食品推荐建模为受约束的公平分配问题，用神经-符号框架学习个体效用、按福利目标分配食品、以确定性过滤器保证饮食规则合规，并给出理论保证与实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统通常一次只服务单个用户，而家庭成员共享有限食品储备且各自遵循不同饮食规则，独立推荐易导致资源冲突与饮食规则违规，因此需要将家庭食品推荐视为受约束的公平分配问题。

Method: 提出FairFRESH神经-符号框架：(1)学习每位成员的效用函数；(2)在显式福利目标（功利主义、平滑Nash）下通过贪心分配食品；(3)用确定性过滤器强制执行饮食规则；(4)用Shapley与Banzhaf归因解释推荐。理论上证明有限惩罚在饮食规则阈值附近无法保证合规、给出过滤前无库存模型下贪心分配的近似保证、刻画Nash福利的尺度不变性，并用极小极大定理分析轮换共享餐。

Result: 在NHANES衍生数据上，学习效用模型Recall@20为0.293，优于五个学习型推荐器（最高0.244），但不及前日重复启发式（0.321）；在留出的四人家庭上，Nash分配相较冲突消解将Gini不平等降低42%，Recall@20由0.180提升至0.197，距平滑Nash最优上界2%以内，对单个成员效用重缩放仅改变至多1.5%的分配；所有推荐均满足编码的饮食规则，归因分数与组件移除后的效用损失相关（r=0.76）；仿真中共享餐使食品储备消耗减少约三分之一，周轮换使最差成员效用较最佳固定餐提升38%。

Conclusion: FairFRESH表明将家庭食品推荐表述为受约束公平分配、并结合神经效用学习与符号约束过滤，可在保证饮食规则合规的同时改善公平性与推荐质量，共享餐与轮换机制进一步提升资源效率与最差成员福利。

Abstract: Recommender systems typically serve one user at a time. However, in households, members share a finite pantry and follow different dietary rules, making independent recommendations prone to resource conflicts and dietary violations. We formulate household food recommendation as constrained fair division and present FairFRESH, a neuro-symbolic framework that learns per-member utilities, allocates foods under explicit welfare objectives, enforces dietary rules through a deterministic filter, and explains recommendations using Shapley and Banzhaf attributions. Theoretically, we show that no finite penalty guarantees compliance near a dietary-rule threshold, establish approximation guarantees for greedy allocation under utilitarian and smoothed Nash objectives in the stock-free model before filtering, and characterize the scale invariance of Nash welfare. We also use the minimax theorem to analyze rotating shared meals. On NHANES-derived data, the learned utility model achieves Recall@20 of 0.293, outperforming five learned recommenders (at most 0.244) but not a previous-day repetition heuristic (0.321). On held-out four-person households, Nash allocation reduces Gini disparity by 42% relative to conflict resolution while improving Recall@20 from 0.180 to 0.197. It comes within 2% of an upper bound on the smoothed Nash optimum, and rescaling one member's utilities changes at most 1.5% of assignments. All recommendations satisfy the encoded dietary rules, while attribution scores correlate with utility loss upon component removal (r = 0.76). In simulation, shared meals reduce pantry use by about one-third, and weekly rotation increases the worst-off member's utility by 38% over the best fixed meal.

</details>


### [204] [DIAL: Steerable Language Models with Distilled Instruction-Adherence Levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31438)
*Thanh Q. Tran,John Russell Himawan,Arun Verma,Kiwan Wong,Wei Xiao,Daniela Rus,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DIAL 通过将无分类器引导(CFG)产生的训练信号用于在线蒸馏，配合一个基于隐藏状态的轻量级文本条件转向适配器，实现对 LLM 指令遵循程度的细粒度、高效、可扩展的数值控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户与应用的指令遵循强度需求各异（如"简短点"应多短），但提示词(prompting)无法可靠提供细粒度控制。现有可转向语言模型方法无法同时满足细粒度控制、效率与跨多样指令的可扩展性，因此需要一种三者兼备的方案。

Method: 提出 DIAL（Distilled Instruction-Adherence Levels）：1) 细粒度控制——用冻结 LLM 执行无分类器引导(CFG)，为不同遵循等级的在线策略蒸馏(on-policy distillation)提供训练信号；2) 效率——设计轻量级转向适配器，用作用于 LLM 隐藏状态的分类器计算闭式转向编辑(closed-form steering edit)，按目标遵循等级逐 token 自适应设定转向幅度，并考虑隐藏状态几何以保持响应连贯性；3) 可扩展——分类器以文本为条件，共享适配器可支持新指令而无需逐指令拟合，训练仅需指令与请求文本。

Result: 跨多个 LLM 系列评测显示，DIAL 在概念表达与响应流畅度间取得有竞争力的平衡，概念得分随遵循等级单调上升。同时发布 SteerIF——含 98,625 条概念、人格、可验证与描述性指令的纯指令数据集；在 SteerIF 上训练后，DIAL 可细粒度控制人格，与提示词结合还能提升可验证指令遵循。

Conclusion: DIAL 实现了细粒度、高效、可扩展的指令遵循可控性，兼具概念表达与流畅度的良好权衡，并通过 SteerIF 数据集验证了其在人格控制与可验证指令遵循上的实用价值。

Abstract: When a user says "Keep it short", how short should the LLM's response be? More generally, users and applications require different levels of instruction adherence, but prompting does not reliably provide fine-grained control over them. We therefore study "steerable language models" that provide fine-grained numerical control over the degree to which models follow general instructions specified in text. However, existing approaches do not jointly offer fine-grained control, efficiency, and scalability across diverse instructions. To address this gap, we introduce DIAL (Distilled Instruction-Adherence Levels) to achieve all three properties. For fine-grained control, we perform classifier-free guidance (CFG) with a frozen LLM to provide training signals for on-policy distillation of instruction adherence at different levels. For efficiency, we design a lightweight steering adapter that uses a classifier over the LLM's hidden states to compute a closed-form steering edit. The classifier adaptively sets the steering magnitude at each token based on the target adherence level, while accounting for the geometry of the LLM's hidden states to preserve response coherence. For scalability, the classifier is text-conditioned, allowing a shared adapter to support new instructions without per-instruction fitting, and training requires only instruction and request text. Evaluation across multiple LLM families shows that DIAL achieves a competitive balance between concept expression and response fluency, with concept scores increasing monotonically across adherence levels. We introduce SteerIF, an instruction-only dataset of 98, 625 concept, persona, verifiable, and descriptive instructions. When trained on SteerIF, DIAL provides fine-grained control over personas and, when combined with prompting, improves verifiable instruction following.

</details>


### [205] [Conservative Meta-Agent Search: Architecture Adaptation under Non-Stationarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31436)
*Ali Atiah Alzahrani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将元智能体架构搜索形式化为带切换成本与硬风险约束的序贯"在位者替换"问题，提出保守式元智能体搜索（CMAS）与MetaInvest-Bench基准；在96条流上降低归一化动态oracle遗憾（0.116→0.104）并提升留出效用（0.44→0.48）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化智能体系统设计多在静态任务集与即时标量奖励上评估，而真实部署面临持续漂移、延迟且相互依赖的反馈、组件相关失效及高昂架构变更成本，评估与决策范式不匹配。

Method: 形式化元智能体架构搜索为带切换成本与硬风险约束的序贯在位者替换；CMAS检索历史架构并提出类型化图编辑，用共同随机数下的成对影子回放（含干预式组件归因）评分；晋升需经验校准、方差惩罚的联合得分超过预设边际，随后经过模拟金丝雀阶段与预设回滚。另构建MetaInvest-Bench：区制切换仿真器，指定worker能力使代理最优架构可计算，含防泄漏回放、对抗治理轨迹与五项负对照。

Result: 96条流上，归一化平均代理动态oracle遗憾由0.116降至0.104，留出效用由0.44升至0.48（对比最强自适应基线）；无增益时收缩至近单智能体团队；部署推理成本约降6%，但含搜索的token总成本最高；每次更新误晋升率3.4%（名义5%），密封重跑为4.8%。

Conclusion: 校准属经验性，仅针对作者自设基准上的更新级回放统计量，不保证整体效用提升、不保证模拟金丝雀阶段无害，也不提供序列级错误控制；金融流仅作风险敏感测试床，不主张alpha。

Abstract: Automated agentic-system design is typically evaluated on static task suites with immediate scalar rewards. Deployed systems face persistent shift, delayed dependent feedback, correlated component failures, and costly architecture changes. We formalize meta-agent architecture search as sequential incumbent replacement with switching costs and hard risk constraints and introduce Conservative Meta-Agent Search (CMAS). CMAS retrieves archived architectures and proposes typed graph edits, then scores each against the incumbent by paired shadow replay under common random numbers, with interventional component credit. Promotion requires an empirically calibrated, variance-penalized simultaneous score above a pre-specified margin, and is followed by a simulated canary stage and pre-specified rollback. Since backtests cannot certify meta-level skill, we introduce MetaInvest-Bench, a regime-switching simulator whose specified worker competence makes a surrogate-optimal architecture computable, with leakage-controlled replay, adversarial governance tracks, and five negative controls.
